TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-GNN，一种在大规模图上训练图神经网络的可扩展方法。它通过并行执行粗化图上的跨簇传播和局部簇内传播，同时解决图粗化（丢失节点区分度）和Cluster-GCN（丢失长程信息）的互补缺陷，并配有簇级批处理方案以扩展到百万级节点图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模图上训练GNN面临存储所有层节点表示的高内存开销。现有可扩展方法（如图粗化和Cluster-GCN）各有局限性：图粗化通过低秩近似提供谱保证但给簇内节点赋予统一表示，Cluster-GCN限制簇内传播便于批处理但切断长程信息。

Method: CoRe-GNN在每一层并行执行两种传播：1）粗化后的跨簇项捕捉长程结构；2）局部簇内项保留逐节点可区分性。同时引入基于簇的自然批处理方案，并证明其继承了图粗化的近似保证。

Result: 在同质性、异质性、大规模和长程图等节点分类基准上，CoRe-GNN优于图粗化和Cluster-GCN基线。特别是在长程任务上达到竞争性的准确率，同时通过批处理保持内存效率。

Conclusion: CoRe-GNN通过将图粗化与Cluster-GCN的互补优势结合在同一分解框架下，实现了既保留长程信息又保持逐节点区分性的大规模GNN训练，在精度和内存效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Training Graph Neural Networks on large graphs is challenged by the memory cost of storing all node representations across layers. We show that several existing scalable approaches can be written as structured modifications of the GNN propagation matrix, providing a unified perspective that exposes their respective limitations. In particular, graph coarsening replaces it by a low-rank approximation that enables spectral guarantees but assigns uniform representations to clustered nodes, while Cluster-GCN restricts the propagation matrix to intra-cluster connections that allow efficient batching but sever long-range information. These are complementary failures of the \emph{same} decomposition of the graph into groups of nodes. To obtain the best of both worlds, we propose \textbf{CoRe-GNN}, which performs both propagations in parallel at each layer: a coarsened inter-cluster term capturing long-range structure, and a local intra-cluster term preserving per-node discriminability. We prove that CoRe-GNN inherits analogous approximation guarantees to those of graph coarsening, and introduce a natural cluster-based \emph{batching scheme} that scales to graphs with millions of nodes. On node classification benchmarks spanning homophilic, heterophilic, large-scale, and long-range graphs, CoRe-GNN outperforms both graph coarsening and Cluster-GCN baselines. Notably, CoRe-GNN reaches competitive accuracy on \emph{long-range} tasks, while remaining memory-efficient through batching.

</details>


### [136] [RamanPFN: learning from Raman spectral structure with a tabular foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02157)
*Xingyu Pan,Huan Wang,Jinjia Guo,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong,Jixi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RamanPFN是一个光谱表示框架，通过全局成分解混和局部振动子空间编码两种表示方法，在TabPFN推理前对拉曼光谱进行编码，在150个任务（74个公开数据集）上实现了回归RMSE平均降低19.6%、分类错误率进一步降低9.0%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱预测数据集通常样本量少但波数维度极高（数千个有序波数），信息存在于谱带内部及跨区域的相关性中。现有方法存在矛盾：传统潜变量化学计量学可处理共线性小样本但会模糊精细峰形态；深度光谱网络虽能解析结构但需任务特定训练；TabPFN虽无需任务特定参数拟合，但处理极宽输入时会进行特征子采样视图，无法保持相关谱带的联合可见性。

Method: RamanPFN包含两个核心编码组件：（1）全局成分解混——在完整光谱上构建非负坐标，使共享潜在变异的远方谱带占据共同的预测轴；（2）局部振动子空间编码——将连续波数区域编码为多个正交模式，保留峰形、强度和位置的独立变化。两种表示分别评估并在预测层面组合，随后输入TabPFN进行推理。

Result: 在150个任务（来自74个公开拉曼数据集）上的评估中，RamanPFN相对于直接TabPFN推理，在129个回归目标上平均降低均方根误差19.6%，并在21个分类任务上进一步降低剩余分类错误9.0%。

Conclusion: 明确的频谱表示方法可作为高维拉曼测量与可复用表格推理之间的有效接口，证明了在TabPFN前进行显式光谱编码能够显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: Raman spectroscopy enables non-destructive, label-free molecular characterization across materials science, biomedicine and process monitoring. Predictive Raman datasets often contain few labelled spectra and thousands of ordered wavenumbers, with informative variation within bands and across distant spectral regions. Latent-variable chemometrics accommodates collinear small-sample data but can obscure fine peak morphology, whereas deep spectral networks resolve this structure only after task-specific training. TabPFN avoids task-specific parameter fitting through pretrained in-context inference, but processes very wide inputs as feature-subsampled views that do not preserve joint visibility of related bands. We present RamanPFN, a spectral representation framework that encodes these dependencies before TabPFN inference. Global Compositional Unmixing constructs non-negative coordinates over the complete spectrum so that distant bands with shared latent variation occupy a common predictive axis. Local Vibrational Subspace Encoding represents contiguous wavenumber regions with multiple orthogonal modes that retain independent changes in peak shape, intensity and position. The representations are evaluated separately and combined at the prediction level. Evaluation covered 150 tasks from 74 public Raman datasets. RamanPFN reduced root-mean-square error by 19.6% on average across 129 regression targets relative to direct TabPFN inference and further reduced the remaining classification error by 9.0% across 21 classification tasks. These results establish explicit spectral representation as an effective interface between high-dimensional Raman measurements and reusable tabular inference.

</details>


### [137] [Empowering Credit Risk Detection in Weixin Pay with Billion-Scale Deep Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02168)
*Xin Liu,Xiyuan Chen,Chenglong Wu,Xuan Zong,Jun Zhou,Dawei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向大规模信用风险检测的风险感知重叠子图学习框架。该框架通过预算约束采样选择信息丰富的长尾节点以保留风险传播模式，并设计了跨子图一致性对齐机制来协调各局部子图中的节点表征，最终在微信支付的工业数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式图神经网络训练策略在子图划分时往往为了负载均衡而牺牲拓扑完整性，这会切断风险传播所需的长尾证据链；而重叠子图虽能恢复被切断的风险上下文，但会引入冗余和噪声，且忽视了不同局部子图间的表征对齐问题。

Method: 首先构建基础划分以保证负载均衡；然后进行预算约束采样，选择信息丰富的长尾节点以保留关键风险扩散模式并过滤噪声；最后设计跨子图一致性对齐机制，通过在重叠节点上施加对齐约束，将局部表征统一到全局一致的潜在空间中。

Result: 在微信支付的工业级生产数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明所提出的框架在风险检测任务上显著优于现有的多种策略，为工业图学习提供了可扩展且有效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该工作有效解决了大规模图神经网络分布式训练中拓扑完整性与负载均衡之间的矛盾，通过保留风险证据链和实现跨子图表征对齐，为工业级信用风险检测提供了一种兼顾可扩展性与准确性的新范式。

Abstract: Credit risk detection, particularly mitigating individual fraud, is crucial for maintaining the stability of digital financial ecosystems. Accurately identifying credit fraud among billions of users is critical for minimizing financial losses and safeguarding the sustainability of inclusive financial services. Given that credit fraud risks are often concealed within heterogeneous user-risk graphs, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as an effective tool for risk mining by capturing complex dependencies. To address the scalability bottleneck of industrial GNNs, distributed training based on subgraphs is indispensable. However, existing strategies often compromise topological integrity for load balancing. This can be catastrophic for risk detection, as it indiscriminately severs the long-tail evidence chains essential for risk propagation. Overlapping subgraphs can restore severed risk contexts but inevitably introduce redundancy and noise, while overlooking the representation alignment across different local subgraphs. In this paper, we propose a risk-aware overlapping subgraph learning framework for large-scale credit risk detection. We first construct base partitions to ensure load balance. Then, we perform budget-constrained sampling that selects informative long-tail nodes, thereby preserving critical risk diffusion patterns while filtering out noise. To mitigate representation inconsistency, we design a cross-subgraph consistency alignment mechanism. By enforcing alignment constraints on the overlapping nodes, we harmonize the local representations into a globally consistent latent space. Extensive experiments on Weixin Pay's production dataset demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms existing strategies for risk detection, offering a scalable and effective solution for industrial graph learning.

</details>


### [138] [Start Classifying: Categorical Critics for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02181)
*Zhijian Zhou,Long Li,Xuan Zhang,Zongkai Liu,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HL-Gauss PPO方法，将PPO中critic的标量MSE回归替换为基于离散化价值支持的分类目标（HL-Gauss平滑交叉熵），在RLVR场景下显著优于PPO和DAPO基线，且增益来源于分类价值学习本身而非输出头大小或二分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO在大语言模型训练中通常使用标量MSE回归训练critic，但在RLVR中稀疏二元奖励使得critic优化和校准尤为关键——较小的价值误差会直接扭曲PPO使用的标量优势估计。作者试图探究基于分类的训练目标是否能改善critic信号。

Method: 将标量MSE头替换为离散化价值支持上的分类预测头，使用平滑HL-Gauss目标的交叉熵进行训练。输出解码为标量期望值用于标准GAE和PPO，actor更新保持不变（非分布式的）。同时设计了一热、两热、伯努利两箱等对照组以排除混淆因素。

Result: 在数学推理、工具增强数学和Search-R1任务上，以及Qwen2.5和Qwen3骨干网络上，HL-Gauss PPO一致优于强PPO和DAPO基线。对照组表明增益并非来自更大的输出头或二分类本身。HL-Gauss还改善了Brier分数和校准误差，产生更对称、更低方差的价值优势。

Conclusion: 分类价值学习可作为RLVR中PPO critic的有效优化替代方案，在保持actor更新不变的前提下提升了训练效果和校准性能。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for large language models typically trains its critic by mean-squared-error (MSE) regression on scalar value targets. Although scalar MSE is statistically valid for estimating the conditional expected return, sparse binary rewards in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) make critic optimization and calibration especially consequential: small value errors directly distort the scalar advantages used by PPO. We study whether a classification-based training objective can improve this critic signal. HL-Gauss PPO replaces the scalar MSE head with a categorical predictor over a discretized value support, trained by cross-entropy against smoothed HL-Gauss targets. Its output is decoded to a scalar expectation for standard GAE and PPO; the actor update is therefore unchanged and is not distributional. Across mathematical reasoning, tool-augmented math, and Search-R1, and on both Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 backbones, HL-Gauss PPO consistently improves over strong PPO and DAPO baselines. Controls with one-hot, two-hot, and Bernoulli two-bin critics show that neither a larger output head nor binary classification alone explains the gains. On a common collection of reasoning prefixes, HL-Gauss improves Brier score and calibration error and yields more symmetric, lower-variance advantages. These results position categorical value learning as an effective optimization surrogate for PPO critics in RLVR.

</details>


### [139] [CRIP: Channel Level Representation Injection for Personalized One-Shot Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02222)
*Zijian Jiang,Chaoli Sun,Handing Wang,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRIP，一种在表示空间通过通道级特征对齐实现个性化单次联邦学习（OSFL）的框架。每个客户端上传特征提取器，服务器广播所有提取器给所有客户端，CRIP通过度量目标客户端与源客户端之间的通道级表示相似性，仅选择性融合最兼容的特征，解决领域异构下的特征偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OSFL方法在严重领域异构场景下表现受限，因为缺乏迭代知识交换。大多数方法依赖公共数据集进行知识蒸馏或利用统计信息进行参数级聚合，忽略了领域异构导致的特征偏移问题，阻碍了联邦学习在异构场景下的性能。

Method: CRIP框架的核心包括：（1）每个客户端上传特征提取器到服务器，服务器广播所有提取器回所有客户端；（2）在本地小批量数据上度量目标客户端与各源客户端之间的通道级表示相似性；（3）根据相似性分数选择性地融合最兼容的跨客户端特征，避免引入领域特定噪声。

Result: 在DomainNet、PACS和Office-Home等领域异构基准上的大量实验表明，CRIP在性能上持续优于本地模型和最先进的基线方法，验证了在极端领域异构下表示空间个性化的有效性。

Conclusion: CRIP通过在表示空间进行通道级特征对齐和有选择性融合，有效解决了OSFL在严重领域异构下的特征偏移问题，为个性化联邦学习提供了一种无需公共数据集的可行方案。

Abstract: One-shot federated learning (OSFL) has emerged as a promising collaborative model learning framework with only a single round of communication, offering significant advantages in communication efficiency and privacy preservation. However, OSFL often faces inherent limitations under severe domain heterogeneity across clients due to the lack of iterative knowledge exchange. Most existing OSFL methods require an auxiliary public dataset for knowledge distillation or leverage statistical information for parameter-level aggregation, overlooking feature shift caused by domain heterogeneity. To address these challenges, we propose CRIP, a personalized OSFL framework that operates in the representation space via channel-level feature alignment. To achieve this, each client uploads its feature extractor to the server, which broadcasts all extractors back to every client. Since not all source clients share compatible feature distributions with the target client, indiscriminate fusion of cross-client features would introduce domain-specific noise. Therefore, CRIP effectively measures the channel-wise representational similarity between the target client and each source client on a small local mini-batch, and selectively fuses only the most compatible features. Extensive experiments on domain-heterogeneous benchmarks such as DomainNet, PACS, and Office-Home demonstrate that CRIP consistently outperforms local models and state-of-the-art baselines, validating the effectiveness of representation-space personalization under extreme domain heterogeneity.

</details>


### [140] [Constrained Co-Design for Photonic Bayesian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02229)
*Hendrik Borras,Xiao Wang,Bernhard Klein,Robin Janssen,Frank Brückerhoff-Plückelmann,Wolfram Pernice,Holger Fröning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了光子贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）推理中硬件约束的影响，通过将光子BNN推理形式化为受约束的随机变分推断，并对随机性位置、模态、量化、编程误差和均值/方差界进行系统消融研究，提出了区分"可通过训练补偿"与"需要硬件/架构干预"的硬件约束的协同设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典神经网络在模糊或分布外数据上常产生过度自信的预测，这在安全关键场景中是重大隐患。贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）虽提供不确定性感知的框架，但重复采样导致高延迟、内存流量和能耗。光子概率计算利用光学随机性提供快速并行采样，但光子BNN并非理想采样器——模拟硬件的量化、编程误差、动态范围和可表示均值/方差等约束限制了可实现变分族的范围。因此需要系统研究哪些硬件约束限制了可扩展的光子BNN推理，以及这些约束的可容忍范围。

Method: 将光子BNN推理形式化为受约束的随机变分推断（constrained stochastic variational inference），系统进行消融研究，覆盖随机性位置、随机性模态、量化、编程误差和均值/方差边界等多个维度。在Dirty-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CINIC-10数据集上验证，并使用Fashion-MNIST和SVHN作为分布外基准，在耦合的硬件现实约束下测试硬件感知训练的效果。

Result: 研究表明，当所需的变分族在硬件中仍可表示时，硬件感知训练可以恢复预测性能和不确定性质量；而当违反可表示性限制时，则需要针对性的硬件修改。研究得出了具体的协同设计指南，能够区分可训练的补偿型硬件约束与需硬件/架构干预的约束。

Conclusion: 本文为光子BNN的硬件-算法协同设计提供了系统性框架和实用指南，明确了硬件约束与训练补偿能力之间的边界，为在安全关键场景中部署可扩展的光子贝叶斯神经网络提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Classical neural networks frequently produce overconfident predictions on ambiguous or out-of-distribution (OOD) data, a liability that grows with each AI system deployed in safety-critical real-world scenarios. Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) provide a principled framework for uncertainty-aware prediction by replacing deterministic parameters with probability distributions, but repeated sampling increases latency, memory traffic, and energy consumption. Photonic probabilistic computing offers a promising alternative by exploiting intrinsic optical stochasticity for fast and parallel sampling. However, photonic BNNs are not ideal samplers: analog constraints on quantization, programming error, dynamic range, and representable mean and variance restrict the variational families that can be implemented in hardware. In this work, we study which hardware-imposed constraints limit scalable photonic BNN inference, how these constraints can be represented, and which ranges can be tolerated by photonic BNNs beyond small proof-of-concept networks. We formulate photonic BNN inference as constrained stochastic variational inference and perform a systematic ablation study over stochasticity location, stochasticity modality, quantization, programming error, and mean/variance bounds. From these results, we derive concrete co-design guidelines that distinguish hardware constraints that can be compensated by training from those requiring hardware or architecture intervention. We validate these guidelines under coupled, hardware-realistic constraints on Dirty-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CINIC-10, using Fashion-MNIST and SVHN as OOD benchmarks, showing that hardware-aware training recovers predictive performance and uncertainty quality whenever the required variational family remains representable, whereas violations of representational limits require targeted hardware modifications.

</details>


### [141] [Assessing the Impacts of Imperfect Datasets on Client Selections in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02250)
*Yuan-Heng Tsai,Li-Hsing Yen,Yan-Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习（FL）中非独立同分布（non-IID）数据和噪声数据对模型性能的影响，并提出了一种隐私保护的客户端贡献评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，非IID数据和噪声数据集会导致模型精度低或收敛延迟高，而现有的客户端选择方法可能引入偏差，进一步降低学习性能。因此需要量化这些问题的影响并提出公平的评估方法。

Method: 首先通过实验测量非IID数据（包括数据量偏斜和标签分布偏斜）、噪声数据以及客户端选择公平性对模型精度和收敛速度的影响；随后提出一种隐私保护的评分方法来评估每个客户端在联邦学习中的贡献。

Result: 通过实验验证了所提出的隐私保护客户端贡献评分方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地分析了非IID和噪声数据对联邦学习性能的影响，并提供了可保护隐私的客户端贡献评估方案，有助于改善FL中的客户端选择策略。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a popular distributed learning framework where multiple clients perform local training and a server aggregates the locally updated models. FL enables decentralized training while preserving the privacy of clients' datasets. However, non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) or noisy datasets can lead to low model accuracy or high convergence latency. Precluding these clients through client selection may mitigate the problem, but heavily biased client selections may also degrade the learning performance. In this study, we first experimentally measure the impact of non-IID data (including skews in data quantity and label distribution), noisy data, and fairness in client selection on model accuracy and convergence. We then propose a privacy-preserving scoring method to assess each client's contribution in FL, with experiments conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed assessment.

</details>


### [142] [Z-PEFT: Zero-shot Backdoor Detection in Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via Canonical Spectral Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02271)
*Nicola Pitzalis,Donald Shenaj,Giacomo Cignoni,Andrea Cossu,Davide Bacciu,Antonio Carta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-PEFT，一种基于逐层谱特征（layer-wise spectral measures）的轻量级元分类器，用于在开放世界/零样本条件下检测参数高效微调（PEFT）模型中的后门攻击。研究发现传统闭世界设置下的高性能并不一定能转化为零样本后门检测的高准确率，而Z-PEFT在保持低计算成本的同时取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中大量PEFT模型从开源仓库下载，恶意攻击者可以发布植入后门的模型，造成严重安全隐患。现有后门检测方法大多在闭世界设置下设计评估，即训练和测试使用同一攻击类型，但在真实场景中会遇到未见过的攻击和数据集，因此需要研究更鲁棒的零样本后门检测方法。

Method: 提出Z-PEFT轻量级元分类器，仅依赖逐层的谱度量（spectral measures）作为特征进行分类。该方法通过分析模型权重的谱特性来区分正常模型与带后门的恶意模型，避免了复杂的权重分析过程，具有轻量化和可扩展的计算成本。

Result: 实验表明，闭世界设置下的强性能并不能保证零样本后门检测的高准确率。在现有权重空间检测器中，Z-PEFT在零样本条件下表现最佳，同时保持较低且可扩展的计算成本。

Conclusion: 权重空间后门检测需要关注开放世界的实际部署场景，Z-PEFT通过谱特征实现了轻量高效且具有良好泛化能力的后门检测方案，为PEFT模型安全下载提供了一种实用的防护机制。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuned (PEFT) models are frequently downloaded from open repositories by practitioners. This widespread practice creates a significant attack surface, as malicious actors can publish backdoored models that induce specific behaviors in response to predefined triggers. We study the problem of weight-space backdoor detection, where a detector classifier predicts whether a model is malicious using only its weights, enabling a lightweight safety mechanism. Most existing methods are designed and evaluated in a closed-world setting, where the detector is trained and tested on the same attack type. In contrast, we evaluate backdoor detection under novel conditions, including previously unseen attacks and datasets. We propose Z-PEFT, a lightweight meta-classifier that relies exclusively on layer-wise spectral measures for classification. Our experiments show that strong performance in the closed-world setting does not necessarily translate to high accuracy in zero-shot backdoor detection. Among weight-space detectors, Z-PEFT achieves the best performance while maintaining low and scalable computational cost.

</details>


### [143] [BRiG-AFA: Bellman Risk-to-Go Learning for Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02305)
*Jiaorong Feng,Qian Li,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\method的监督式替代方法，用于主动特征获取（AFA）。该方法为每个剩余预算学习独立的候选条件风险函数，通过Bellman目标反向拟合，推理时贪心最小化学习到的终端风险。实验表明在受控基准和Fashion-MNIST上显著优于单步消融版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFA方法存在不足：贪心规则容易训练但会忽略那些只有通过后续获取才能实现价值的上下文特征；强化学习和生成式方法则面临困难的优化问题或条件密度估计问题。作者希望提出一种既易于部署又具有非贪心视野的监督式替代方案。

Method: 从单步终端分类风险出发，为每个剩余预算学习一个独立的"候选条件风险函数"（candidate-conditioned risk-to-go function）。这些函数通过Bellman目标反向拟合（backward fitting），推理时仅利用已观测值、掩码、候选身份和剩余预算，贪心地最小化学习到的终端风险。

Result: 在受控非贪心基准上，预算为2和3时，方法相比单步消融分别提升4.84±2.17和4.39±1.10个百分点（5个种子的均值±标准误）。在Fashion-MNIST（20个候选像素）上，每个非平凡报告预算平均都有提升，4次获取时提升10.20±0.74个百分点；跨预算{2,4,8,12,16}的平均配对增益为3.50±0.37个百分点。MiniBooNE的三种子研究中，小预算结果好坏参半，但在8和16次获取时表现积极。

Conclusion: 实验结果为直接Bellman风险回归建立了可复现的机制级证据，同时界定了仍需进行的最新方法对比实验。作者承认MiniBooNE研究揭示了当前方法的边界，而非支持普适性论断。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) asks which unobserved feature to measure next for each test instance under a budget. Greedy rules are easy to train but can overlook context features whose value is realized only through later acquisitions, while reinforcement-learning and generative approaches introduce difficult optimization or conditional-density estimation. We introduce \method, a deployable, supervised alternative that learns a separate candidate-conditioned risk-to-go function for every remaining budget. Starting from the one-step terminal classification risk, the functions are fitted backward with Bellman targets; inference greedily minimizes the learned terminal risk using only observed values, the mask, candidate identity, and remaining budget. A controlled non-myopic benchmark shows the expected mechanism: at budgets two and three, \method improves accuracy over its one-step ablation by $4.84\pm2.17$ and $4.39\pm1.10$ percentage points (mean $\pm$ standard error over five seeds). On Fashion-MNIST with 20 candidate pixels, it improves accuracy at every nontrivial reported budget on average, including $10.20\pm0.74$ points at four acquisitions; its mean paired gain across budgets $\{2,4,8,12,16\}$ is $3.50\pm0.37$ points. A three-seed MiniBooNE study is mixed at small budgets but positive at 8 and 16 acquisitions, identifying a current boundary rather than supporting a universal claim. These results establish a reproducible mechanism-level case for direct Bellman risk regression and delimit the experiments still needed for state-of-the-art comparison.

</details>


### [144] [Diffusion Policy with Behavioral Advantage Correction for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02332)
*Botao Dong,Longyang Huang,Ning Pang,Hongtian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为优势校正的离线强化学习方法（DPBAC），通过行为策略的Q函数校正学习策略的Q函数来解决分布偏移问题，并结合扩散模型进行策略表示和分布匹配，在D4RL基准任务上取得了优于现有SOTA算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，行为数据与学习策略之间的分布偏移会导致Q值估计错误，进而误导策略优化方向。现有方法存在保守性过强或过度估计偏差的问题，需要一种更有效的校正机制。

Method: 提出BAC-PE方法，利用行为策略的Q函数来校正学习策略的Q函数估计，理论上分析了收敛性并推导了Q函数误差上界；同时采用扩散模型分别表示行为策略和学习策略，通过分布匹配进行策略正则化，并在训练中融入Q值引导以实现策略改进，最终形成DPBAC算法。

Result: 在D4RL基准的多个领域上进行实验，DPBAC相比现有SOTA算法表现出更优的性能，具备更强的策略表示能力，并能有效缓解Q值估计偏差。

Conclusion: DPBAC通过行为优势校正与扩散策略建模的结合，有效解决了离线强化学习中的分布偏移和Q值估计偏差问题，为离线RL提供了新的有效思路。

Abstract: In offline reinforcement learning (RL), the distribution shift between behavioral data and the learned policy can lead to erroneous \emph{Q}-value estimation, thereby misguiding the direction of policy optimization. To address this issue, we develop a behavioral advantage corrected policy evaluation (BAC-PE) approach, which utilizes the \emph{Q}-function of the behavior policy to correct the learned policy's \emph{Q}-function, thus mitigating pessimistic conservatism and overestimation bias. Furthermore, the convergence of BAC-PE is analyzed theoretically, and an upper bound on the difference between the learned \emph{Q}-function and the true \emph{Q}-function is derived. To alleviate distribution shift, this work employs diffusion models to represent both the behavior policy and the learned policy, performing distribution matching for accurate policy regularization. Additionally, \emph{Q}-value guidance is incorporated into the training process to achieve effective policy improvement. By combining BAC-PE with diffusion policy modeling, we propose the diffusion policy with behavioral advantage correction (DPBAC) algorithm. Compared to existing offline methods, DPBAC demonstrates stronger policy representation capabilities and effectively mitigates the bias in \emph{Q}-value estimation. Experimental results on multiple domains of D4RL tasks show that DPBAC achieves superior performance, with notable advantages over state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms.

</details>


### [145] [Qwen-CUA: Native Computer Use for (almost) Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02352)
*Dunjie Lu,Shuai Bai,Tianyi Bai,Sicheng Fan,Chang Gao,Jian Guan,Feng Hu,Mianqiu Huang,Xingyang Huang,Yizhen Jiang,Yuheng Jing,Dehui Kong,Ning Li,Dayiheng Liu,Shixuan Liu,Zheng Liu,Que Shen,Bowen Wang,Junli Wang,Chencan Wu,Rui Xie,Tianbao Xie,Zhihui Xie,Haiyang Xu,An Yang,Tao Yu,Wenzhen Yuan,Xi Zhang,Zhenru Zhang,Mingkang Zhu,Zhaoqing Zhu,Yizhong Cao,Kai Dang,Binyuan Hui,Kaixin Li,Junyang Lin,Haiquan Wang,Zekun Wang,Yiheng Xu,Fan Yan,Mengqi Yuan,Danyang Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Zhipeng Zhang,Fan Zhou,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-CUA，一个基于397B-A17B MoE架构的原生计算机使用智能体，仅通过截图观察和键盘鼠标操作，无需DOM树或API。通过大规模云端回滚部署（近10万vCPUs）、约4万个可验证任务和长视野工作流训练，在八个基准上超越Qwen3.7并与领先专有系统竞争，OSWorld-Verified达86.2，OSWorld 2.0达18.5/48.4。扩展到万亿参数得到Qwen-CUA-Max进一步提升表现，同时将攻击成功率从36.6降至16.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生计算机使用为智能体提供了操作几乎所有软件的通用界面，但面临长期状态追踪、大规模交互经验获取以及从稀疏但可验证结果中学习等挑战。现有方法依赖DOM树或任务特定API，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。

Method: 采用Qwen MoE骨干网络（397B-A17B），仅以截图作为观察输入，通过键盘鼠标事件执行操作。scaffold维持最多20张活动截图并以固定大小块折叠旧视觉历史。构建云端回滚舰队（近10万vCPUs、数万并发环境），收集约4万个可验证任务及个性化长视野工作流。使用可验证奖励和轨迹切片优化完整轨迹，迭代训练刷新监督数据并重新校准强化学习任务。

Result: 在八个基准上优于Qwen3.7，OSWorld-Verified达86.2，OSWorld 2.0达18.5/48.4。扩展到万亿参数的Qwen-CUA-Max进一步提升至87.6和21.2/53.3。RedTeamCUA攻击成功率从36.6降至16.4。额外进行了效率分析、浏览器部署和Bash增强实验。

Conclusion: 原生计算机使用可以成为广泛能力的智能体基础，可扩展的可验证交互和混合工具使用是关键研究方向。

Abstract: Native computer use offers a general interface for agents to operate almost any software available to people, but requires long-horizon state tracking, large-scale interactive experience, and learning from sparse yet verifiable outcomes. We introduce Qwen-CUA, a native computer-use agent with a 397B-A17B Qwen mixture-of-experts backbone. It observes only screenshots and acts through keyboard and mouse events, without DOM trees, accessibility metadata, or task-specific APIs. Its scaffold maintains up to 20 active screenshots and folds older visual history in fixed-size blocks to retain recent evidence while preserving reusable prompt prefixes. For training, we build a cloud rollout fleet with access to nearly 100,000 vCPUs and tens of thousands of concurrent environments, construct approximately 40,000 verifiable tasks, and collect personalized long-horizon workflows across everyday and professional software. We optimize complete trajectories with verifiable rewards and trajectory slicing, while iterative training runs refresh supervised data and recalibrate reinforcement-learning tasks. Across eight benchmarks, Qwen-CUA outperforms Qwen3.7 and remains competitive with leading proprietary systems, reaching 86.2 on OSWorld-Verified and 18.5/48.4 binary/partial completion on OSWorld 2.0. Scaling the same recipe to a model with over one trillion parameters yields Qwen-CUA-Max, improving these scores to 87.6 and 21.2/53.3. Qwen-CUA also reduces RedTeamCUA attack success from 36.6 to 16.4 relative to Qwen3.7. Efficiency analyses, a browser deployment, and Bash-augmented experiments further characterize practical behavior. These results establish native computer use as a broadly capable agent foundation and highlight scalable verifiable interaction and hybrid tool use as key directions.

</details>


### [146] [GLAIM: Learning Global and Local Adaptive Inter-Variable Dependency for Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02366)
*Mingyang Wang,Rongwen Li,Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLAIM框架，用于多变量时间序列插补，通过结合稳定的全局依赖建模与样本自适应的局部依赖细化，在九个真实数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么学习全局依赖（稳定但对样本变化和非平稳性适应差），要么学习局部依赖（自适应但在观测不足时不可靠），两者各有局限，无法同时兼顾稳定性与自适应性。

Method: GLAIM包含两个互补组件：Stable Global Dependency Constructor从互补的时间表示中提取稳健的全局变量间依赖关系，作为稳定骨干；Sample-Conditioned Dependency Refiner利用样本的时间状态和可用观测将骨干依赖自适应地细化到每个样本和时间步，实现不完整观测下的可靠局部细化。

Result: 在九个真实数据集上的实验表明，GLAIM在随机缺失和块缺失场景下均达到最先进性能，对缺失率变化具有鲁棒性，且全局与局部组件互补有效。

Conclusion: GLAIM通过全局-局部自适应的变量间依赖建模，有效解决了现有方法在稳定性与自适应性之间的权衡问题，为多变量时间序列插补提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation is fundamental to downstream analysis, yet modeling inter-variable dependencies with incomplete observations remains challenging. Existing methods learn global dependencies across samples or dynamic local dependencies per sample. Global dependencies are stable but adapt poorly to sample variations and temporal non-stationarity, whereas local dependencies are adaptive yet unreliable when observations are insufficient, causing erroneous information propagation. To address these limitations, we propose GLAIM, a Global-Local Adaptive Inter-variable Dependency Modeling framework for multivariate time series imputation. GLAIM comprises two complementary components. The Stable Global Dependency Constructor derives robust global inter-variable dependencies from complementary temporal representations, providing a stable backbone less affected by sample-specific missingness and noise. The Sample-Conditioned Dependency Refiner adapts this backbone to each sample and time step using its temporal state and available observations, enabling reliable local refinement under incomplete observations. Extensive experiments on nine real-world datasets demonstrate that GLAIM achieves state-of-the-art performance under random and block missingness, remains robust to missing-rate shifts, and benefits from its complementary global and local components. Code is available at https://github.com/LuRenjias/GLAIM.

</details>


### [147] [Gecko: Fast Private Inference via Secure Public Encoder Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02378)
*Cheng'an Wei,Kai Chen,Yue Zhao,Congyi Li,Shenchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gecko是一种隐私推理框架，在边界外运行公共编码器、仅对小型私有预测器进行加密评估，通过层级特征、固定的Fastfood投影和私有特征门控设计，防止特征空间捷径攻击，在0.4-2.2秒推理时间和至多10.8MB通信开销下实现与迁移学习基线相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私推理方案（如密码协议实现神经网络推断）速度过慢，难以实际部署。将公共编码器放在保护边界外、仅加密评估小型私有预测器的方案虽然高效，但存在安全缺陷：攻击者可能利用特征空间捷径，比原模型更容易学习到私有预测器的特征到输出映射，从而窃取模型。

Method: Gecko采用固定冻结的骨干网络贡献层级特征，使用固定的Fastfood投影对特征进行压缩，并通过私有特征门控机制为预测做准备。形式化定义了理想独立性和信息保持条件作为设计指南，并分别评估了组件重用提取攻击。

Result: 在图像和音频任务上，Gecko实现0.4-2.2秒的推理时间，最多10.8MB通信量，精度与迁移学习基线相当。在评估的攻击场景下，重用公共编码器对模型提取攻击者没有显著的额外优势。

Conclusion: Gecko证明了在保持加密预测器紧凑的同时，可以有效限制因公共编码器外置带来的额外模型提取风险，为高效且安全的隐私推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Private inference protects both user inputs and server models during neural network inference, but existing solutions remain too slow for practical deployment. This motivates recent efforts to run a public encoder, such as a pretrained backbone, outside the protection boundary and evaluate only a small private predictor cryptographically. While appealing for efficiency, this design is not inherently secure: naively offloading a public encoder may create a feature-space shortcut: an extraction adversary may learn the remaining private predictor's feature-to-output mapping more easily than the original model's input-to-output behavior.
  We present Gecko, designed to limit this additional risk while retaining a compact encrypted predictor. We leverage a frozen backbone that contributes hierarchical features, fixed Fastfood projections that compress them, and private feature gating that prepares them for prediction. We formalize ideal independence and information-preservation conditions as design guidance, then separately evaluate component-reuse extraction attacks. Across image and audio tasks, Gecko achieves 0.4-2.2 second inference with at most 10.8 MB communication and accuracy comparable to transfer-learning baselines. Under the evaluated attacks, reusing the offloaded public encoder provides no significant advantage to model-extraction adversaries. Source code and a demo are available at https://github.com/CassiniHuy/gecko-infer.

</details>


### [148] [From fragmented data to actionable design: Physics-calibrated learning for plastic upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02402)
*Jingyang Bai,Zijia Wang,Xiangyi Long,Marcos Millan,Binjian Nie,Mingyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PC-MG-MoE框架，通过物理校准、缺失值门控和负载均衡的混合专家模型，直接从不完整的塑料废弃物热化学升级实验数据中学习，无需目标值插补，实现了跨实验室异构条件下的准确预测和可解释的工程筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑料废弃物热化学升级是重要的升级回收途径，但现有实验文献因条件异质和报告不完整而碎片化。完整案例学习仅能保留10.99%的实验数据，而目标插补会引入有偏监督，严重限制了数据驱动模型的开发。

Method: 开发了物理校准、缺失值门控和负载均衡的混合专家模型（PC-MG-MoE）。该框架将结构化缺失转化为信息性学习信号，直接从不完全观测的实验数据中学习，无需目标值插补；重构物理一致的产物分布，适应跨实验室异质性，并提供可解释的模型行为。

Result: 在严格的源分组验证下，PC-MG-MoE在所有评估模型中实现了最低的聚合绝对误差，支持跨实验室异质性下的工程筛选。湿实验室实验提供外部对比，展示了关键组成依赖趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了将碎片化文献数据转化为实验可操作指导的迁移性方法，支持正向筛选、物理约束的反向设计、定向实验规划以降低实验工作量，以及基于新平台数据的实验室特定适配。

Abstract: Thermochemical upgrading of plastic waste is a key upcycling pathway, yet the experimental literature is fragmented by heterogeneous conditions and incomplete reporting. Complete-case learning would retain only 10.99% of the curated experiments, while target imputation can introduce biased supervision. Here we develop a Physics-Calibrated, Missingness-Gated, and Load-Balanced Mixture-of-Experts (PC-MG-MoE) framework that converts structured missingness into an informative learning signal. PC-MG-MoE learns directly from partially observed experiments without target imputation, reconstructs physically consistent product distributions, accommodates cross-laboratory heterogeneity, and provides interpretable model behaviour rather than black-box prediction alone. Under stringent source-grouped validation, it achieved the lowest aggregate absolute error among the evaluated models, supporting engineering screening under cross-laboratory heterogeneity. Wet-lab experiments provide an external comparison, showing key composition-dependent trends. Implemented as an interactive web-based workflow, PC-MG-MoE enables forward screening, physics-grounded constrained inverse design, targeted experimental planning that supports reduced experimental workload and trial-and-error, and laboratory-specific adaptation with new platform-specific data. This work establishes a transferable framework for converting fragmented literature data into experimentally actionable guidance for model-guided plastic upcycling and broader thermochemical systems.

</details>


### [149] [Deep Learning-Based Estimation of Ground Reaction Forces in Parkinsonian Gait Using an Optimized Set of IMU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02408)
*Run Lin,Yingtian Tang,Jiawen Xu,Dongfei Huo,Lefan Wang,Helen Dawes,Dominic J. Farris,Dong Wang,Xijin Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一种基于深度学习框架的帕金森病（PD）双侧垂直地面反作用力（vGRFs）估算方法，利用优化的可穿戴惯性测量单元（IMUs）配置，通过混合CNN-BiLSTM模型在61名PD患者和65名健康对照者数据上训练，实现了高精度估算（R²=0.98个体内，R²=0.93/0.91个体间），并识别出PD患者的最佳传感器配置为4个IMU，最少2个IMU仍可稳健估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的帕金森病步态分析依赖实验室设备（如测力台）测量地面反作用力，而基于IMU的估算虽然可行，但在PD这种高变异性、高复杂性的病理步态中仍具挑战。现有方案通常需要多个传感器，影响实用性和患者依从性，且尚无研究将深度学习应用于此问题。作者旨在提出首个深度学习框架，用最少的IMU实现PD患者vGRFs的精准估算。

Method: 采用混合CNN-BiLSTM深度学习模型，分别使用61名PD患者和65名健康对照者（HC）的数据进行训练，输入来自13个IMU的传感器数据，输出为双侧垂直地面反作用力。通过系统性实验评估不同传感器配置对估算精度的影响，对比PD和HC人群的最优传感器布局，并探索最小可行的传感器配置方案。

Result: 模型在个体内测试中达到R²=0.98的高精度，个体间泛化能力也较强（HC为R²=0.93，PD为R²=0.91）。传感器配置显著影响估算精度，且PD患者与HC的最优传感器放置位置不同。PD患者在减少到单个IMU时精度显著下降，最优配置为4个IMU，但仅用2个IMU的最小设置仍能实现稳健的估算。

Conclusion: 该研究证明深度学习结合优化的IMU配置可以有效估算帕金森病的双侧vGRFs，其中2-4个IMU的紧凑配置即可满足需求，为可穿戴vGRF步态分析系统提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，有望推动PD及其他病理步态的临床评估、远程监测和个性化康复发展。

Abstract: Accurate gait analysis in Parkinson's disease (PD) typically relies on laboratory-based systems to capture biomechanical data, such as ground reaction forces (GRFs). Estimating GRFs using inertial measurement units (IMUs) provides a feasible alternative. However, this approach remains challenging in pathological gait like PD due to its high variability and complexity. Moreover, existing monitoring approaches often require multiple body-mounted sensors, which limit practicality and reduce patient compliance. To date, no study has investigated the application of deep learning approaches to address this challenge. This study proposes, for the first time, a deep learning framework to estimate bilateral vertical GRFs (vGRFs) in PD using an optimized set of wearable IMUs. A hybrid CNN-BiLSTM model was trained separately on data from 61 PD patients and 65 healthy controls (HC) using 13 IMUs. The model achieved high intra-subject accuracy ($R^2$ = 0.98) and strong inter-subject generalization ($R^2$ = 0.93 for HC, $R^2$ = 0.91 for PD). Sensor configuration was found to significantly influence estimation accuracy, with optimal sensor placement varying between PD patients and HC. For PD patients, estimation accuracy dropped markedly when reducing to a single IMU. The optimal configuration for PD used four IMUs. We identified a minimal setup with only two IMUs still enabled robust estimation. This compact setup offers a practical and scalable solution. Overall, the proposed approach supports the development of wearable vGRF-based gait analysis systems for Parkinsonian gait and potentially other pathological conditions, enabling accessible clinical assessments, remote monitoring, and personalized rehabilitation.

</details>


### [150] [Why Large Language Models Fail at Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02412)
*Marta Garnelo,Wojciech M. Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了为什么大型语言模型（LLMs）在表格数据预测任务上表现不佳。通过系统评估五个假设，发现噪声处理、CSV格式、token化、每查询测试点数和维度中，前四个均被证伪，唯独维度是关键因素——LLM是九种方法中唯一随着维度增加精度下降的，且在高维下无法被任何噪声增强的经典模型所模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在众多任务中表现出色，但在最基础的表格数据预测任务上却一直落后于经典基线方法。尽管表格基础模型领域发展迅速，但"为什么通用LLM会失败"这一根本问题悬而未决，本文旨在系统性地解答这一问题。

Method: 作者在纯推理场景下（单次生成、无工具、无agentic、无微调）评估了一个前沿LLM，系统测试了五个失败假设：通过受控实验证伪了(a)噪声/非线性、(b)CSV格式、©数字token化、(d)每查询测试点数；然后通过随机线性投影31个基准数据集来系统性操纵维度，并将LLM与252个配置的经典模型进行行为对比。

Result: 五个假设中(a)-(d)均被证伪，维度是决定性因素。LLM是九种方法中唯一随维度增加精度下降的模型，而所有经典基线保持平稳或提升。在二维情况下LLM的行为类似局部距离法（网格一致率高达91.6%），但高维下没有任何经典模型（即使添加了维度相关的调参噪声）能复现其预测。

Conclusion: LLM在表格数据上的能力随维度增加而消失，且这种行为不被任何噪声损坏的经典学习器所模仿。这解释了为什么LLM在其他领域如此强大却在表格任务上输给五十年前的经典基线。作者不声称找到了内部机制，而是更克制地将机制问题留作开放问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become the default tool for a remarkable range of tasks, yet they have had conspicuously little success at one of the most common machine learning workloads: predictive analytics over tabular data. This gap is the founding premise of the fast-growing field of tabular foundation models, but the question of why generic LLMs fail has remained open. We study a frontier LLM in its purest inference regime - a single generation pass over a prompt containing the full training and test data, with no tools, no agentic scaffolding, and no fine-tuning - and systematically evaluate five hypotheses for the failure: (a) an inability to handle noisy or non-linearly-separable data; (b) the linearised CSV format obscuring column structure; (c) the tokenisation of numeric values; (d) the number of test points classified per query; and (e) the dimensionality of the input. Controlled experiments falsify (a)-(d). Dimensionality, in contrast, is decisive: sweeping random linear projections of thirty-one benchmark datasets, the LLM is the only method among nine whose accuracy decreases as dimensionality grows, while every classical baseline stays flat or improves. A behavioural comparison against 252 configured classical models finds that in two dimensions the LLM predicts like a local, distance-based method (up to 91.6% grid agreement), but in higher dimensions no classical model - even when augmented with tuned, dimension-dependent noise - reproduces its predictions. We do not claim to have identified the internal mechanism; our results show, more modestly, that the LLM's capability dissolves with dimension in a way no noise-corrupted classical learner mimics - which explains why LLMs, so capable elsewhere, keep losing to fifty-year-old baselines on tables, while leaving the mechanism of the prediction as an open question.

</details>


### [151] [Foundations of Reinforcement Learning and Control:Connections and New Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02433)
*Claire Vernade,Onno Eberhard,Martha White,Florian Dörfler,Csaba Szepesvári,Miroslav Krstic,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程介绍了自适应控制、演员-评论家强化学习算法，以及将这两种范式结合用于数据驱动决策的新方法，并以经典运动控制问题为例，旨在帮助两个领域的专家相互理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习与控制理论虽然同源于动态规划，但两个领域在方法论、目标和研究文化上存在显著差异，彼此之间的沟通鸿沟阻碍了跨领域合作与发展。

Method: 通过教程形式介绍自适应控制与演员-评论家强化学习算法，并提出一种将两者结合的新范式，应用于经典运动控制问题以进行数据驱动决策。

Result: 为理解两个领域的核心差异提供了基础，并为各自领域的专家提供了了解对方工具和方法的洞见。

Conclusion: 该教程旨在弥合强化学习与控制理论两个社区之间的差距，促进跨学科理解和合作。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and control theory are two adjacent scientific fields that focus on optimizing the controller of unknown dynamical systems using feedback. While both fields have common roots in dynamic programming, they have evolved with distinct methodologies, goals, and cultures. Despite decades of mutual influence, a significant gap persists between the two communities. This tutorial introduces adaptive control, actor-critic reinforcement algorithms, and a new way to combine these two paradigms for data-driven decision making on a classical locomotion control problem. Our aim is to provide a foundation for understanding the core differences between the two approaches and insights to help experts in each field better understand and engage with the tools and approaches of the other.

</details>


### [152] [Aggregate-then-Calibrate for Human-centered Assessment with Theoretical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02455)
*Zejun Xie,Xintong Li,Guang Wang,Desheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Aggregate-then-Calibrate (AtC)两阶段框架，将人类比较性判断与预测模型分数相结合进行以人为中心的评估。第一阶段通过考虑标注者可靠性的排名聚合模型生成共识排序，第二阶段通过等渗投影校准模型分数以保持序数一致性。理论上证明了异构标注者建模、等渗校准风险界和渐近最优性，实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的评估任务缺乏可验证的真实标签，现有方法陷入两难：仅依赖人类判断会面临专业知识异构和评分尺度不一致的问题，仅依赖模型生成分数则需从不完美的代理指标或不完整的特征中学习。需要一种能互补利用两者优势的框架。

Method: 两阶段框架AtC：阶段1使用考虑标注者可靠性的排名聚合模型，将异构的比较性判断聚合成共识排序；阶段2将任何预测模型的分数通过等渗（isotonic）投影校准到该排序上，在保持序数一致性的同时尽可能保留模型的定量信息。理论分析涵盖标注者异质性建模的效率、等渗校准的风险界以及渐近性能优势。

Result: 理论结果显示：(1)建模标注者异质性比同质性假设获得更高效的共识估计；(2)即使共识排序被错误设定，等渗校准仍享有风险界；(3)AtC在渐近意义下优于仅用模型的评估。在半合成和真实数据集上，AtC相比仅人类或仅模型评估持续提升了准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AtC框架弥合了判断聚合与无模型校准之间的鸿沟，为真实标签成本高、稀缺或无法验证的以人为中心评估任务提供了原则性的解决方案，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Human-centered assessment tasks, which are essential for systematic decision-making, rely heavily on human judgment and typically lack verifiable ground truth. Existing approaches face a dilemma: methods using only human judgments suffer from heterogeneous expertise and inconsistent rating scales, while methods using only model-generated scores must learn from imperfect proxies or incomplete features. We propose Aggregate-then-Calibrate (AtC), a two-stage framework that combines these complementary sources. Stage-1 aggregates heterogeneous comparative judgments into a consensus ranking using a rank-aggregation model that accounts for annotator reliability. Stage-2 calibrates any predictive model's scores by an isotonic projection onto the order, enforcing ordinal consistency while preserving as much of the model's quantitative information as possible. Theoretically, we show: (1) modeling annotator heterogeneity yields strictly more efficient consensus estimation than homogeneity; (2) isotonic calibration enjoys risk bounds even when the consensus ranking is misspecified; and (3) AtC asymptotically outperforms model-only assessment. Across semi-synthetic and real-world datasets, AtC consistently improves accuracy and robustness over human-only or model-only assessments. Our results bridge judgment aggregation with model-free calibration, providing a principled recipe for human-centered assessment when ground truth is costly, scarce, or unverifiable.

</details>


### [153] [RoMeRL: Balancing Feedback Coverage and the Memory-Reward Trap in Self-Evolving Agent Memory via Reduced-Order Utility States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02508)
*Yi Yang,Zhennan Chen,Yihong Zhuang,Tiehan Fan,Yinan Chen,Jian Li,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoMeRL通过将不断增长的轨迹索引效用空间压缩为固定维度的按任务记忆状态（按结果极性和记忆动态分解），来解决自进化LLM代理记忆系统中的反馈分散和记忆奖励陷阱问题。在ALFWorld和LifelongAgentBench上，该方法将Cold-Q比率降低80%、反馈密度提升约6倍、内存占用减少84.4%、LLM调用减少21.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化LLM代理的记忆系统面临两个紧密耦合的挑战：一是轨迹索引效用随交互历史增长，导致有限反馈分散在日益扩大的状态空间中；二是轨迹级奖励被共同分配给协同检索的记忆，使无关经验获得误导性的效用更新，从而陷入记忆奖励陷阱。

Method: 提出降阶记忆强化学习（RoMeRL），用固定维度的按任务记忆状态来表示不断增长的轨迹索引效用空间，该状态按结果极性和记忆动态进行因子分解。通过固定的语义坐标集合来融入新经验，对内容进行更新或替换，从而将反馈集中在有界效用支持上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和LifelongAgentBench基准上，RoMeRL显著提升任务性能，将Cold-Q比率降低80.0%，反馈密度提高约6.0倍，维护内存大小减少84.4%，LLM调用减少21.1%。理论分析表明降阶参数化增加了每个效用坐标的平均反馈，并刻画了错误坐标的稳态占用。

Conclusion: 降阶效用状态能够支持高效的自进化代理记忆，同时限制持续的奖励污染，为LLM代理的长期记忆学习提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-based memory systems for self-evolving LLM agents face two tightly coupled challenges. First, trajectory-indexed utilities grow with the interaction history, thereby dispersing limited feedback over an ever-expanding state space. Second, because trajectory-level rewards are jointly assigned to co-retrieved memories, irrelevant experiences may receive misleading utility updates and consequently enter the memory-reward trap. To address these challenges, we introduce Reduced-Order Memory Reinforcement Learning (RoMeRL), which represents the growing trajectory-indexed utility space using a fixed-dimensional per-task memory state factorized by outcome polarity and memory dynamics. RoMeRL incorporates new experiences through a fixed set of semantic coordinates whose contents are updated or replaced over time, thereby concentrating feedback over a bounded utility support. Theoretically, we show that this reduced-order parameterization increases the average feedback received by each utility coordinate and characterize the steady-state occupancy of erroneous coordinates under a generic coordinate-transition model. Empirically, across ALFWorld and LifelongAgentBench, RoMeRL improves task performance, reduces the Cold-Q ratio by 80.0%, increases feedback density by approximately 6.0 times, reduces the maintained memory size by 84.4%, and cuts LLM calls by 21.1%. These results show that reduced-order utility states support efficient self-evolving agent memory while limiting persistent reward contamination. Code is available at: https://github.com/YOUNG-fnxm/RoMeRL

</details>


### [154] [Analytic Planning under Uncertainty with Moment Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02519)
*Shishir Sharma,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在随机环境中无需限制性策略或奖励结构即可进行分布感知规划的新方法。通过二次动作-价值参数化将Bellman备份简化为对状态价值函数的期望，并利用预测转移分布与价值函数类之间的兼容性原则，实现闭合形式的备份。采用高斯转移模型配径向基价值函数，可同时传播预测均值和协方差，在连续控制任务中降低了目标方差并产生了校准良好的预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于模型强化学习在随机环境中需要处理预测不确定性，但全状态分布解析传播通常需要限制性策略或奖励结构才能保持可解性。现有方法要么依赖随机采样导致高目标方差，要么使用忽略预测协方差的确定性点估计。研究者希望探索无需这些约束即可实现分布感知规划的可能性。

Method: 首先使用二次动作-价值参数化将Bellman备份化简为对状态价值函数的期望；然后提出预测转移分布与价值函数类之间的兼容性原则，使该期望在分布矩上具有解析形式；最后将该原则实例化为高斯转移模型与径向基价值函数的组合，得到可同时传播预测均值和协方差的闭合形式备份。

Result: 在连续控制的随机观测环境中，该方法有效降低了目标方差，并产生了校准良好的预测不确定性。

Conclusion: 该方法为使用学习到的分布模型进行规划提供了原则性框架，证明了无需采样或确定性近似即可实现分布感知的模型基强化学习。

Abstract: Effective model-based reinforcement learning in stochastic environments requires planning that accounts for predictive uncertainty. Propagating full state distributions analytically offers a principled way to do this, but has traditionally required restrictive policy or reward structures to remain tractable. Consequently, modern deep reinforcement learning has largely retreated to either stochastic sampling, which introduces significant target variance, or deterministic point estimates that ignore predictive covariance entirely. We investigate whether distribution-aware planning is possible without these constraints. Using a quadratic action-value parameterization, we first reduce the Bellman backup to an expectation over the state-value function alone; the key idea is then a compatibility principle between the predictive transition distribution and the value function class, under which this expectation is analytic in the distribution's moments. We instantiate this principle with a Gaussian transition model paired with a radial-basis value function, yielding a closed-form backup that propagates both predictive mean and covariance. Empirically, our approach reduces target variance and yields well-calibrated predictive uncertainty under stochastic observations in continuous control, providing a principled framework for planning with learned distribution models.

</details>


### [155] [Uncertainty Is Not Enough: Value-of-Information Routing for Mixtures of LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02528)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VI-MoLE方法，将低秩适应专家路由形式化为"信息价值"认证分配问题，通过校准数据上的同时上界风险证书来区分"当前模糊"、"可恢复风险"和"残余风险"，在全局适配器预算下选择单位成本边际风险降低最大的token-层动作，最终证书决定回答或弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态路由器在出现不确定性时激活更多专家，但这一规则隐含假设"不确定=需要更多计算"，忽略了不确定样本可能包含互补证据也可能在所有专家一致后仍然模糊的情况，需要区分可恢复风险与残余风险。

Method: 将路由形式化为认证的信息价值分配：学习每个专家前缀后的反事实残余风险，在保留校准数据上转换为同时上界风险证书，按单位成本的边际风险降低最大化的贪心准则分配全局适配器预算，最后由终端证书决定回答或弃权。理论证明同时证书有效性和在递减认证收益下贪心分配的优性。

Result: 在匹配计算量精度、证书覆盖率、风险-覆盖、分布偏移和尾部延迟等指标上，与固定和动态MoE-LoRA路由器对比评估。

Conclusion: 相比不确定性门控，VI-MoLE能够清晰区分当前模糊、可恢复风险与残余风险，实现更优的适配器预算分配和决策可靠性。

Abstract: Mixtures of low-rank adaptation experts increase parameter-efficient capacity by routing each input through a subset of adapters. Recent dynamic routers activate more experts when the router or prediction is uncertain. This rule silently equates uncertainty with useful additional computation: an uncertain example may contain complementary, unqueried expert evidence, but it may instead remain ambiguous after every expert agrees. We formulate routing as certified value-of-information allocation. VI-MoLE learns the counterfactual risk remaining after each expert prefix, converts these predictions into simultaneous upper-risk certificates on held-out calibration data, and spends a global adapter budget on the token--layer action with the largest certified marginal risk reduction per unit cost. A terminal certificate then decides whether to answer or abstain. Unlike an uncertainty gate, this procedure distinguishes present ambiguity from recoverable and residual risk. We prove simultaneous certificate validity, optimal greedy allocation under diminishing certified gains, and allocation regret under value-estimation error. The evaluation protocol tests matched-compute accuracy, certificate coverage, risk--coverage, distribution shift, and tail latency against fixed and dynamic MoE-LoRA routers.

</details>


### [156] [Benchmarking Sheaf Neural Networks for Inductive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02558)
*Stefano Fiorini,Edoardo Coppola,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对Sheaf神经网络（SNNs）进行归纳式（inductive）学习协议下的基准测试，通过1,890个受控实验揭示了限制映射（restriction maps）是主导设计选择、更大的stalk尺寸增加容量但不增强远距离信息传递能力、架构组件比sheaf特定设计空间影响更大的关键洞见，并发现SNNs在归纳设置下能迁移但未达最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SNNs具有坚实的理论基础和有前景的转导式（transductive）结果，但现有评估几乎仅限于转导式节点分类，对归纳式协议下的行为认知完全空白，亟需填补这一研究缺口。

Method: 对sheaf设计空间进行系统基准测试，涵盖三种扩散机制（神经sheaf扩散、sheaf注意力、与GATv2结合的sheaf注意力）、三种限制映射参数化方式、三种stalk维度及六种现代GNN架构组件；采用消息传递重表述以避免构建沉重的sheaf拉普拉斯算子，使完整设计空间可在跨图批处理下训练。

Result: 在14个归纳式数据集上的1,890个控制实验中，发现限制映射是主导设计选择且一般映射更优；更大的stalk尺寸增加容量但不增强远距离可达性；架构组件比sheaf特定设计空间本身更影响性能差异。在匹配协议下，SNNs可迁移到归纳设置但未达最强基线，差距因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 单个sheaf配置可跨数据集泛化，因此应将调优精力更多投入到周边架构配方中，而非sheaf算子本身。

Abstract: Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) generalize message passing by replacing scalar edge weights of standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) with learnable, edge-dependent restriction maps between node stalks. Despite their strong theoretical foundations and promising transductive results, SNNs have been evaluated almost exclusively on transductive node classification, leaving their behaviour under inductive protocols unknown. We address this gap through the first systematic benchmark of the sheaf design space, evaluating three diffusion mechanisms (neural sheaf diffusion, sheaf attention, and sheaf attention with Graph Attention Network v2), three restriction-map parameterizations, three stalk dimensions, and six modern GNN architectural components, within a message-passing reformulation that never assembles the heavy sheaf Laplacian, making the full design space trainable under cross-graph batching. Across $1{,}890$ controlled experiments on 14 inductive datasets, multiple insights emerge: restriction maps are the dominant design choice and general maps are preferable, larger stalks add capacity but not long-range reach, architectural components explain more performance variation than the entire sheaf-specific design space itself. Under a matched protocol, SNNs transfer to inductive settings but do not reach the strongest baselines, with gaps being dataset-dependent. Practically, a single sheaf configuration can generalize across datasets, so effort is better spent tuning the surrounding architectural recipe than the sheaf operator itself.

</details>


### [157] [Pseudorandom Streams within Diffusion Models Act as Learnable Inputs That Affect Generation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02575)
*Shengzhi Deng,Chenqi Ye,Yanze Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在有限精度硬件上使用的伪随机数生成器（PRNG）对其训练和生成质量的影响。研究发现，伪随机轨道的可预测结构会成为模型可学习的输入，不同的轨道导致显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量，且与IID基线相比遵循经验幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型依赖随机输入，但在有限精度硬件上这些"随机"实为伪随机规则的确定性数值轨道。由于训练损失和梯度依赖于每个优化步骤消耗的具体伪随机值，可访问的轨道结构可能成为可学习输入，影响训练和生成。论文旨在系统性地探究伪随机源是否以及如何影响扩散模型的性能。

Method: 论文采用两种测量工具：(1) 小型多层感知机（MLP）从历史序列预测轨道下一个值，度量序列的可预测性；(2) 扩散探针（diffusion probe）——用在线随机张量替换真实图像，同时保留扩散架构和训练目标，衡量目标系统能否利用轨道结构。实验在控制边际统计量并排除动力学和有限精度失败后，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行评估。

Result: 剩余的伪随机轨道仍产生显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量。两种测量指标与宏观生成退化之间存在强秩相关，但局部排序不同。经IID基线归一化后，探针损失与真实数据扩散损失近似遵循经验幂律，且两个数据集上的指数不同。

Conclusion: 伪随机源不仅是分布选择，更是一个依赖于具体模型的结构化输入。这一发现意味着扩散模型的随机性维度需要被当作模型设计的一部分来审慎对待。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on stochastic inputs, yet on finite-precision hardware, the "randomness" they consume is realized as deterministic numerical orbits generated by pseudorandom rules. Accessible orbit structure can become a learnable input and affect both training and generation because the realized loss and its gradient depend on the concrete pseudorandom values consumed at each optimization step. A small multilayer perceptron predicts the next value of an orbit from its recent history, measuring general sequence predictability. A diffusion probe replaces real images with online random tensors while preserving the diffusion architecture and training objective, measuring whether the target system can exploit orbit structure. After controlling marginal statistics and screening out clear dynamical and finite-precision failures, the remaining orbits still produce markedly different diffusion losses and generation quality on MNIST and CIFAR-10. Both measures show strong rank correlations with macroscopic generation degradation, although their local rankings differ. After normalization by the IID baseline, the probe loss and the real-data diffusion loss approximately follow an empirical power law, with different exponents on the two datasets. These results suggest that a pseudorandom source is not only a distributional choice, but also a model-dependent structured input.

</details>


### [158] [Smooth Reparameterizations of Functions on Simplicial Product Spaces: Applications to Probabilistic Tensor Decomposition and Functional Data Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02576)
*Shashwat Kumar,Arafat Rahman,Anuj Srivastava,P. -A. Absil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在单纯形乘积空间上的优化问题，提出用光滑逐元素严格凸的参数化方法替代乘积单纯形，将约束优化转化为流形上的无约束优化，并证明二阶KKT点在两种表述间的对应关系，进而提出Riemannian梯度下降（RGD）算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积单纯形上的约束优化问题广泛存在于多个领域（如低秩离散多元概率分布分解、SRVF函数配准），但传统投影梯度下降（PGD）等方法效率有限。作者希望探索一种替代参数化方式，使问题在光滑流形上无约束求解，从而提升优化性能。

Method: 采用光滑、逐元素严格凸的重新参数化方法将乘积单纯形映射为流形上的无约束优化问题；理论证明流形上的二阶KKT点映射回乘积单纯形后对应弱二阶KKT点；基于此设计Riemannian Gradient Descent（RGD）算法求解重参数化后的问题。

Result: RGD算法在求解重参数化问题上优于Projected Gradient Descent（PGD），并且在进行曲线配准时能更忠实地保留原始函数的形状特征。

Conclusion: 将乘积单纯形上的优化问题通过严格凸参数化转化为流形上的无约束问题在理论和实践上都是可行的，RGD提供了一种更优的替代求解方案。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems defined on product spaces of simplices. Examples of this class of problems include learning low-rank discrete multivariate probability distributions via simplex constrained tensor decomposition and performing functional data registration under the Square Root Velocity Function (SRVF) representation. In this work, we demonstrate the feasibility of replacing the product simplex with a smooth, elementwise strictly convex reparameterization, resulting in an unconstrained optimization problem on a manifold. We show that performing such a reparameterization results in the second order Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points on the smooth manifold being mapped to the weak second order KKT points on the product simplex. This leads to a Riemannian Gradient Descent (RGD) algorithm for solving the reparameterized problem, which outperforms Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and provides a more faithful representation of the original function shapes while performing curve registration.

</details>


### [159] [GradCuit: Credit-Assigned Gradient Flow Enables Robust and Interpretable Test-Time Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02585)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qi Shen,Hengli Li,Zhaowei Zhang,Song-Chun Zhu,Chi Zhang,Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GradCuit方法，通过在Transformer中间层插入可优化的潜变量状态，利用因果自注意力机制为梯度提供从续写token到潜变量的可微路径，实现直接对内部推理状态的序列级奖励加权优化。在五个指令微调模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上，平均准确率达64.5%，超越思维链提示6.6个百分点，超越现有最强方法2.4个百分点，同时具有更强的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于优化的潜在推理方法通过解码token间接地将潜变量状态与推理轨迹关联，导致序列级信用分配不直接，无法清晰理解潜变量更新如何影响后续推理。作者希望直接对内部推理状态进行优化，建立从续写序列到潜变量的直接梯度路径。

Method: GradCuit在选定的Transformer层中，于提示的隐藏表示与生成的续写之间插入可优化的潜变量状态。利用因果自注意力机制，使每个续写token的对数概率都有一条经由剩余Transformer模块到达每个前置潜变量的可微路径，从而将整个续写的奖励加权梯度直接分配给潜变量。

Result: 在五个指令微调骨干模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上，平均准确率达64.5%。超越思维链提示6.6个百分点，超越最强对比方法2.4个百分点。在七个学习率设置下，标准差从1.53降至0.82，随机游走变体仍具竞争力。Token级梯度归因显示潜变量影响集中在推理连接词上，层分析显示早期至中层Transformer是最有效的优化空间。

Conclusion: GradCuit通过直接从结果反馈优化内部推理，开辟了稳健且可解释的测试时扩展新方向，使LLM能够适应自身的推理方式，而非仅仅重新生成、采样或重排序输出。

Abstract: Optimization-based latent reasoning improves large language model outputs by optimizing instance-specific continuous states at test time while keeping model parameters frozen. Existing methods, however, typically connect these states to the reasoning trajectory through decoded tokens, making sequence-level credit assignment indirect and obscuring how latent updates shape subsequent reasoning. We introduce GradCuit (gradient through circuit), which inserts optimizable latent states at a selected Transformer layer between the hidden representations of the prompt and the generated continuation. Causal self-attention provides every continuation-token log-probability with a differentiable path to every preceding latent state through the remaining Transformer blocks, enabling reward-weighted gradients from the entire continuation to be assigned directly to the latents. Across five instruction-tuned backbones, three reasoning benchmarks, and two answer formats, GradCuit achieves an average accuracy of 64.5%, outperforming chain-of-thought prompting by 6.6 percentage points and the strongest competing method by 2.4 points. GradCuit also demonstrates greater robustness: across seven learning-rate settings, it consistently outperforms LatentSeek while reducing the standard deviation of accuracy from 1.53 to 0.82, and even its random-walk variant remains competitive with LatentSeek. For interpretability, token-level gradient attribution reveals that latent influence concentrates on reasoning-connector tokens, while layer analysis identifies early-to-middle Transformer layers as the most effective optimization space. By directly optimizing internal reasoning from outcome feedback, GradCuit opens a new axis of robust and interpretable test-time scaling, where LLMs adapt how they reason rather than merely regenerate, sample, or rerank outputs.

</details>


### [160] [onepot-Bench 0: towards lab-aware in silico chemistry benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02595)
*Brandon Wang,Andrei S. Tyrin,Daniil A. Boiko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了onepot-Bench 0，一个专有的基准测试套件，用于评估语言模型在湿实验室合成化学方面的能力，包含三个互补的评估任务：ChemAbacus（化学信息学素养）、SynthRefusal（安全与拒绝行为）和SynthBench（反应结果预测和催化剂选择），使用私有实验数据避免训练数据泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估很少衡量语言模型在物理实验室中做出可靠决策所需的能力，且依赖可能已出现在模型训练语料中的公开数据，因此难以精确评估语言模型在实验室科学中的真实能力。

Method: 构建包含三个互补评估的专有基准套件onepot-Bench 0：ChemAbacus评估无工具的化学信息学素养和数值推理；SynthRefusal表征对不同类型药物目标的安全性和拒绝行为；SynthBench使用实验室内部生成的私有实验数据进行反应结果预测和催化剂选择评估。

Result: 论文提出了onepot-Bench 0基准套件，包含三个维度评估：基础能力（ChemAbacus）、可靠性（SynthRefusal）和深层知识（SynthBench），共同覆盖实验室可靠表现所需的技能。

Conclusion: onepot-Bench 0通过专有数据和多维度评估设计，能够更准确地衡量语言模型在湿实验室合成化学中的实际能力，弥补了现有评估在数据隐私和实验室决策能力测量方面的不足。

Abstract: Language models are playing an increasingly important role in laboratory science, performing tasks such as experiment planning, execution, and post-hoc analysis. However, precisely measuring their abilities is difficult, as scientific capabilities require a mixture of both problem-solving skills and domain-specific intuition. Existing evaluations rarely measure the capabilities required to make reliable decisions in a physical laboratory and often rely on public data that may have appeared in model training corpora.
  We introduce onepot-Bench 0, a proprietary benchmark suite for evaluating language models on synthetic chemistry capabilities relevant to wet-lab execution. onepot-Bench 0 comprises three complementary evaluations: ChemAbacus measures tool-free cheminformatics literacy and numerical reasoning; SynthRefusal characterizes safety and refusal behavior across a variety of benign, controlled, and designer-drug targets; and SynthBench evaluates reaction-outcome prediction and catalyst selection using private experimental data generated in our laboratory. Together, these evaluations probe basic competency, reliability, and deeper knowledge, all skills which are required for reliable performance in the lab.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [A Portable and Versatile Limited-Memory BFGS Implementation in PETSc/TAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00196)
*Hansol Suh,Tobin Isaac,Alp Dener,Todd Munson,Hong Zhang,Richard Tran Mills*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对L-BFGS Hessian更新方案在准牛顿优化算法中的性能瓶颈，提出了一种介于递归和紧凑稠密表示之间的中间稠密BFGS公式，并在PETSc/TAO中实现，在Polaris和Frontier机器上进行了单节点性能测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L-BFGS实现采用2m个顺序秩1更新，在同步延迟显著时性能受影响，且当向量不适合缓存时，其较差的时间局部性会增加内存流量。紧凑稠密表示虽能减少同步延迟并改善时间局部性，但需要额外的基向量遍历和重新计算基，尤其在B0矩阵变化（如变度量方法）时开销更大。

Method: 在PETSc/TAO中实现了一种中间稠密BFGS公式，该公式保留了递归和紧凑稠密两种方法的大部分优点，同时避免了各自的缺点。在DOE的Polaris和Frontier机器上进行了GPU和CPU的单节点性能测试。

Result: 研究在Polaris和Frontier两大超级计算机上完成了基于GPU和CPU的单节点性能基准测试，评估了不同L-BFGS实现方案在同步延迟和内存流量方面的表现。

Conclusion: 中间稠密BFGS公式在同步延迟、时间局部性和内存流量之间取得了更好的平衡，为高性能计算环境中的准牛顿优化提供了更优的L-BFGS实现选择。

Abstract: The limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) Hessian update scheme is the critical kernel in many quasi-Newton optimization algorithms. The most common approach to implementing L-BFGS uses $2m$ sequential rank-1 updates as part of solving a linear system when there are $m$ history steps. The performance of this approach suffers when the latency of synchronization is significant, and its poor temporal locality increases the memory traffic when vectors do not fit in cache. The compact dense representation of L-BFGS results in an approach that has minimal synchronization latency and better temporal locality, but it requires an additional pass over the basis vectors and an additional basis that must be recomputed when the $B_0$ matrix changes as in variable-metric methods. In the Portable Extensible Toolkit for Scientific Computation and the Toolkit for Advanced Optimization (PETSc/TAO), we have implemented an intermediate dense formulation of BFGS that retains most of the good characteristics of both the recursive and compact dense approaches. We report single-node performance tests of these implementations on the U.S. Department of Energy's Polaris and Frontier machines, testing both GPU-based and CPU-based computations.

</details>


### [162] [Design and Implementation of Schwarz Information Criterion-Aided Intelligent Decentralized Resource Allocation in Dynamic LoRa Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00409)
*Aohan Li,Ryota Ariyoshi,Mikio Hasegawa,Miao Pan,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级分布式学习方法，结合Thompson Sampling（TS）与Schwarz信息准则（SIC）进行LoRa网络传输参数选择，在动态高密度LoRa网络中相比传统UCB1-tuned方案，传输成功率从64.0%提升至71.1%，能效从293.9 bit/J提升至328.3 bit/J。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa端设备（EDs）内存和计算资源有限，而现有基于UCB等方法的传输参数选择在动态变化环境中适应性差。TS方法虽能有效平衡探索与利用，但过度依赖历史学习经验，在通信环境突变时适应缓慢。

Method: 提出TS与SIC相结合的轻量级分布式学习方法：TS用于传输参数选择，平衡探索与利用；SIC用于环境变化检测，计算成本低，适合资源受限的LoRa EDs。当SIC检测到环境变化时，重置TS的学习历史以快速重新学习；方法仅依赖ACK反馈和所选传输参数实现完全分布式操作。

Result: 在高密度动态LoRa网络实验中，所提方法相比传统UCB1-tuned方案，传输成功率从64.0%提高到71.1%，能量效率从293.9 bit/J提高到328.3 bit/J。

Conclusion: 所提出的TS+SIC分布式学习方法能有效提升LoRa网络在动态环境下的传输可靠性和能量效率，且计算开销低，适合资源受限的LoRa端设备部署。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight distributed learning method for selecting transmission parameters in Long-Range (LoRa) networks that adapts to dynamically changing communication environments. In the proposed method, the Thompson Sampling (TS) is adopted for transmission parameter selection, whereas the Schwarz Information Criterion (SIC) is employed for environmental change detection. TS is a reinforcement learning approach that effectively balances exploration and exploitation by updating parameters based on probability distributions. Additionally, it demonstrates stable performance even with a small number of trials, thereby making it well-suited for LoRa end devices (EDs) with limited memory capacity and computational resources. Furthermore, to address the issue that TS-based methods strongly depend on past learning histories and therefore adapt slowly to abrupt changes in communication environments, a statistical change detection mechanism based on the SIC is integrated into our proposed method. SIC is adopted because it can detect environmental changes with low computational cost and is suitable for implementation on resource-constrained LoRa EDs. When a change in the communication environment is detected by SIC, the learning history of TS is reset, thereby enabling rapid re-learning under new environmental conditions. Moreover, to achieve fully distributed communication parameter selection while enhancing transmission reliability and energy efficiency, the proposed method relies solely on Acknowledgment (ACK) feedback and the selected transmission parameters. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves the transmission success rate from 64.0% to 71.1% and increases energy efficiency from 293.9 bit/J to 328.3 bit/J compared with the conventional Upper Confidence Bound (UCB)1-tuned scheme under high-density dynamic LoRa networks.

</details>


### [163] [Cache-Consistent Dynamic Load Balancing for Kubernetes Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00454)
*Shoma Ansai,Yasuo Okabe,Daisuke Kotani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合轻量级负载均衡与缓存同步的Kubernetes控制器水平扩展方法，通过两级Hash将对象映射到虚拟节点，并在标签中记录虚拟节点ID，利用一致性哈希分配虚拟节点到实例，从而避免重新分配时重写所有对象的标签，同时通过缓存锁定和数据存储版本标识符实现缓存一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Kubernetes集群规模增长，控制器单实例运行成为系统性能瓶颈。横向扩展控制器面临两大难题：一是对象重新分配成本高（需要重写所有对象的标签），二是缓存一致性问题（控制器只读取自身缓存，重新分配时需要显式更新缓存且需跨对象类型同步）。

Method: 提出两级Hash负载均衡方案：对象通过两级Hash映射到虚拟节点，虚拟节点ID记录在标签中，虚拟节点通过一致性哈希分配给各控制器实例。实例增减时只需更新查询用的标签值，无需重写对象标签；重新分配期间锁定缓存防止读取过期数据，并通过数据存储的版本标识符同步各对象类型的缓存。

Result: 在Kubernetes上实现并评估了该方法：处理吞吐量随实例数量增加而提升，重新分配所需时间保持在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效实现Kubernetes控制器的水平扩展，在提升吞吐量的同时将重新分配的代价控制在可接受范围内，解决了控制器分布式扩展中的标签重写与缓存一致性问题。

Abstract: As Kubernetes clusters grow, the scalability of controllers can become a bottleneck for the performance of the system. Distributing the load dynamically across multiple controller instances, however, raises the following two problems, and a controller can therefore be run only as a single instance today. The first problem is the cost of reassignment. A controller retrieves objects on the basis of the Labels attached to them, so in a naive design in which the assigned instance is recorded in a Label on every object, the Labels must be rewritten in proportion to the total number of objects whenever instances are added or removed. The second problem is cache consistency. A controller consults only its own cache when it reads an object and never refers to the actual data, so the cache has to be updated explicitly at the time of a reassignment. Furthermore, a controller manages a cache independently for each kind of object, so cache updates have to be synchronized across the kinds of objects. We propose a method for scaling Kubernetes controllers horizontally that combines lightweight load balancing with cache synchronization. A two-level Hash maps objects to Virtual Nodes, records their identifiers in Labels, and assigns Virtual Nodes to instances by Consistent Hashing. Whenever instances are added or removed, it therefore suffices to update the Label value specified when objects are retrieved, and no Label on an object has to be rewritten. The cache is also locked until the reassignment has completed, which prevents any reference to a stale cache. The version identifier of the data store is used to synchronize the kinds of objects with one another. We implemented the proposed method on Kubernetes and evaluated it: the processing throughput rises with the number of instances, and the time required for reassignment remains within an acceptable range.

</details>


### [164] [AReaL-DTE: Sparse Policy-Weight Transfer for Online Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00455)
*Yingqi Peng,Jiawei Zhang,Wenhao Zhou,Ruida Xu,Ran Yan,Wei Dong,Yi Gao,Zhiqiang Ding,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AReaL-DTE，一个无快照的Delta传输引擎，通过将推理可见的权重稀疏性转化为端到端系统效率，大幅加速在线智能体强化学习中的策略权重同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体强化学习中，策略训练与推理由微服务分离部署，频繁的策略权重同步成为关键系统开销。传统的稠密权重同步代价高昂，而基于检查点的稀疏同步方法仍可能保留旧模型并物化完整中间结果，难以高效桥接异构的训练与推理布局。

Method: AReaL-DTE利用连续策略版本间不足2%的BF16权重元素变化这一特性：通过逆向AdamW更新按需重建被覆盖的权重，通过转换器对齐的BF16变化检测流式处理重建参数与当前参数，并将变更元素直接重映射到接收方本地坐标。支持基于manifest提交的跨集群共享存储稀疏传输，以及集群内的无死锁两轮协议，最终将变更直接应用于推理分片。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-30B-A3B上的四个在线RL工作负载中，跨集群场景下比ByteCheckpoint提速最高19.9倍、比PULSE提速最高3.2倍；集群内分别提速最高7.6倍和7.4倍。在同集群Qwen3-30B-A3B实验中，峰值GPU内存降低约41%，峰值CPU内存降低至少87%。

Conclusion: AReaL-DTE通过无快照的Delta传输机制有效消除了在线RL中策略同步带来的系统瓶颈，显著提升了跨集群与集群内的同步效率，并大幅降低了内存开销。

Abstract: Online agentic reinforcement learning implemented with micro-services separates policy training from rollout generation, improving scalability and modularity while potentially making frequent policy-weight synchronization a critical systems overhead. Shared storage naturally connects these services across clusters, but vanilla dense policy weight synchronization could incur model-scale construction, transfer, and application costs. Sparse synchronization reduces transferred data, yet checkpoint-oriented approaches can still retain a previous model and materialize complete intermediates to bridge heterogeneous training and inference layouts. We present AReaL-DTE, a snapshot-free Delta Transfer Engine that translates inference-visible weight sparsity into end-to-end system efficiency. Across our evaluated workloads, fewer than 2% of BF16 weight elements change between consecutive policy versions. AReaL-DTE reconstructs overwritten weights on demand by inverting AdamW updates, streams reconstructed and current parameters through converter-aligned BF16 change detection, and remaps changed elements directly into receiver-local coordinates. AReaL-DTE supports manifest-committed sparse transfer through shared storage across clusters and a deadlock-safe two-round protocol within a cluster, followed by direct application to inference shards. We evaluate AReaL-DTE on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-30B-A3B across four online RL workloads. AReaL-DTE achieves speedups of up to 19.9x over ByteCheckpoint and 3.2x over PULSE across clusters, and up to 7.6x and 7.4x, respectively, within a cluster. In the same-cluster Qwen3-30B-A3B experiments, it reduces peak GPU memory by approximately 41% and peak CPU memory by at least 87%.

</details>


### [165] [Collaborative Orbital Edge Intelligence: A Decentralized Paradigm for Energy-Efficient Computing in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00487)
*Yuvraj Sahni,Jiannong Cao,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为协作轨道边缘智能（COEI）的新型范式，利用低地球轨道（LEO）卫星之间的去中心化协作，实现能效更高的星上数据处理。COEI通过多提供商、多轨道的巨型星座协作，提供全球弹性连接、实时智能和节能服务，并通过去中心化能量感知任务卸载的案例研究验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道边缘计算研究通常假设集中式控制且卫星间缺乏跨提供商协作，导致连接性受限、延迟高和卫星电池消耗增加，未能充分探索异构LEO星座下的去中心化星间协作。

Method: 提出COEI范式，设计包含网络、计算和电源管理三个方面的系统架构，并通过去中心化能量感知卫星任务卸载进行案例研究，目标是最大化任务成功率同时最小化所有卫星最大电池放电深度之和。

Result: COEI能够创建多方、多轨道的巨型卫星星座，提供全球弹性连接、实时智能和能效服务；案例研究验证了去中心化能量感知任务卸载的有效性。

Conclusion: COEI为轨道边缘计算提供了一种新的去中心化协作范式，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: In recent years, Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites have been increasingly deployed to enable connectivity in remote and disaster-prone areas. Researchers have proposed Orbital Edge Computing, which adds computational intelligence to LEO satellites to process data on orbit, providing edge intelligence close to space data sources. Existing work on Orbital Edge Computing typically assumes centralized control without collaboration among satellites from different providers, leading to limited connectivity, higher latency, and increased satellite battery depletion. They have not fully explored decentralized inter-satellite collaboration for energy-efficient intelligence under heterogeneous LEO constellations. This paper introduces a novel paradigm, Collaborative Orbital Edge Intelligence (COEI), that leverages decentralized collaboration among LEO satellites to enable energy-efficient on-orbit processing of space data. We describe the overall system architecture of COEI, including the issues related to networking, computing, and power management. COEI can help create a multi-party, multi-orbit megaconstellation of satellites that delivers better service quality by providing benefits, including global resilient connectivity, real-time intelligence, and energy-efficient services. To demonstrate the COEI benefits, we conduct a case study on decentralized energy-aware satellite task offloading to maximize the task success rate while minimizing the sum of the maximum battery depth-of-discharge across all satellites. Finally, we outline future directions for COEI that offer opportunities for further investigation.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-tenant Kubernetes Use Cases for AI, Secure Computing and Data Services, and More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00742)
*Jake Watson,Sadaf R Alam,Christopher Woods,Abdelwahab Kawafi,Thomas Green,Ian Johnson,Ellis Pires,Jessica R. Jones,Utz-Uwe Haus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了在HPE Cray EX超算平台（Isambard-AI）上部署Kubernetes的可行性与实际效果，聚焦于两个联合设计用例：一个用于医疗健康领域的可信研究环境，另一个结合KubeRay、Ray和vLLM构建面向多租户机密计算的分布式AI模型托管服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单租户批处理系统在超算环境中无法满足AI、安全机密计算和混合工作负载编排等日益多样化的需求，而Kubernetes作为公有云用户熟悉的编排引擎，在裸金属性能优先的超算生态中采用有限，需要探索其作为补充服务的可行性。

Method: 在搭载HPE Slingshot互连的HPE Cray EX超算平台Isambard-AI上部署Kubernetes，并通过两个联合设计用例进行实际评估：一是用于医学和健康科学的可信研究环境（TRE）；二是结合KubeRay、Ray和vLLM的分布式、沙箱化、持久化AI模型托管服务，面向多租户机密计算。

Result: 通过两个实际用例的部署与运行，评估了Kubernetes在超算平台上的表现，发现了挑战并总结了经验教训，明确了在HPE Cray EX（及后续平台）上提供生产级Kubernetes-as-a-Service所需的进一步开发方向。

Conclusion: Kubernetes不应被视为批处理调度系统的替代品，而是作为国家AI研究资源的一部分提供互补服务；在超算平台上实现生产级Kubernetes服务仍需进一步开发以满足特定需求。

Abstract: Kubernetes, as a container orchestration engine, has been widely used in cloud-native ecosystems for several years. In supercomputing ecosystems, especially where bare-metal performance for compute and network devices are considered, the adoption is somewhat limited. However, with the increasing diversity of use cases such as AI, secure and confidential computing for sensitive data, and mixed workload orchestration, a traditional, single-tenant batch computing system does not offer the flexibility and reproducibility to which public cloud users are accustomed. Note that Kubernetes is not considered a replacement for batch scheduling systems, which have powerful features for large-scale MPI jobs with thousands of network end points. Rather, it is a complementary service provided as part of a national AI Research Resource. We evaluate Kubernetes deployment on a Hewlett Packard Enterprise (HPE) Cray EX supercomputerwith HPE Slingshot interconnect, called Isambard-AI, with co-design use cases. One is a Trusted Research Environment used for medical and health sciences. The other combines KubeRay, Ray, and vLLM to provide a distributed, sandboxed, persistent AI model hosting service targeting multi-tenant confidential computing. We discuss challenges and lessons learned, and where further development is needed to offer a production Kubernetes-as-a-Service on HPE Cray EX (and later) platforms.

</details>


### [167] [NUNA: Characterizing and Mitigating Non-Uniform Network Access in Multi-Die GPU Scale-Up Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00867)
*Conor James Green,William Won,Tuan Ta,Bradford M. Beckmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出非均匀网络访问（NUNA）概念，针对多GPU系统中因芯片规模增大导致的内部互连延迟问题，引入NUNA感知路由（NAR）和NUNA感知放置（NAP）两种优化策略，分别优化空间感知的GPU间通信路径和线程块/数据放置，实验表明组合优化可带来最高1.8倍集体通信加速和28%的机器学习推理速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU架构规模不断增大，导致套接字内部线传输延迟显著增加。以往研究主要关注计算和内存的局部性优化，但忽略了GPU间通信的空间影响。作者提出NUNA概念来刻画多GPU系统中这一新兴优化维度，特别是针对机器学习推理中常见的延迟敏感型集体通信。

Method: 提出两个核心优化方法：（1）NUNA感知路由（NAR）：在大规模扩展网络拓扑中选择优化后的空间感知GPU间通信路径；（2）NUNA感知放置（NAP）：将线程块和数据放置在靠近I/O的位置以优化GPU间流量。

Result: NAP单独优化相比不考虑局部性的基线可获得最高1.5倍集体通信加速；NAP与NAR组合可达到1.8倍集体通信加速；在机器学习推理任务中带来平均7%（最高28%）的每token输出时间加速。

Conclusion: 多GPU系统中的空间布局对通信性能有显著影响，NUNA感知的放置和路由优化能够有效提升集体通信效率，进而改善机器学习推理的端到端性能。

Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) architectures are growing in size to meet the increasing compute and memory requirements. As GPU sizes increase, intra-socket wire transfer delay increases significantly. While previous research has optimized for compute and memory locality within a socket, the spatial impact on inter-GPU communication has not been well-studied. We introduce the term non-uniform network access (NUNA) to describe this emerging optimization dimension in multi-GPU systems. We specifically focus on latency-sensitive collective communication, common in machine learning inference. First, we highlight the need for NUNA-aware routing (NAR), choosing optimized, spatially-aware inter-GPU paths in large scale-up network topologies. Second, we introduce NUNA-aware placement (NAP), placing threadblocks and data near I/O to optimize the inter-GPU traffic. We demonstrate that the NAP optimizations alone offer up to 1.5x collective speedups over a locality-unaware baseline. Combining NAP with NAR yields up to 1.8x faster collectives over the locality-unaware baseline. This leads to 7% mean (28% max) time per output token speedup in machine learning inference.

</details>


### [168] [From Cloud to Crowd: Democratizing LLM Service with Decentralized Edge Collaboration for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00922)
*Jiaxing Li,Hengzhi Wang,Feng Wang,Chi Xu,Danyang Song,Ruixiao Zhang,Edith C. H. Ngai,Jiangchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFRAG，一个面向异构边缘设备的去中心化协作RAG系统，通过压缩共享知识图谱实现混合检索以扩大知识覆盖，并引入优化器自适应选择SLM和RAG参数以平衡精度与成本，实验表明可将SLM与云端LLM的精度差距缩小，同时相比中心化服务降低成本高达98.4%并提升峰值吞吐量97.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端LLM部署成本高、存在厂商锁定且负载下性能不稳定，而边缘SLM虽低成本可扩展但知识覆盖有限、与云端LLM存在明显精度差距。如何兼顾成本、可扩展性与精度是核心痛点。

Method: DEFRAG包含两个核心模块：检索方面，压缩并共享知识图谱，采用混合检索策略扩大知识覆盖；生成方面，引入优化器根据每条查询自适应选择SLM和RAG参数，在精度与成本之间动态权衡。系统在异构边缘测试平台上实现并评估。

Result: 在基准QA数据集、移动路由压力、非均匀数据分布及领域特定QA工作负载下，DEFRAG均能保持稳定的服务质量和成本效率。相比中心化服务，成本降低高达98.4%，峰值吞吐量提升97.8%，同时有效缩小了SLM与云端LLM的精度差距。
