Conclusion: DEFRAG展示了去中心化边缘协作在实现低成本、高可扩展、精度接近云端LLM服务的可行性，为LLM服务在边缘端的民主化部署提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has increased demand for scalable and cost-effective deployment, especially for mobile and edge devices. Cloud-hosted LLMs are powerful but expensive and difficult to scale due to vendor lock-in and high resource needs, resulting in high expenses and unstable performance under load. Recent efforts focus on deploying small language models (SLMs), distilled or pruned from LLMs, on resource-constrained edge devices to reduce costs and improve scalability. However, edge-based SLMs face limited knowledge coverage and notable accuracy gap compared to cloud-based LLMs. To address this, we present DEFRAG, a decentralized edge collaboration system for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that optimizes both retrieval and generation across heterogeneous edge devices. For retrieval, DEFRAG compresses and shares knowledge graphs, using hybrid retrieval to expand knowledge coverage. For generation, DEFRAG introduces an optimizer that adaptively selects SLMs and RAG parameters per query, balancing accuracy and cost. We implement DEFRAG on a heterogeneous edge testbed and evaluate it on benchmark QA datasets. We also test it under mobile route stress, non-uniform data placement, and a domain-specific QA workload. The results show that DEFRAG maintains stable service quality and cost efficiency under these broader settings. Results show that DEFRAG narrows the SLM-LLM accuracy gap, while reducing cost by up to 98.4% and increasing peak throughput by up to 97.8% over centralized services. These findings demonstrate the potential of DEFRAG for democratized LLM services at the edge.

</details>


### [169] [TIDE-MC: Two-Sided Interpolative Decomposition for Billion-Scale GPU Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00977)
*Chengying Huan,Yubo Wang,Pinhuan Wang,Lizheng Chen,Jie Zhang,Fangxin Liu,Qing Wang,Ruixuan Liu,Shaonan Ma,Mingxing Zhang,Zhibin Wang,Rong Gu,Guihai Chen,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TIDE-MC是一个基于双面插值分解（TSID）的有界内存GPU矩阵补全框架，通过采样模板子矩阵作为锚点重构完整低秩矩阵，解决现有GPU求解器在矩阵超出设备内存时的内存溢出或PCIe开销问题，在15个基准测试中实现高达11,647倍加速、8.5倍内存缩减和99.7%重构误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU矩阵补全求解器普遍假设观测矩阵或其稠密因子可完全装入设备内存，但在实际大规模工作负载中这一假设不成立，导致内存溢出或朴素的页交换带来严重的PCIe开销。

Method: TIDE-MC基于双面插值分解（TSID），利用采样模板子矩阵作为锚点重构完整低秩矩阵，使计算和存储随模板与活动数据块规模增长而非完整矩阵规模。框架包含两个执行阶段：第一，通过无冲突同步引擎，使用并行分解和分层梯度聚合恢复模板；第二，通过分块重构流水线扩展模板到其余矩阵，同时将PCIe传输与GPU计算重叠。此外采用非对称梯度裁剪方案稳定混合精度Tensor Core执行。

Result: 在15个基准测试中，TIDE-MC能够完成导致现有GPU求解器内存溢出的工作负载。与最先进基线相比，最高实现11,647倍加速、峰值内存使用降低8.5倍、重构误差降低99.7%。

Conclusion: 模板锚定分解与阶段特定的GPU执行策略能够将矩阵补全扩展到超出设备内存容量的规模，实证结果验证了该方法的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Matrix completion supports large-scale recommendation and scientific computing, yet existing GPU solvers commonly assume that the observed matrix or its dense factors fit in device memory. On real workloads, this assumption leads to out-of-memory failures or severe PCIe overhead under naive paging.
  We present TIDE-MC, a bounded-memory GPU framework built on Two-Sided Interpolative Decomposition (TSID). TSID uses a sampled template submatrix as an anchor for reconstructing the full low-rank matrix, allowing computation and storage to scale with the template and active data chunks rather than the complete matrix. TIDE-MC realizes this formulation through two execution stages. First, a conflict-free synchronization engine recovers the template using parallel factorization and hierarchical gradient aggregation. Second, a chunked reconstruction pipeline extends the recovered template to the remaining matrix while overlapping PCIe transfers with GPU computation. An asymmetric gradient-clipping scheme stabilizes mixed-precision Tensor Core execution.
  Across 15 benchmarks, TIDE-MC completes workloads that cause existing GPU solvers to run out of memory. Compared with the evaluated state-of-the-art baselines, it achieves up to 11,647x speedup, reduces peak memory usage by up to 8.5x, and lowers reconstruction error by up to 99.7%. These results show that template-anchored decomposition and stage-specific GPU execution can scale matrix completion beyond device-memory capacity.

</details>


### [170] [Zellige: Moldable Sequence Placement for Mixed Image-Video DiT Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01150)
*Guangyu Xiang,Xueze Kang,Minwei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Shaohuai Shi,Lin Zhang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Zellige是一个可塑序列放置系统，用于解决混合长度图像/视频数据在DiT训练中的并行问题，通过硬件分析器、两阶段规划器和聚合注意力引擎，在多个GPU配置下相比现有系统实现了1.12-1.54倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量视频生成需要联合训练图像和视频数据，这带来了跨GPU的混合长度序列训练问题。现有系统依赖数据并行(DP)、上下文并行(CP)或其组合，但这些非重叠组放置设计面临组间负载不均衡和组内通信冗余之间的根本性权衡。

Method: Zellige由三个组件构成：1) 硬件分析器，估算候选放置方案的执行时间和内存消耗；2) 两阶段规划器，首先平衡计算密集的锚定序列，然后将较轻的填充序列打包到剩余容量中；3) 聚合注意力引擎，高效执行完整序列与分布式注意力分片。

Result: 在21个方案中，硬件分析器预测步长时间和峰值分配内存的平均绝对百分比误差分别为3.4%和1.5%。两阶段规划器每个批次仅需33-119ms求解，远快于联合放置参考方法，且建模的makespan差异最多0.32%。端到端评估中，Zellige在16块A800 GPU上比KnapFormer快1.12-1.48倍，在32块A6000 GPU上快1.27-1.54倍。

Conclusion: Zellige通过同时选择每个序列的并行配置和参与秩次，有效解决了混合长度序列训练中的负载均衡与通信效率问题，在多个硬件平台上均显著优于现有系统。

Abstract: High-quality video generation requires training Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) jointly on image and video data, posing a mixed-length sequence training problem across GPUs. Existing systems rely on data parallelism (DP), context parallelism (CP), or their combination; we model these designs as disjoint-group placement and prove that they face a fundamental tradeoff between inter-group load imbalance and intra-group communication redundancy. We present Zellige, a moldable sequence placement system that jointly selects each sequence's parallelism configuration and participating ranks. Zellige consists of three components: a hardware profiler that estimates the execution time and memory consumption of candidate placements, a two-stage planner that balances compute-heavy anchor sequences and packs lighter filler sequences into the remaining capacity, and a coalesced attention engine that efficiently executes whole sequences alongside distributed-attention shards. Across 21 plans, the hardware profile predicts step makespan and peak allocated memory with mean absolute percentage errors of $3.4%$ and $1.5%$, respectively. The two-stage planner solves each batch in 33--119 ms, significantly faster than a joint-placement reference that optimizes all sequences together, while their modeled makespans differ by at most $0.32%$. In end-to-end evaluations, Zellige outperforms KnapFormer by $1.12$--$1.48\times$ on 16 A800 GPUs and $1.27$--$1.54\times$ on 32 A6000 GPUs.

</details>


### [171] [NIXT: A NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool for Observability of Collective Communication in Large Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01449)
*Ziyang Jia,Sirshak Das,Jason Sewall,Laxmi Bhuyan,Pasha Shamis,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NIXT（NCCL Inspector导出工具），旨在提升大规模机器学习训练中集体通信的可观测性。该工具将NCCL Inspector收集的大量性能统计数据进行转换和呈现，提供易于访问的分析和可操作的见解，并通过Nemotron-4 LLM在2048个H100 GPU上的预训练案例研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习工作负载的规模扩大，对集体通信性能的可观测性需求日益迫切，以快速识别性能变化和加速根因定位。虽然NCCL Inspector提供了轻量级和持续的通信性能统计报告，但其产生的大量数据难以评估和提取有价值的见解。

Method: 提出了NIXT（NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool），它作为NCCL Inspector的分析导出工具，将原始性能统计数据处理为可读性强的分析结果，并生成可操作的见解，提升集体通信的可观测性。

Result: 通过在Nvidia H100 GPU集群上对Nemotron-4 LLM预训练（最高2048个GPU）的案例研究，演示了通信阶段随ML并行策略和GPU规模变化的情况，成功实现了性能变化归因和落后者（stragglers）的根因分析。

Conclusion: NIXT工具显著提升了大规模分布式训练中集体通信的可观测性，能够帮助用户从大量NCCL Inspector数据中快速提取有价值的分析结果，加速性能问题的识别和定位。

Abstract: As machine learning workloads scale, it is increasingly important to gain more observability into the performance of collective communication to easily identify performance vari- ations and accelerate root cause identification. Towards this goal, the Nvidia Collective Communication Library (NCCL) introduced NCCL Inspector, a profiler plugin that provides lightweight and continuous reporting of NCCL communication performance statistics. However, the large volume of data collected by NCCL Inspector can be difficult to assess and to extract actionable insights from. This paper presents NIXT, a NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool that improves the observability of collective communication by providing readily accessible analysis and actionable insights from NCCL Inspector profiling. To highlight the benefits of our Exporter Tool, we present a case study of Nemotron-4 LLM pretraining on an Nvidia H100 GPU cluster with up to 2,048 GPUs, demonstrate observability into how communication phases change with ML parallelism and GPU scale, and perform attribution of performance variation and root cause analysis of stragglers.

</details>


### [172] [GPU-Accelerated Multilevel Graph Clustering: A Parallel Perspective on Louvain and Leiden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01503)
*Michael S. Gilbert,Kamesh Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了pLouvain和pLeiden两种GPU并行化算法用于大规模图的模块度优化聚类。pLouvain基于Louvain+扩展，pLeiden是首个能证明保留顺序Leiden算法所有质量保证的并行实现（通过新颖的生成树细化方法）。两者在使用轻量级对称破缺技术模拟有序顶点遍历的同时，优化了LambdaCC目标函数。在57个图上的实验表明，相比当前最快的开源并行实现，pLouvain和pLeiden分别获得3.1倍和8.8倍的几何平均加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序Louvain和Leiden算法是大规模图聚类（社区检测）中广泛使用的模块度优化方法，但现有并行化实现存在速度慢或无法保留顺序算法质量保证的问题。作者旨在提供两种GPU并行化方案，在不牺牲聚类质量的前提下显著加速计算。

Method: 提出两种GPU并行化方法：(1) pLouvain基于Louvain+扩展，采用轻量级对称破缺技术模拟顶点有序遍历，并开发了替代迭代策略以修复Louvain/Louvain+中内部簇连通性弱的问题；(2) pLeiden采用新颖的基于生成树的细化方法，是首个能够可证明保留顺序Leiden算法所有质量保证的并行实现。两者均优化LambdaCC目标函数（模块度和Constant Potts模型的泛化）。

Result: 在来自10个图家族的57个图上进行测试，pLouvain和pLeiden分别实现了3.1倍和8.8倍的几何平均加速比（相较于当前最快的开源Louvain和Leiden并行化实现）。在几乎所有测试图上，pLouvain生成的聚类具有最高的模块度分数。

Conclusion: pLouvain和pLeiden在GPU上实现了显著的加速效果，同时保持了聚类质量。pLeiden首次在并行环境下保留了顺序Leiden的全部质量保证，而pLouvain在模块度分数上表现最优。两种多级方法中的子程序可为其他基于Louvain的技术并行化提供参考。

Abstract: The sequential Louvain and Leiden algorithms are widely used techniques for modularity-optimizing clustering (or community detection) in large graphs. We present pLouvain and pLeiden, two new GPU parallelizations. pLouvain is based on the Louvain+ extension. pLeiden is the first parallel implementation to provably preserve all quality guarantees of sequential Leiden. We achieve this through a novel spanning-tree-based refinement approach. Both pLouvain and pLeiden use a lightweight symmetry-breaking technique that emulates an ordered traversal of vertices. For pLouvain, we develop an alternative iteration strategy to rectify the weak internal cluster connectivity observed in Louvain/Louvain+. Further, both pLouvain and pLeiden optimize the LambdaCC objective function, a generalization of modularity and the related Constant Potts model.
  On a collection of 57 graphs from 10 families, our results show that pLouvain and pLeiden achieve geometric mean speedups of 3.1x and 8.8x, respectively, over the current fastest open-source parallelizations of Louvain and Leiden. For the clusterings generated, pLouvain yields the highest modularity scores on nearly all tested graphs. The subroutines within these two multilevel approaches could aid in the parallelization of other Louvain-based techniques.

</details>


### [173] [Source-Bounded Exact Recovery over Docker's Logs API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01564)
*Kelvin Amoaba*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Docker日志收集中的精确恢复（exact recovery）契约。作者提出了"源有界精确性"（source-bounded exactness）概念，即每个保留且可区分的源记录最终恰好出现一次在持久化的收集器输出中。通过代际感知的多重集预言机（generation-aware multiset oracle），发现LogDeck存在启动到附加竞态和单记录附加重叠问题，修复后在60/60次运行中实现精确性，而使用相同API的Grafana Alloy仅在20/60次中成功。Alloy在源退出或重启时无法恢复保留的历史记录，而注入容器ID后20/20次全部恢复。结果表明生命周期重新获取（lifecycle reacquisition）而非持久化位置决定精确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Docker日志收集器在附加前或停机期间可能遗漏记录，仅持久化读取位置并不能保证生命周期变化后的恢复。作者希望明确通过Docker官方Logs API能实现的确切恢复契约边界。

Method: 定义了源有界精确性契约；使用代际感知的多重集预言机区分源截断与收集器遗漏，暴露同时丢失和重放问题；通过精确插入修复启动竞态和附加重叠；在多种环境（OrbStack、Ubuntu主机、Docker 29.4.0和24.0.9）中进行对照实验，与Grafana Alloy 1.18.0比较。

Result: 修复后的LogDeck在60/60次运行中精确，Alloy仅20/60次成功。Alloy在守护进程暂停时可恢复，但在源退出或重启后无法恢复保留历史。在因果控制实验中，5,000条记录源退出后，使用容器ID的读取器20/20次完全恢复，而stock discovery 0/20次获取任何数据。200,000条字节相同记录未产生冲突。

Conclusion: 在保留源的时间范围内，决定精确恢复的关键是生命周期重新获取（发现退出或重启的源并重新连接），而非仅持久化读取位置。这是有界接口声明，不构成通用收集器排名或冲突自由的证明。

Abstract: Docker can retain records that a collector misses before attachment or during downtime. A persisted read position does not by itself ensure recovery after lifecycle changes. We study what exact recovery contract is achievable through Docker's supported Logs API. We define source-bounded exactness: every retained, distinguishable source record eventually appears exactly once in durable collector output. Our method uses a generation-aware multiset oracle that separates source truncation from collector omission and exposes simultaneous loss and replay. Applied to LogDeck, it uncovered a start-to-attachment race; a one-record attachment overlap, finite Docker-API reconciliation, and exact insertion closed the tested boundary. We compare the fixed revision with unmodified Grafana Alloy 1.18.0, which uses the same API and persists read positions; across 120 collector-runs, LogDeck was exact in 60/60 and Alloy in 20/60. Alloy succeeded at guarded startup and process pause but omitted retained history when recovery required discovering an exited or restarted source. In a causal control, a 5,000-record source exited before collection: stock discovery was exact in 0/20 trials and acquired nothing, while the same reader given the container ID recovered all records exactly in 20/20. This reproduced on OrbStack and independent Ubuntu hosts with Docker 29.4.0 and 24.0.9; both collectors recovered daemon restart, while neither recovered records after source removal. Exactness assumes distinct tuples of physical generation, timestamp, stream, and bytes; 200,000 byte-identical records across two drivers produced no observed collisions. Our results show that lifecycle reacquisition, not a persisted position alone, determines exact recovery within the retained-source horizon. This is a bounded interface claim, not a universal collector ranking or proof of collision freedom.

</details>


### [174] [Bole: Efficient Tree Speculation for Hybrid-Attention Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01651)
*Li Wang,Yi Su,Xiabao Wu,Chiran You,Yongchao Liu,Zhan Qiu,Juelu Zhang,Jiajun Zheng,Fangxin Liu,Jie Zhang,Chen Tian,Chengying Huan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Bole是一个针对混合注意力大语言模型的kernel-runtime协同设计系统，通过将线性注意力递归转化为树形闭式解、无损编码推测状态更新，以及批量级验证预算调度，显著提升树推测解码的效率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力LLM结合了全注意力和循环线性注意力以降低长上下文推理成本，但其自回归解码仍受内存带宽限制。现有树推测解码系统是为全注意力模型的KV缓存设计的，在混合模型上逐分支遍历循环层并为每个推测节点物化完整状态，导致验证延迟和瞬时内存随树和批大小急剧扩展。

Method: Bole采用三层设计：(1) 将线性注意力递归转化为树形闭式解，用资源高效的GPU kernel并行验证所有推测节点；(2) 将推测状态更新无损编码为token级因子，仅重建采样后选中的状态，将瞬时状态内存减少82-99倍；(3) 集成到SGLang生产推理引擎，将高效状态管理与覆盖完整混合前向传播的批量级验证预算相结合。

Result: 线性注意力树验证加速3.4-7.7倍；瞬时状态内存减少82-99倍；在四个模型、两个GPU平台和多样数据集上，离线解码吞吐量相比自回归解码提升最高4.72倍，相比最强树推测基线提升最高2.03倍；在线agent工作负载下，TTFT和TPOT相比最强树推测基线分别降低最高67.6%和49.9%。

Conclusion: Bole通过kernel-runtime协同设计成功解决了混合注意力LLM中树推测解码的效率瓶颈，证明了将推测状态管理从全注意力范式适配到混合注意力架构可以带来显著的性能提升，为生产级混合LLM推理加速提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Hybrid-attention large language models combine full attention with recurrent linear attention to reduce long-context inference costs, yet their autoregressive decoding remains memory-bound. Tree speculative decoding offers an attractive acceleration path, but existing tree-speculation systems are designed around the key--value caches of full-attention models. On hybrid models, they traverse recurrent layers branch by branch and materialize a full state for every proposal node, causing verification latency and transient memory to scale poorly with tree and batch sizes. We present Bole, a kernel--runtime co-design that enables efficient tree speculation for hybrid-attention LLMs. Bole transforms the linear-attention recurrence into a tree-structured closed form and realizes it with a resource-efficient GPU kernel, verifying all proposal nodes in parallel and accelerating linear-attention tree verification by 3.4--7.7$\times$. It losslessly encodes speculative state updates as token-level factors and reconstructs only the state selected after sampling, reducing transient state memory by 82--99$\times$ and freeing GPU capacity for KV caches. Its integration into SGLang, a widely deployed production LLM serving engine, couples efficient state management with a batch-wide verification budget calibrated to the complete hybrid forward. Across four models, two GPU platforms, and diverse datasets, Bole delivers up to $4.72\times$ the offline decode throughput of autoregressive decoding and up to $2.03\times$ that of the strongest tree-speculative baseline. Under online agent workloads, it reduces TTFT and TPOT by up to $67.6%$ and $49.9%$, respectively, over the strongest tree-speculative baseline.

</details>


### [175] [PrefixPlace: Provable Prefix Key-Value Placement for Large Language Model Serving under Heterogeneous Compute and Transfer Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01655)
*Zhiyu Wang,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PrefixPlace是一个epoch级别的规划器，用于优化LLM推理中的前缀KV缓存放置，超越了基于命中率的传统方法，在多个硬件和基准测试上接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的前缀KV缓存复用策略仅基于命中率进行放置决策，但本地缓存未命中时重计算与副本获取的相对成本会随硬件、前缀深度、KV吞吐率及副本位置的不同而变化，导致基于命中率的放置方案并非最优。

Method: 提出PrefixPlace的epoch级规划器，在内存预算下分配前缀完整目标，目标是分解为本地副本价值加上首个副本覆盖率，将源依赖成本转化为单调设施选址问题。每个worker更新被转化为加性有根树问题，在O(nk)时间内精确求解（n为块数，k为容量），通过坐标细化和顺序多样化的起点获得固定顺序的1/2近似。

Result: 在T4、L4和A100上测量显示不同机制；在432个具有精确最优解的实例中，PrefixPlace平均达到最优解的99.84%，最低不低于98.02%。在RAG回放中，相比vLLM-APC提升物化成本节省40.3%，相比最佳离线基线提升6.3%；在WikiQA上提升分别为40.4%和5.3%。另外，在单处理器上12.3秒内解决50,000节点、16 worker的放置问题。

Conclusion: PrefixPlace通过考虑计算和传输成本而不仅是命中率，显著提升了前缀KV缓存的放置效率，且具备较高的可扩展性，适合实际生产环境中的及时重新规划。

Abstract: Prefix Key-Value (KV) reuse avoids repeated prefill in Large Language Model (LLM) inference, but local misses require recomputation or replica fetches. Their relative cost varies with hardware, prefix depth, KV goodput, and replica location, making hit-rate-based placement suboptimal. To address this issue, we propose an epoch-level planner, PrefixPlace, which assigns prefix-complete targets under memory budgets and profiled demand, compute, and transfer costs. The objective decomposes into local-copy value plus first-replica coverage, and source-dependent costs yield a monotone facility-location objective; each worker update is an additive rooted-tree problem solved exactly in O(nk) time for n chunks and capacity k, giving a fixed-order 1/2-approximation that coordinate refinement and order-diverse starts improve without weakening. T4, L4, and A100 measurements reveal distinct regimes. Across 432 instances with exact optima, PrefixPlace averages 99.84% of optimum and never falls below 98.02%. In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) replays, it improves materialization-cost saving by 40.3% over vLLM Automatic Prefix Caching (vLLM-APC) and 6.3% over the best offline baseline. On WikiQA, gains are 40.4% and 5.3%. Finally, PrefixPlace solves a 50,000-node, 16-worker placement in 12.3 s on one processor, enabling timely replanning.

</details>


### [176] [Preserving Admission Responsibility in Multi-Tenant Large Language Model Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01657)
*Zhiyu Wang,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PrefixShield通过计量新物化的KV块、跨请求携带责任、在债务未清时限制复用提升，并利用预估债务选择驱逐候选组，弥合了共享前缀缓存中的"准入-责任鸿沟"，在vLLM中实现显著优于LRU和S3-FIFO的缓存命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU共享前缀缓存存在一个根本性错配：一组租户物化新KV块可能导致另一组丢失可复用状态，但请求时调度只考虑瞬时服务，替换策略主要按对象价值排序，静态分区又会浪费空闲容量。论文将这种不匹配称为"准入-责任鸿沟"。

Method: 提出PrefixShield机制，核心包括：1) 计量新物化的完整KV块；2) 跨请求携带责任（responsibility）；3) 在债务未清时限制复用提升（gating reuse promotion）；4) 使用预估债务选择提供驱逐候选的租户组。系统在vLLM中实现。

Result: 在单次污染配对运行中，PrefixShield相比LRU提升受害缓存命中率9.39个百分点，相比S3-FIFO提升8.64个百分点；在4096块规模下将受害者从4.92%恢复至84.87%。两遍回放下比S3-FIFO提升2.00个百分点。保留良性ShareGPT行为并保持工作保守地访问空闲容量；延迟回放下债务未清时获得35.16个百分点的优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明对象价值信号决定保留什么内容，而持久责任决定哪个组承受回收压力。这一双重信号机制是解决LLM多租户共享前缀缓存中公平性与效率矛盾的关键。

Abstract: Shared prefix caching turns Graphics Processing Unit (GPU) memory into persistent state shared across Large Language Model (LLM) tenants. A group that materializes new Key-Value (KV) blocks can force another to lose reusable state, yet request-time schedulers account for transient service, replacement policies primarily rank object value, and static partitioning strands idle capacity. We call this mismatch the admission-responsibility gap. To close it, we propose PrefixShield, which meters newly materialized full KV blocks, carries responsibility across requests, gates reuse promotion while debt remains, and uses projected debt to select the group supplying eviction candidates. We implement PrefixShield in vLLM. In paired runs under one-touch pollution, PrefixShield improves victim cache hit ratio by 9.39 percentage points over the Least Recently Used (LRU) policy and 8.64 points over S3-FIFO, restoring the victim from 4.92% to 84.87% at 4096-block scale, and gains 2.00 points over S3-FIFO under two-pass replay. It preserves benign ShareGPT behavior and work-conserving access to idle capacity. Delayed replay yields a 35.16-point advantage while debt remains. These results show that object-value signals rank what to retain, while persistent responsibility determines which group bears reclamation pressure.

</details>


### [177] [HorizonServe: Coordinating Request Scheduling with GPU Sharing for Omni-Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01785)
*Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HorizonServe是一个面向单GPU的全模态（omni-model）服务系统，通过协调请求准入和GPU分配来解决异构SLO下的调度问题，在三种全模态工作负载和两种GPU平台上，将SLO达成率提升最高4.9倍（到达率扫描）和7.0倍（下游密集型流量），并将每类首响应延迟降低38.4%-63.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态模型统一文本、语音、图像和多模态推理于单一服务后端，但统一部署引入了新的调度问题：不同输出模态的请求可能共享初始多模态主干网络，然后在下游生成阶段分叉，导致同一GPU上出现异构的首响应指标和SLO目标。现有LLM和多模态服务系统主要优化token进度或输入侧处理，无法联合控制共享阶段的时间共享和并行运行阶段间的空间共享。

Method: HorizonServe采用四方面策略：(1) 按类别建模首响应延迟；(2) 保护松弛度有限的请求；(3) 在执行路径间轮转共享阶段的机会；(4) 当下游阶段活跃时，限制共享阶段的流式多处理器（SM）分配。

Result: 在三种全模态工作负载和两种GPU平台上，HorizonServe将SLO达成率提升最高4.9倍（到达率扫描场景）和7.0倍（下游密集型流量场景），并将每类首响应延迟降低38.4%-63.7%。

Conclusion: HorizonServe通过同时协调请求准入和GPU资源分配，有效解决了全模态统一服务系统中的异构SLO调度难题，证明了联合控制时间共享与空间共享对于全模态服务性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Omni models unify text, speech, image, and multimodal reasoning in a single serving backend, but this unified deployment exposes a new scheduling problem. Requests with different output modalities may share an initial multimodal backbone and then diverge into downstream generation stages, creating heterogeneous first-response metrics and service-level objective (SLO) targets on the same GPU. Existing large language model (LLM) and multimodal serving systems mainly optimize token progress or input-side processing, and they do not jointly control temporal sharing in the shared stage and spatial sharing among co-running stages. This paper presents HorizonServe, a single-GPU omni-model serving system that coordinates request admission and GPU allocation under heterogeneous SLOs. HorizonServe profiles per-class first-response latency, protects requests with limited slack, rotates shared-stage opportunities across execution paths, and throttles the shared-stage streaming multiprocessor (SM) allocation when downstream stages are active. Across three omni-model workloads and two GPU platforms, HorizonServe improves SLO attainment by up to 4.9$\times$ in arrival-rate sweeps and 7.0$\times$ under downstream-heavy traffic, and reduces per-class first-response latency by 38.4--63.7\%.

</details>


### [178] [FedJigsaw: Multi-Agent Collaborative Model Reassembly for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01861)
*Jifeng Chen,Haibo Zhang,Yawen Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FedJigsaw提出了一种动态去中心化的模型组装框架来解决模型异构联邦学习（MHFL）中的个性化问题，通过从邻居客户端学习可复用模块来构建定制模型，替代传统基于预定义架构模板或超网络的部分训练方法，在精度、性能方差、决策延迟和内存占用方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的部分训练（PT）方法通常依赖预定义的架构模板或过参数化的超网络，这限制了细粒度的个性化能力，同时引入了大量的计算和内存开销。作者希望突破这一局限，实现更灵活、高效的模型个性化。

Method: FedJigsaw包含三个核心组件：1) AttenAssemble——客户端级模块，利用注意力机制从邻居处自适应组装定制模型；2) SymbioArchitect——支持客户端与拓扑邻居交换细粒度模型模块的机制，在通信和隐私约束下实现高效知识共享；3) CoRe-Tune——注意力增强的集中式训练与去中心化执行策略，引导本地策略促进隐式协作并稳定训练动态，同时不损害数据隐私。

Result: 在多项评估中，FedJigsaw相比最先进的MHFL基线方法在相对准确率上最高提升13.8%，显著缩小了跨客户端的性能方差，同时相比现有策略驱动方法大幅降低了决策延迟和峰值内存占用。

Conclusion: FedJigsaw验证了将模型个性化重塑为动态去中心化模型组装问题的有效性，为MHFL提供了一种更灵活、高效且可扩展的解决方案，在精度、资源效率和稳定性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Model Heterogeneous Federated Learning (MHFL) addresses client-level resource heterogeneity by allowing each participant to train a personalized model architecture under a shared training objective. A prevalent paradigm, Partial Training (PT), achieves this by allowing each client to train a subnetwork of the global model. However, existing PT methods typically rely on predefined architectural templates or over-parameterized supernets, limiting fine-grained personalization and imposing substantial computational and memory overhead. We propose FedJigsaw, a novel framework that reshapes model personalization as a dynamic and decentralized model assembly problem. Instead of selecting subnetworks from a predefined supernetwork, each client constructs its model by assembling reusable modules learned from neighboring clients. At the client level, we introduce AttenAssemble to enable each participant to adaptively construct a tailored model based on local observations. To support efficient knowledge sharing under communication and privacy constraints, we design SymbioArchitect, a mechanism that allows clients to exchange granular model modules with their topological neighbors. To mitigate training instability introduced by decentralized module exchange, we design CoRe-Tune, an attention-enhanced centralized training with a decentralized execution strategy, which guides local policies to foster implicit collaboration and stabilize training dynamics, without compromising data privacy. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FedJigsaw outperforms state-of-the-art MHFL baselines by up to 13.8% in relative accuracy while significantly shrinking cross-client performance variance, but also slashes decision-making latency and peak memory footprint compared to existing policy-driven methods.

</details>


### [179] [Energy-Efficient LLM Serving via Disaggregated Attention--FFN and Flexible Frequency Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01891)
*Cunchen Hu,Liangliang Xu,Tian Liu,Min Lyu,Yongkun Li,Sa Wang,Shuo Quan,Yanan Yang,Wenda Tang,Yiduo Wang,Fu Yu,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AFlex框架，针对大规模语言模型（LLM）服务中Attention和FFN算子的能耗最优频率差异，联合优化资源分配与GPU频率调节，在满足SLO的前提下显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务场景多样且SLO要求严格，GPU通常以最高频率运行导致能耗过高。现有能耗管理方法仅在请求或推理阶段层面调整GPU频率，忽略了Attention与FFN（A/F）在算子层面频率敏感性的差异，无法实现最优能耗。

Method: 提出AFlex框架，包括：（1）全局调度器与局部算子级DVFS控制器，联合确定A/F资源分配与频率；（2）交错式A/F流水线，配合动态微批次深度与自适应请求批处理，以减少流水线气泡；（3）在SGLang中实现，基于NVIDIA A800 GPU进行评估。

Result: 使用Qwen3-32B和Mixtral-8×7B模型在生产规模Conversation和Coding轨迹上评估，AFlex相比最先进的分离式服务能耗降低最高49%，相比频率调节系统能耗降低48%，同时满足TTFT和TPOT的SLO要求。

Conclusion: 通过识别A/F算子在频率敏感性上的差异并构建联合优化的资源调度与DVFS框架，AFlex能够在不牺牲服务质量的前提下大幅降低LLM服务的单位token能耗，为可持续的LLM推理系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving spans diverse applications with stringent service-level objectives (SLOs), often requiring GPUs to run at maximum frequencies and increasing energy consumption. Existing energy-management approaches adapt GPU frequencies only at the request or inference-phase level, overlooking operator-level differences in frequency sensitivity between Attention and feed-forward networks (FFNs). We find that the energy-optimal frequencies of Attention and FFN (A/F) differ and vary with the inference phase, workload, and system configurations. However, runtime variability and independent A/F frequency control create a large search space and high communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present AFlex, a framework that jointly optimizes resource provisioning and GPU frequency scaling for disaggregated A/F serving. AFlex introduces a global scheduler and a local operator-level dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) controller to determine A/F resource allocations and frequencies. It further introduces an interleaved A/F pipeline with dynamic microbatch depth and adaptive request batching to reduce pipeline bubbles. We implement AFlex in SGLang and evaluate it on NVIDIA A800 GPUs using Qwen3-32B and Mixtral-8$\times$7B under production Conversation and Coding traces. \AFlex reduces energy per token by up to 49\% over state-of-the-art disaggregated serving and 48\% over frequency-scaling systems while satisfying TTFT and TPOT SLOs.

</details>


### [180] [Diagnosing High-Performance BFT Consensus via Mixture Modeling of Block Time Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01934)
*Hongru He,Akihiro Fujihara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过对HotStuff-based高性能BFT共识的区块时间分布进行建模诊断，提出基于法定人数组播框架的混合模型，并在Hyperliquid和Aptos主网上验证，发现Hyperliquid呈单峰分布而Aptos呈多峰结构，表明该模型可作为实用的共识诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能BFT区块链虽追求高吞吐和低延迟，但其区块时间分布常表现出由网络、流水线和部署异质性导致的复杂行为，缺乏系统性的诊断和建模方法。

Method: 基于法定人数（quorum）组播框架建模区块时间，将其关联到法定人数形成延迟；使用混合模型（mixture models）捕获多模态区块时间分布，每个分量代表一种以有效传输速率为特征的不同网络条件；主体数据拟合与尾部衰减分别分析，并应用于Hyperliquid和Aptos主网数据。

Result: Hyperliquid的区块时间由单峰分布良好解释，对应相对同质的验证者部署；Aptos表现出持续的多模态结构，并在共识升级后出现显著偏移，反映异质部署和多样化通信路径。

Conclusion: 混合建模区块时间分布是一种实用且信息丰富的诊断工具，可用于分析和监控高性能BFT共识系统的运行状态。

Abstract: High-performance Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) blockchains are designed to achieve high throughput and low latency, yet their observed block time distributions often reveal complex behaviors arising from networking, pipelining, and deployment heterogeneity. In this paper, we diagnose HotStuff-based high-performance BFT consensus by modeling block times through a quorum-based multicast framework that links each block interval to quorum formation latency. We capture multimodal block time distributions using mixture models, where each component represents a distinct network condition characterized by effective transfer rate of block information. The proposed model is fitted to the bulk of mainnet block time data, while tail decay is analyzed separately to assess asymptotic behavior. Applying this methodology to Hyperliquid and Aptos mainnets, we find that Hyperliquid is well explained by a unimodal distribution, consistent with a relatively homogeneous validator deployment. In contrast, Aptos exhibits persistent multimodal structure and a pronounced shift following a consensus upgrade, reflecting heterogeneous deployments and diverse communication paths. These results demonstrate that mixture modeling of block time provides a practical and informative diagnostic tool for analyzing and monitoring high-performance BFT consensus.

</details>


### [181] [TALSC: Timeliness-Aware Large-Small VLM Collaboration for Infrastructure-Assisted Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01998)
*Mengmeng Zhu,Yuxuan Sun,Wei Chen,Bo Ai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TALSC框架，用于自动驾驶系统中大小视觉语言模型（SVLM与LVLM）之间的协作调度，通过建模信息年龄（AoI）设计在线调度算法，在动态车联网环境中平衡LVLM的精度增益与延迟导致的信息时效性损失，实验表明在Micro-F1指标上相比最佳基线提升高达12.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载算力有限只能部署小型VLM，虽可通过基础设施协作使用边缘服务器上的大型VLM，但动态车联网环境中感知数据的效用随信息老化迅速衰减，造成精度与时效性之间的矛盾，需要一种感知时效性的协作机制。

Method: 提出TALSC框架：1）对VLM推理过程建模AoI演化，刻画AoI、token长度与任务性能之间的耦合关系，构建通用时效性度量；2）设计TALSC在线调度算法，采用Lyapunov漂移加估计惩罚方法处理调度决策对未来时效性的延迟影响及输出token数不可预知的问题，并提供性能保证。

Result: 基于nuScenes数据集进行案例研究拟合时效性度量，并在多种通信与计算配置下验证TALSC优于各基线，相比最优基线在Micro-F1分数上实现高达12.6%的归一化提升。

Conclusion: 通过感知时效性的大小VLM协作调度，可以在有限车载资源的约束下有效兼顾大模型的推理精度与信息的及时性，为基础设施辅助的自动驾驶感知提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous driving (AD) systems is constrained by on-board computing power, restricting vehicles to small VLMs (SVLMs) with limited perception and reasoning capabilities. Infrastructure-assisted AD alleviates this resource constraint by enabling collaboration with large VLMs (LVLMs) at edge servers. However, in dynamic vehicular environments, the utility of sensory data for downstream tasks decays rapidly, making timeliness of information a critical concern. To balance the accuracy gains of LVLMs with their latency-induced timeliness degradation, we develop a Timeliness-Aware Large-Small VLM Collaboration (TALSC) framework. Specifically, we first model the Age of Information (AoI) evolution for VLM inference and characterize the coupling among AoI, token length, and task performance to formulate a general timeliness metric. Building on this, we propose the TALSC online scheduling algorithm. Since scheduling decisions have a delayed impact on future timeliness metric and the output token number is unknown at scheduling time, we design a Lyapunov drift-plus-estimated-penalty algorithm and provides a guaranteed performance. In simulation, we first conduct a case study to derive a fitted timeliness metric based on nuScenes dataset, and further show that TALSC outperforms baselines under various communication and computing settings, achieving up to a 12.6\% normalized improvement in Micro-F1 score compared with the best-performing baseline.

</details>


### [182] [Learning-Based Collaborative MEC for LLM Inference with Soft-Deadline Awareness via Transformer-Enhanced PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02031)
*Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Bjorn Landfeldt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了软截止时间约束下，移动边缘计算（MEC）服务器协作执行大语言模型（LLM）推理任务的问题。提出了一种具有受限灵活性的扩展截止时间机制，并开发了基于Transformer增强的近端策略优化（PPO）框架，用于协调多MEC服务器间的任务迁移与调度，最大化截止时间内完成的任务数量，同时最小化截止时间扩展的使用。仿真结果表明，该方法在任务完成率和系统效率上显著优于传统PPO和启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理任务在MEC环境中存在严格的延迟约束，且任务间存在依赖关系，任何子任务的截止时间错过都可能导致整个请求失败。现有方法未能有效处理大规模计算、严格延迟约束以及受限截止时间扩展之间的平衡问题。

Method: 提出一种Transformer增强的PPO框架：1）设计扩展截止时间机制，允许有限次数的截止时间延长；2）利用Transformer捕获任务间的时间依赖关系和多服务器间的交互信息；3）通过强化学习PPO算法优化任务迁移决策，以最大化截止时间内完成任务数并最小化截止时间扩展使用。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的Transformer增强PPO方法在任务完成率和整体系统效率方面显著优于传统PPO算法和基于启发式的方法。

Conclusion: Transformer增强的PPO框架能够有效解决MEC环境中LLM推理任务在软截止时间约束下的协作调度问题，通过合理的任务迁移决策实现系统性能提升。未来可进一步探索更复杂的多服务器协作场景和更精细的截止时间管理策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates collaborative mobile edge computing (MEC) servers for large language model (LLM) inference under soft deadline constraints. In this system, to improve the quality of service, computations are expected to be completed within their deadlines. However, due to dependencies among tasks or subtasks, any missed deadline can lead to catastrophic consequences for the entire request. In this context, this work proposes an extended deadline mechanism with constrained flexibility. The main challenges lie in handling large-scale computations under strict latency constraints while limiting the number of allowable deadline extensions, especially in the presence of task dependencies within each request. To tackle these challenges, we develop a transformer-enhanced proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework that enables efficient collaboration among MEC servers. The proposed approach aims to maximize the number of tasks completed within their deadlines while minimizing the use of deadline extensions. By capturing temporal dependencies and cross-server interactions, the transformer improves decision-making for task migration. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms conventional PPO and heuristic-based approaches in terms of task completion rate and overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [183] [DEFT: Joint Task Placement and DVFS for Energy-Efficient Multi-GPU Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02122)
*Jing Chen,Miquel Pericas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DEFT是一个面向多GPU任务型应用的能耗感知调度框架，通过联合优化任务到设备的分配和每GPU的DVFS配置来降低能耗。在NVIDIA L40S和L4上平均降低能耗14.8%和4.8%，EDP降低9.9%和3.7%，同时性能损失控制在1.5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU调度系统将DVFS与任务放置和GPU间通信解耦，多集中于单GPU执行，无法在多GPU环境下根据任务粒度和运行时竞争自适应调节频率，从而无法把握任务放置、频率选择与GPU间数据移动之间的紧密耦合关系。

Method: DEFT采用成本模型驱动的策略，联合优化任务到设备的分配和每GPU的DVFS配置。该方法整合了松弛感知、吞吐量感知，并显式建模任务执行成本、GPU间数据移动和DVFS转换开销，在任务粒度上实现协调的放置与频率决策，适应动态运行时条件。原型实现在CUDASTF运行时中。

Result: 在五个优化目标上验证了DEFT的有效性。在NVIDIA L40S和L4上分别平均降低能耗14.8%和4.8%，EDP分别降低9.9%和3.7%，同时将性能保持在最快基线方案的1.5%以内。

Conclusion: DEFT证明了在多GPU任务型应用中，将任务放置与DVFS频率选择联合优化能够有效降低能耗和EDP，同时几乎不损失性能，为多GPU系统的能耗感知调度提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Energy efficiency has become a first-order concern in modern high-performance computing systems, as it directly determines achievable throughput under fixed power budgets. Although Dynamic Voltage and Frequency Scaling (DVFS) provides an effective mechanism for reducing GPU energy consumption, existing runtime systems decouple DVFS from task placement and inter-GPU communication, focus on single-GPU execution, or cannot adapt frequency to task granularity and runtime contention in multi-GPU environments. Consequently, current schedulers fail to capture the tight coupling between task placement, frequency selection, and inter-GPU data movement that fundamentally governs energy-performance trade-offs on multi-GPU systems.
  This paper presents DEFT, an energy-aware scheduling framework that jointly optimizes task-to-device assignment and per-GPU DVFS configuration for task-based multi-GPU applications. DEFT employs a cost-model-driven strategy that integrates slack awareness, throughput awareness, and explicit modeling of task execution cost, inter-GPU data movement, and DVFS transition overheads, enabling coordinated placement and frequency decisions at task granularity under dynamic runtime conditions. We prototype DEFT within the CUDASTF runtime and demonstrate its effectiveness across five optimization objectives. The evaluation shows that DEFT reduces energy consumption by 14.8% and 4.8% on average on NVIDIA L40S and L4, and reduces EDP by 9.9% and 3.7%, respectively, while maintaining performance within 1.5% of the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [184] [Efficiency and Cost Alignment in Batched LLM Serving via Resource-Fair Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02244)
*Dayi Yao,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了批量大语言模型（LLM）服务中的资源分配效率问题：异构请求共享解码批次时，批次成本由最大KV缓存足迹决定，导致短请求与长请求共批次时遭受不成比例的延迟和GPU资源消耗。作者将这一现象形式化为资源公平调度问题，提出ISJL（Insert-Short-Jobs-with-Limit）算法，并证明其全局竞争比下界为3/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM服务中存在资源分配无效率：由于解码批次步骤的墙钟成本主要由最大的活动KV缓存足迹决定，短请求与长请求共批次时会承担与其自身token工作量不成比例的延迟和GPU资源消耗，需要设计兼顾公平性和吞吐量的调度策略。

Method: 首先将批量资源不公平现象形式化为资源公平调度问题，建立连接批内资源公平性与系统吞吐量的数学调度模型。公平性约束限定了共批次请求间解码进度（即KV缓存足迹）的差异。基于该模型设计了ISJL算法，一种参数化的混合批处理策略，并分析其在token计量定价下的利润影响。

Result: 证明ISJL算法实现全局竞争比下界3/4。数值实验表明ISJL在FCFS（存在大批量外部性）和LJF（成本对齐但牺牲批量灵活性）之间占据有利中间地带，实现了高吞吐量与成本-收入对齐的双目标调度。

Conclusion: ISJL提供了双准则调度策略：在保持高吞吐量的同时，将最大驱动批次成本与token计量收入对齐，有效平衡了资源公平性与系统效率。

Abstract: This paper studies a resource-allocation inefficiency in batched large language model (LLM) serving: heterogeneous requests that share a decode batch impose max-driven computational costs on one another. Because the wall-clock cost of a batch step is largely governed by the largest active KV-cache footprint, a short request co-batched with a long request can experience latency and GPU-resource consumption disproportionate to its own token workload. We formalize this phenomenon as a resource-fair scheduling problem. We develop a mathematical scheduling model that connects within-batch resource fairness to system throughput. The proposed fairness constraint bounds the disparity in decode progress, equivalently KV-cache footprint, among co-batched requests. Based on this model, we design the Insert-Short-Jobs-with-Limit (ISJL) algorithm, a parameterized hybrid batching policy. We prove that ISJL achieves a global competitive-ratio lower bound of $3/4$. We further examine the profit implications of resource-fair scheduling under the token-metered pricing convention used by commercial LLM APIs. Numerical experiments show that ISJL occupies a favorable middle ground between FCFS, which has large batching externalities, and LJF, which is cost-aligned but sacrifices batching flexibility. Thus, ISJL provides a bi-criterion scheduling policy: it maintains high throughput while aligning max-driven batch cost with token-metered revenue.

</details>


### [185] [Epico: Long-Lived WebAssembly Components for High-Performance Serverless Stream Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02361)
*Matteo Della Bartola,Valerio Besozzi,Patrizio Dazzi,Marco Danelutto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Epico是一个专为流处理设计的无服务器运行时，通过持久化WebAssembly组件、基于队列深度的独立弹性伸缩以及broker-free的ZeroMQ直接路由，解决了FaaS模型在流处理中的低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的FaaS模型（如无状态、中心化编排的函数）不适合流处理场景，因为它无法高效处理连续、低延迟的事件流。现有无服务器架构在流处理中存在编排器瓶颈和容器开销等问题。

Method: Epico将流水线阶段执行为持久化WebAssembly组件，支持基于队列深度SLO的零到无限独立自动伸缩；使用broker-free ZeroMQ通道在阶段间直接路由事件；采用基于信用的滑动窗口优化短执行路径，摊销进程间通信成本；使用AOT编译减少冷启动延迟。

Result: AOT编译将冷启动延迟从数百毫秒降至亚毫秒级；信用窗口将单worker吞吐量提升最多4.3倍；与Apache OpenWhisk相比，绕过了编排器瓶颈和容器开销。

Conclusion: Epico在运行时层面解决了FaaS流处理的固有不高效问题，证明了通过合理设计（持久化组件、直接路由、AOT编译）无服务器架构可以高效支持流处理工作负载。

Abstract: While serverless computing is popular, its dominant Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) model is ill-suited for stream processing because its stateless, centrally orchestrated functions cannot efficiently handle continuous, low-latency event flows. We introduce Epico, a serverless runtime explicitly designed to resolve these inefficiencies at the runtime level. Epico executes pipeline stages as persistent WebAssembly components, enabling independent, zero-to-infinity autoscaling based on queue-depth SLOs and routing events directly between stages using broker-free ZeroMQ channels. To optimize short execution paths, it utilizes a credit-based sliding window to amortize inter-process communication costs. Evaluations demonstrate that Ahead-of-Time (AOT) compilation reduces cold-start latencies from hundreds of milliseconds to sub-millisecond ranges, while the credit window improves single-worker throughput by up to \(4.3\times\). Compared to Apache OpenWhisk, Epico bypasses the orchestrator bottlenecks and container overheads that typically hinder FaaS streaming workloads.

</details>


### [186] [Greedy-Like Defective Coloring: Distributed Algorithms and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02386)
*Marc Fuchs,Fabian Kuhn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Barenboim和Elkin提出的两趟贪心算法，将其推广到列表缺陷染色问题，并分析了广义两趟算法在标准缺陷染色中的改进效果与理论局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷染色算法是许多确定性分布式染色算法的核心。若某些版本的缺陷染色能被更高效地解决，整体复杂度可以得到改进。当前最好的缺陷/颜色权衡在O(log* n)时间算法中仍有提升空间。

Method: 1) 将Barenboim和Elkin的两趟贪心算法推广到列表缺陷染色问题；2) 分析广义两趟算法在标准缺陷染色中的表现，证明当颜色数c不是完全平方数时，可在多数情况下将缺陷常数因子改进；3) 证明该广义算法在c-染色中无法将缺陷降至(1-o(1))·Δ/√c以下。

Result: 推广到列表缺陷染色后，获得了在CONGEST模型中计算proper (Δ+1)-染色需要Õ(√Δ)+O(log* n)轮的替代算法。对于标准缺陷染色，当c不是完全平方数时可在多数情况下改进缺陷常数因子；但存在理论下界，缺陷不能低于(1-o(1))·Δ/√c。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了两趟贪心算法的适用范围（列表缺陷染色），在标准缺陷染色中取得了常数因子的改进，同时揭示了该算法框架的固有限制，为后续算法设计提供了理论边界。

Abstract: A $d$-defective $c$-coloring of a graph $G=(V,E)$ is a coloring of the nodes $V$ with $c$ colors such that every node has at most $d$ neighbors of the same color. Distributed algorithms for computing different variants of defective coloring are at the core of most deterministic state-of-the-art distributed coloring algorithms, and they are also an important tool in many other distributed graph algorithms. In several cases, the overall complexity could be improved if some version of defective coloring could be solved more efficiently. Barenboim and Elkin [STOC '09] introduced a two-pass greedy algorithm that uses $p^2$ colors with defect $\lfloor Δ/p\rfloor$ in $O(Δ+\log^{\ast} n)$ rounds. This remains the best defect/color tradeoff for $O(\log^{\ast} n)$-time algorithms in bounded-degree graphs. This paper expands the capabilities of this two-pass algorithm. First, we generalize it to the \emph{list defective coloring} problem (Fuchs and Kuhn, [DISC '23]). Consequently, we obtain an alternative algorithm for computing a proper $(Δ+1)$-coloring in $\tilde{O}(\sqrtΔ) + O(\log^{\ast} n)$ rounds in the CONGEST model. Second, we analyze a generalized two-pass algorithm for standard defective colorings. We prove that if the number of colors $c$ is not a perfect square, we can improve the state-of-the-art defect for distributed $c$-colorings by a constant factor in most cases. However, we also prove a limitation: for any $c\geq 1$, this generalized algorithm cannot achieve a $c$-coloring with defect below $(1-o(1))\cdotΔ/\sqrt{c}$.

</details>


### [187] [Analyzing GPU Performance in Virtualized Environments: A~Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02414)
*Adel Belkhiri,Michel Dagenais*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种针对vGPU（虚拟GPU）系统的性能分析工具，兼容Intel GVT-g虚拟化方案，通过软件追踪技术收集运行时数据并生成性能指标，提供同步图形视图以帮助识别vGPU虚拟机中的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU虚拟化为虚拟机提供了可扩展和自适应的GPU资源，但现有性能调试工具大多不支持虚拟GPU（vGPU），缺乏针对vGPU系统的有效性能分析手段，因此需要更先进的工具来弥补这一空白。

Method: 该工具采用软件追踪（software tracing）技术收集详细的运行时数据，并生成相关的性能指标；同时提供多个同步的图形化视图，以帮助使用者深入了解GVT-g的运作机制并识别潜在的性能瓶颈。

Result: 该工具与Intel GVT-g虚拟化解决方案兼容，能够有效收集vGPU系统的运行时数据并生成性能度量，为vGPU虚拟机的性能分析提供了可视化的支持。

Conclusion: 该性能分析工具填补了vGPU系统缺乏专业调试与分析工具的空白，其核心原理可推广应用于多种基于vGPU的系统，能够帮助实践者深入洞察GVT-g操作并定位性能瓶颈。

Abstract: The graphics processing unit (GPU) plays a crucial role in boosting application performance and enhancing computational tasks. Thanks to its parallel architecture and energy efficiency, the GPU has become essential in many computing scenarios. On the other hand, the advent of GPU virtualization has been a significant breakthrough, as it provides scalable and adaptable GPU resources for virtual machines. However, this technology faces challenges in debugging and analyzing the performance of GPU-accelerated applications. Most current performance tools do not support virtual GPUs (vGPUs), highlighting the need for more advanced tools. Thus, this article introduces a novel performance analysis tool that is designed for systems using vGPUs. Our tool is compatible with the Intel GVT-g virtualization solution, although its underlying principles can apply to many vGPU-based systems. Our tool uses software tracing techniques to gather detailed runtime data and generate relevant performance metrics. It also offers many synchronized graphical views, which gives practitioners deep insights into GVT-g operations and helps them identify potential performance bottlenecks in vGPU-enabled virtual machines.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Hierarchical BM25: Lexical Search at Billion-Document Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00229)
*Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出分层BM25（Hierarchical BM25）方法，通过粗粒度索引选择文档组，再对选定组进行精确搜索，在保持评分与扁平索引完全一致的前提下，将内存占用限制在~4.4GB（与语料规模无关），并在十亿文档上实现~300ms的查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扁平BM25索引在十亿文档规模下占用约400GB内存，磁盘服务每查询需4-12秒，无法在交互延迟预算内实现精确top-k词法检索。现有方法在内存、延迟与精度之间存在不可调和的矛盾。

Method: 构建一个常驻的粗粒度索引，将文档划分为约1000个主题均衡、规模均衡的文档组。利用两个信号选择查询需访问的组：①查询词在组内的总词频；②对于分布过于稀疏的词，判断多个查询词是否出现在同一文档中。随后对选定组进行穷举搜索，并使用约100KB的全局统计信息进行评分。

Result: 常驻内存约4.4GB（与语料规模无关）；十亿文档上16词查询约300ms返回（吞吐量为扁平多线程索引的4.7-5.6倍）；缓存预热后每秒约32次查询（扁平索引不足3次）；50万文档配置下访问5-10%的簇可恢复0.83-0.92的穷举结果得分。

Conclusion: 分层BM25通过将近似限制在"组选择"阶段而非评分阶段，实现了与扁平索引完全一致的评分精度，同时获得固定且独立于语料规模的内存上限和显著优于扁平索引的延迟表现。但十亿规模的召回率及与BlockMax-WAND的直接对比仍有待验证。

Abstract: A flat BM25 index over one billion documents occupies about 400 GB. Holding it in memory requires DRAM proportional to corpus size. Serving it from disk takes 4-12 seconds per query. Exact top-k lexical retrieval at this scale is therefore impractical within an interactive latency budget.
  Hierarchical BM25 gives up exact ranking in exchange for fixed bounds on memory and latency. A resident coarse index selects which of ~1K topical, size-balanced document groups a query visits, using two signals: the total frequency of each query term within a group, and, for informative terms spread too thinly across groups for frequency totals to reflect, whether several of them appear together in one document. Selected groups are then searched exhaustively and scored against ~100 KB of global statistics. Every returned score therefore equals the flat index's score, and the approximation is confined to selection alone. The resident footprint is ~4.4 GB, independent of corpus size. Sixteen-term queries over one billion documents return in ~300 ms (4.7x to 5.6x the throughput of a flat multi-threaded index), and a warmed cache sustains ~32 queries per second versus under 3 for flat indexing. At a 500K-document configuration, visiting 5-10% of clusters recovers 0.83-0.92 of the exhaustive result score. Billion-scale recall and a direct comparison against document-reordered BlockMax-WAND remain open.

</details>


### [189] [CeQe: Grounding Lexical Retrieval in Semantic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00452)
*Adam Kahirov,Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Encoder Query Expansion (CE-QE)，利用交叉编码器的逐token相关性归因，从语义检索结果中提取关键术语并追加到BM25查询中，解决BM25的语义词汇鸿沟问题，在BEIR基准上显著提升词法召回率并超过SPLADEv2和ColBERTv2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM25仅捕获精确关键词匹配，当相关文档与查询用不同词汇表达时无法检索到该文档，且下游重排序或融合无法恢复候选集中缺失的文档。现有的伪相关反馈复用BM25自身的（可能错误的）结果会导致自强化查询漂移；生成式查询扩展（如HyDE）依赖大语言模型的参数化知识，可能引入语料库中不存在的词汇。

Method: CE-QE首先对语义检索的top结果应用交叉编码器，读取每个token的相关性归因分数，选出交叉编码器认为具有决定性作用的术语，将这些术语逐字追加到BM25查询中。扩展词全部来自检索段落原文，不会引入语料库之外的词汇。此外还提出SESF分数融合变体，将CE-QE与交叉编码器分数融合结合。

Result: 在七个BEIR数据集上，CE-QE在查询与答案词汇分叉的场景下显著提升词法召回率（如NQ Recall@100从0.32提升至0.47）。SESF变体在Recall@100上比交叉编码器分数融合高2.5%，在nDCG@10上比SPLADEv2高5.3%、比ColBERTv2高4.6%，且无需修改底层BM25索引。

Conclusion: CE-QE提供了一种轻量、无需修改索引的混合检索增强方案：通过交叉编码器的归因分析将语义信息注入词法检索，避免了伪相关反馈的自强化陷阱和生成式方法引入虚假词汇的问题，在混合检索范式中取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Lexical retrieval (BM25) captures exact keyword matches and weights terms by corpus-wide significance, but it is blind to the semantic vocabulary gap: when a relevant document phrases an answer differently from the query, BM25 never retrieves it, and no amount of downstream reranking or fusion can recover a document that was never in the candidate set. We present Cross-Encoder Query Expansion (CE-QE), which reads the per-token relevance attributions of a cross-encoder applied to top semantic search results, selects the terms the cross-encoder treats as decisive, and appends them to the BM25 query. Unlike classical pseudo-relevance feedback, which reuses BM25's own (possibly wrong) top results, CE-QE seeds expansion from the semantic retriever's results, avoiding self-reinforcing query drift. Unlike recent generative query expansion (HyDE, Query2doc), which prompts a large language model to hallucinate text from its parametric knowledge, every CE-QE expansion term is copied verbatim from a retrieved passage, so it cannot introduce vocabulary the corpus does not contain, and its only added cost is attribution extraction on a cross-encoder a hybrid pipeline already runs for reranking. On seven BEIR datasets, CE-QE improves lexical recall substantially where query and answer vocabulary diverge (e.g., NQ Recall@100 from 0.32 to 0.47), and its score-fusion variant (SESF) beats cross-encoder score fusion by 2.5% on Recall@100 and beats SPLADEv2 and ColBERTv2 by 5.3% and 4.6% on nDCG@10, while leaving the underlying BM25 index completely unmodified.

</details>


### [190] [A Context-Aware Cultural Heritage Guide Powered by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00549)
*Liliana Ardissono,Fabio Ferrero,Angelo Geninatti Cossatin,Claudio Mattutino,Noemi Mauro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个文化遗产Web应用Triangolazioni的扩展，通过松耦合且与LLM无关的架构，利用大语言模型丰富策展内容，提供上下文相关的外部信息搜索与展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产应用中策展内容往往缺乏上下文相关的外部信息，需要利用LLM来增强内容的丰富性和可访问性。

Method: 在Triangolazioni文化遗产Web应用中引入松耦合架构扩展，该架构与具体LLM无关（agnostic），支持上下文相关的信息搜索与展示。

Result: 成功扩展了Triangolazioni应用，使其能够利用LLM在架构层面不依赖特定模型的情况下，为策展内容提供上下文相关的外部信息。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种在文化遗产Web应用中集成LLM的可行方案，其松耦合和模型无关的架构设计具有较好的灵活性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present an extension of Triangolazioni (a Cultural Heritage webapp) to enrich curated content with context-dependent, external information provided by Large Language Models (LLMs) within a loosely-coupled architecture agnostic to the LLM. The system supports context-dependent information search and presentation within an architecture agnostic to the exploited LLM.

</details>


### [191] [PHA-Net: Prototype-based Hierarchical Alignment Network for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00551)
*Xiaolun Jing,Kezhao Yin,Xinxing Yang,Genke Yang,Jian Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原型的分层对齐网络（PHA-Net），通过引入模态共享原型作为桥梁，高效地对齐文本与视频在个体、局部和全局三个层次上的语义表示，并在四个基准数据集上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-视频检索方法需要同时对齐个体、局部和全局三个层级的跨模态语义，但存在两个问题：一是文本简洁与视频丰富之间的固有语义不匹配；二是已有的分层方法仅优化视觉表示且计算成本过高。此外，聚类令牌中不均衡的语义分布也会损害检索性能。

Method: 提出PHA-Net框架：（1）引入多个模态共享原型作为桥梁，同时高效优化文本和视频表示；（2）设计原型支持的令牌合并模块，利用原型语义引导增强强语义令牌并抑制弱语义令牌；（3）提出原型对比损失，鼓励文本和视觉原型聚焦不同的语义信息，确保相同原型的文本-视觉原型相似度高于不同原型。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上取得了显著改进：MSR-VTT上所有召回率之和提升8.8%，ActivityNet提升19.2%，VATEX提升0.7%，Charades提升4.9%。

Conclusion: PHA-Net通过原型机制有效解决了跨模态语义对齐中的语义不匹配和计算开销问题，验证了原型引导的令牌合并和原型对比损失在提升文本-视频检索性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: With the emergence of large-scale image-text pre-training models, e.g., CLIP, text-video retrieval has experienced substantial advances in recent years. Existing best-performing methods involve aligning cross-modal semantics at individual, local, and global levels simultaneously, raising concerns about the intrinsic semantic mismatch between concise texts and rich videos. A canonical approach is to integrate multiple language-video attention modules into the hierarchical framework while this paradigm only optimizes visual representations with prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a new prototype-based hierarchical alignment network (PHA-Net) to align individual/local/global level representations across modalities. Concretely, we introduce multiple modality-shared prototypes as the bridge to efficiently optimize text and video representations for cross-modal alignment. Then, we argue that the imbalanced semantic distribution in clustered tokens may undermine retrieval performance, as tokens with weak semantics are of little interest. To reduce the impact of these tokens, a proposed prototype-supported token merge module is responsible for enhancing tokens with strong semantics and suppressing others with weak semantics via prototype semantics guidance. Moreover, we devise a prototype contrastive loss to encourage textual and visual prototypes to focus on different semantic information. The idea of this auxiliary loss is to ensure higher similarity between textual and visual prototypes from the same prototype than those from different prototypes. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of our PHA-Net, which achieves significant improvements in the sum of all recalls on MSR-VTT (8.8%), ActivityNet (19.2%), VATEX (0.7%), and Charades (4.9%). Code is available at https://github.com/JingXiaolun/PHA-Net.

</details>


### [192] [GARDRec: Decision-Level Graph Grounding for Large Language Model Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00669)
*Yong Wang,Hongliang Sun,Jinlan Liu,Hua Zhang,Dianbo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GARDRec框架，将图结构信息与LLM结合，通过语义-结构项目表征、个性化图上下文和连续多模态提示，提升基于LLM的下一项推荐排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图增强LLM推荐器主要将知识图谱作为提示层面的证据，排序决策缺乏结构化用户-项目关系的强约束，导致在下一项推荐任务中模型难以在同一用户上下文中比较候选项目并保留时序偏好、协同信号和属性匹配。

Method: GARDRec包含四个关键组件：1) 从文本节点特征和图传播构建语义-结构项目表征；2) 从时间加权历史和一阶邻域推导个性化图上下文；3) 通过连续多模态提示将图衍生表征与冻结LLM对齐；4) 通过后期决策分支注入显式交互与匹配特征，并使用候选间注意力和受限生成似然支持最终排序。

Result: 在三个公开基准上、使用多种LLM骨干网络的实验表明，GARDRec普遍优于代表性基线的候选排序性能；消融和诊断分析验证了图投影、邻域检索、显式决策特征、排序损失和生成校准的各自贡献。

Conclusion: GARDRec通过将图结构信息深度融入LLM排序过程，而非仅作为提示补充，有效提升了基于LLM的下一项推荐性能，验证了图投影、决策分支和生成校准等关键设计的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for recommendation by interpreting item descriptions, user instructions, and external knowledge through natural-language prompts. However, existing graph-augmented LLM recommenders often use knowledge graphs mainly as prompt-level evidence, leaving ranking decisions weakly constrained by structured user-item relations. This is problematic for next-item recommendation, where the model must compare candidates under the same user context while preserving temporal preference, collaborative signals, and attribute matches. To address this issue, we propose \emph{GARDRec}, a Graph-grounded Adaptive Reasoning and Decision-aware Recommendation framework for LLM-based next-item ranking. GARDRec constructs semantic-structural item representations from textual node features and graph propagation, derives personalized graph contexts from temporally weighted histories and first-order neighborhoods, and aligns graph-derived representations with a frozen LLM through continuous multimodal prompts. Explicit interaction and matching features are injected through late-stage decision branches, while inter-candidate attention and restricted generative likelihood support final ranking. Experiments on three public benchmarks with multiple LLM backbones show that GARDRec generally improves candidate-ranking performance over representative baselines. Ablation and diagnostic analyses verify the contributions of graph projection, neighborhood retrieval, explicit decision features, ranking loss, and generative calibration.

</details>


### [193] [A Triple-Robustness Analysis of Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Hop Requirements Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00705)
*Meftun Akarsu,Burak Özdemir,Doğancan Büyükçolak,Recep Kaan Karaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"三重稳健性分析"框架，通过在固定五流水线架构矩阵的前提下，同时变化嵌入器、语料库和评判者三个维度，系统性地检验了GraphRAG与传统向量RAG的性能对比结论。研究发现：GraphRAG的检索阶段与合成阶段的引用精度差异巨大（0.12-0.23 vs 0.48-0.65），评估引用测量点会颠倒架构排名；答案级引用胜者具有语料库和层级条件依赖性但嵌入器稳健性；LLM评判者在不同检索状态下显示出严重的脆弱性（自我kappa仅为0.137）。作者主张RAG架构结论必须在更高的稳健性水平下进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG与向量RAG的对比研究结论相互矛盾，其原因在于大多数研究仅基于单一语料库、单一嵌入器和单一评判者进行评估。此外，本文发现引用质量的测量位置（检索集vs合成集）也是导致结论分歧的关键因素，但这一因素在以往研究中被忽视。因此需要一个更全面的稳健性分析框架来调和这些矛盾并指导未来的实证评估。

Method: 采用三重稳健性分析：固定五流水线架构矩阵（GraphRAG、vanilla向量RAG、agentic流水线等），变化三个维度——嵌入器（本地e5-small vs Azure text-embedding-3-small）、语料库（DO-178C类型化边需求 vs MuSiQue维基百科段落链）、评判者（配对GPT-5.4 × GPT-4.1在两个语料库上）。共执行2×4,440主矩阵运行、600跨语料库运行和超过5,000次忠实性评判。同时训练一个基于稠密嵌入的跳数分类路由器，并评估LLM评判者自身的稳定性（同日测试-重测和11周后重评）。

Result: 核心发现包括：(C2a) GraphRAG的图遍历在检索阶段引用精度仅为0.12-0.23，但合成阶段引用精度达0.48-0.65，将检索集视为归因集会颠倒架构排名；(C1) GraphRAG在短跳DO-178C查询上与vanilla持平，在MuSiQue所有层级中胜出，agentic流水线仅在3+跳需求查询中领先；(C2b) DO-178C上忠实性随跳距下降，维基百科链上无此现象；(C3) 单评判者LLM忠实性评估脆弱——GPT-5.4跨嵌入器自kappa仅0.137，11周后重评kappa≤0.14；(C4) 学习路由器在跳数分类上达到macro-F1 0.86。

Conclusion: RAG架构的性能结论高度依赖于评估方法的各个维度，包括引用测量点的选择、语料库特性以及评判者的稳定性。研究者必须在这种多维度稳健性水平上检验RAG架构主张，特别是对引用测量位置保持敏感，否则得出的结论可能具有误导性。本文为未来RAG评估建立了更严格的方法论标准。

Abstract: Reported verdicts on GraphRAG versus vector RAG disagree, and the evidence is typically tied to a single corpus, embedder, and judge -- and, we show, to where citation quality is measured. We present a triple-robustness analysis that holds a five-pipeline architecture matrix fixed and varies embedder (local e5-small vs. Azure text-embedding-3-small), corpus (DO-178C typed-edge requirements vs. Wikipedia paragraph chains via MuSiQue), and judge (paired GPT-5.4 x GPT-4.1 on both corpora), over 2x4,440 main-matrix runs, 600 cross-corpus runs, and over 5,000 faithfulness judgments. (C2a) GraphRAG's graph walk floods the context window at precision 0.12-0.23, but the synthesizer cites selectively at precision 0.48-0.65; scoring the retrieved set as the attribution set inverts the architecture ranking, which reconciles part of the disagreement in prior reports. (C1) Answer-level citation winners are corpus- and stratum-conditional but embedder-robust: GraphRAG ties vanilla on short-hop DO-178C queries and wins every MuSiQue stratum, while agentic pipelines lead only on 3+-hop requirements queries. (C2b) Faithfulness is corpus-conditional: on DO-178C it declines with hop distance (trend p<0.05 in three of four judge x embedder combinations); on Wikipedia chains neither judge shows a collapse. (C3) Single-judge LLM faithfulness is fragile to retrieval state: GPT-5.4's self-kappa across embedders is 0.137 (41% verdict change) against a same-day test-retest floor of 0.76, and re-judging frozen inputs eleven weeks later gives kappa <= 0.14 for both judges. A learned router on dense embeddings alone reaches macro-F1 0.86 on hop classification (C4). We argue that RAG architecture claims should be tested at this level of robustness -- including robustness to the citation-measurement point -- before they are trusted.

</details>


### [194] [Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization for Generative Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00750)
*Kaifeng Guo,Yiming Yang,Jingtong Gao,Guolei Zeng,Fukang Yang,Yukang Liang,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了层次残差策略优化（HRPO）框架，将物品级别的反馈信号转化为稠密的、token对齐的学习信号，用于生成式推荐器的后训练，以解决监督式下一个token预测无法直接优化下游效用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器通过自回归解码语义标识符（SIDs）来选择物品，但SID解码器通过监督式下一个token预测训练，仅模仿记录轨迹而非直接优化下游效用。后训练方法面临稀疏、高方差和层级依赖的token级信用分配问题，因为日志反馈仅在最终曝光物品上观测到。

Method: HRPO包含三个步骤：(1) 通过基于特征用户聚类的组别奖励平滑，估计SID前缀级别的效用；(2) 将这些效用分解为残差token信用并累积为credit-to-go信号；(3) 使用残差回报策略优化（RRPO）通过裁剪更新、组归一化优势函数和KL正则化来优化残差信用。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模商业系统的在线A/B测试中，HRPO在会话级效用和关键业务指标上均取得了一致的提升。源代码和存档工件已公开供复现。

Conclusion: HRPO通过将稀疏的物品级反馈转化为密集的token对齐信号，实现了保守的token级改进，有效地弥合了生成式推荐器中监督训练与下游效用优化之间的差距。

Abstract: Generative recommenders select items by autoregressively decoding semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose token positions induce a coarse-to-fine hierarchy over the item space. In practice, SID decoders are trained via supervised next-token prediction, which imitates logged trajectories rather than directly optimizing downstream utility. This motivates post-training with outcome feedback to guide decoding toward higher utility. However, logged feedback is only observed for the final exposed item, causing most post-training methods to operate at the item level and broadcast the same terminal signal across all SID tokens. As a result, token-level credit assignment becomes sparse, high-variance, and layer-dependent. To this end, we propose Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization (HRPO), a post-training framework that converts item-level outcomes into dense, token-aligned learning signals for conservative token-wise improvement. Specifically, HRPO first estimates SID prefix-level utilities via group-wise reward smoothing over feature-based user clusters. It then decomposes these utilities into residual token credits and accumulates them into credit-to-go signals. Finally, Residual-Return Policy Optimization (RRPO) optimizes the residual credits using clipped updates, group-normalized advantages, and KL regularization to preserve stability. Experiments on a public dataset and an online A/B test in a large-scale commercial system show consistent gains in session-level utility and key business metrics. Source code and the archived artifact are available for reproduction.

</details>


### [195] [Exponential Reward Weighting for Fine-Tuning Generative Recommenders under Sparse and Noisy Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00816)
*Keertana Chidambaram,Sanath Kumar Krishnamurthy,Qiuling Xu,Ko-Jen Hsiao,Moumita Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出指数奖励加权微调（Exp-RSFT）方法，通过对日志交互按exp(r/λ)加权来直接优化日志奖励，利用温度参数λ平衡覆盖成本与噪声成本，在三个公开基准和一个大规模工业数据集上验证了性能随λ呈倒U型曲线，优于PPO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的用户反馈既稀疏又嘈杂，生成式推荐器的后训练面临两大挑战：从日志交互训练出的奖励模型难以泛化，直接优化不完美的奖励又会导致奖励过度优化。需要一种能直接利用日志奖励、又能对抗噪声的微调方法。

Method: Exp-RSFT将每个日志交互按exp(r/λ)加权，直接优化日志奖励而非学习奖励模型，其中温度参数λ用于正则化奖励中的噪声。理论分析表明其次优性分解为覆盖成本（由日志策略限制导致）和噪声成本（由不完美反馈导致），λ在这两种竞争效应间取得最优平衡。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个大规模工业数据集上，性能随λ呈现倒U型趋势，验证了理论预测；PPO和DPO常常过度优化不可靠的奖励模型从而降低推荐质量，而Exp-RSFT无需在线探索或偏好数据即可一致地提升排序性能。

Conclusion: Exp-RSFT通过温度参数λ的调节，在利用高奖励行为与对噪声的鲁棒性之间实现最优权衡，为生成式推荐器的后训练提供了一种简单有效且无需奖励模型、无需在线探索或偏好数据的方案。

Abstract: In recommendation systems, users interact with only a small fraction of a vast item catalog, producing feedback that is both sparse and noisy. This challenges post-training generative recommenders: reward models trained from logged interactions often fail to generalize, while directly optimizing imperfect rewards can lead to reward over-optimization. We propose Exponential reward-weighted fine-tuning (Exp-RSFT), where each logged interaction is weighted by $\exp(r/λ)$, avoids this failure by optimizing directly on the logged rewards, with the temperature $λ$ regularizing against their noise. We theoretically show that Exp-RSFT's suboptimality decomposes into two costs: a coverage cost arising from limitations of the logging policy and a noise cost from imperfect feedback. The temperature $λ$ balances these competing effects, yielding an optimal tradeoff between exploiting high-reward behavior and robustness to noise. Across three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, we verify this theoretical prediction: performance follows an inverted-U trend as a function of $λ$, while PPO and DPO often over-optimize unreliable reward models and degrade recommendation quality. Exp-RSFT consistently improves ranking performance without requiring online exploration or preference data.

</details>


### [196] [Tevatron Meets Megatron: Expert-Parallel LLM Reranker Training on an Academic Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00916)
*Zhichao Xu,Xueguang Ma,Shengyao Zhuang,Luyu Gao,Wenqian Ye,Yu Wang,Jamie Callan,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Tevatron 3.0，将Megatron-Core训练后端集成到Tevatron中，支持大规模MoE重排模型的训练（如30B参数的Qwen3-30B-A3B），在保持数据流程和检查点兼容性的同时，相比FSDP1在推荐配置下快22%，并通过实验发现MoE重排器能以不到一半的激活参数匹配稠密8B模型的质量且推理吞吐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代重排训练方法（亿级cross-encoder、MoE骨干、蒸馏）已超越大多数学术组的训练基础设施能力。现有Tevatron依赖Hugging Face Trainer和DeepSpeed/FSDP1，但这些后端对大规模MoE训练支持不足，亟需更高效的训练框架。

Method: 将Megatron-Core训练后端集成到Tevatron中，保留原有数据流程、评估工作流和Hugging Face兼容检查点。通过专家并行（expert parallelism）支持训练30B参数的MoE重排器，并支持LoRA和全参数微调。在BEIR-15上使用三种第一阶段检索器进行受控对比实验。

Result: Megatron在数据并行设置下与FSDP重排质量和训练效率相当，单节点推荐配置下快22%；成功训练30B参数MoE重排器（FSDP1不可行）；MoE重排器以不到一半激活参数匹配稠密8B模型质量，且推理吞吐显著更高。

Conclusion: Tevatron 3.0证明了基于Megatron-Core的后端能有效支撑大规模MoE重排训练，MoE架构在重排任务中兼顾质量与效率，框架和训练好的检查点将开源发布。

Abstract: Modern reranking recipes---billion-scale cross-encoders, mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbones, and distillation against strong teachers---have outpaced the training infrastructure available to most academic groups. Existing Tevatron reranker training relies on the Hugging Face Trainer with DeepSpeed or PyTorch FSDP1, but these backends lack efficient support for large-scale MoE training. We present Tevatron 3.0, which integrates a Megatron-Core training backend into Tevatron while preserving its data pipeline, evaluation workflow, and Hugging Face-compatible checkpoints. We benchmark existing distributed training configurations against the new backend, showing that Megatron matches FSDP reranker quality and training efficiency under comparable data-parallel settings, is up to 22% faster in the recommended single-node configuration, and supports both LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning. Crucially, expert parallelism enables training a 30B-parameter Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE reranker, which is infeasible with PyTorch FSDP1. Using this framework, we conduct a controlled comparison of MoE versus dense models, LoRA versus full-parameter tuning, and distillation versus contrastive training on BEIR-15 with three first-stage retrievers, and report serving throughput for Hugging Face and vLLM. We find that the MoE reranker matches dense 8B quality while activating less than half as many parameters and achieving substantially higher inference throughput. We will release the framework and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [197] [GRACE: Generative Recommender Acceleration Engine for Real-Time Ads Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00938)
*Zhou Fang,Yuhang Huang,Ang Zhang,Yihan He,Ruichao Xiao,Chao Li,Yavuz Yetim,Sibyl Yang,Xiaohan Wei,Fei Tian,Liang Wang,Liyuan Li,Nathan Yan,Gaoxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRACE是一个面向广告生成式检索的在线服务系统，通过生成式目标匹配（GTM）解决广告投放资格过滤问题，并针对编码器-解码器Transformer的宽波束、短序列解码场景进行深度优化，在GH200上实现最高68倍注意力加速和11.1倍整体解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高流量、实时广告检索场景下，生成式推荐系统面临两大服务挑战：一是资格过滤，确保每个生成的广告都符合广告主设置的受众定向规则；二是计算开销，需要在严格的延迟和GPU成本约束下，通过宽波束解码为每次请求生成数千条广告。

Method: GRACE提出两方面设计：1）生成式目标匹配（GTM），扩展目录级约束解码，利用位图和布隆过滤器匹配器实现基于语义ID前缀的个性化过滤，提升广告级目标匹配通过率；2）针对编码器-解码器Transformer的推理优化，聚焦宽波束、短序列场景，覆盖注意力内核、KV缓存和波束搜索三个层面。

Result: SID级GTM将最终广告级定向匹配通过率从23.55%提升至40.42%。在NVIDIA GH200上，相比FlashAttention-2和FlashAttention-3中更快的基线，GRACE将交叉注意力延迟提升68.0倍，自注意力延迟提升23.4-25.8倍，整体解码器延迟降低11.1倍。

Conclusion: GRACE通过结合资格过滤优化和推理引擎重构，成功将广告生成式检索纳入延迟和计算预算之内，验证了在工业级高吞吐实时广告场景中部署生成式推荐系统的可行性。

Abstract: Productionizing generative recommenders for high-volume, real-time ads retrieval creates two serving challenges: eligibility, ensuring that each generated ad is eligible for the request under the advertiser's audience targeting rules, and compute, which requires meeting strict latency and GPU cost requirements while remaining capable of generating thousands of ads per request with wide-beam decoding. This paper presents GRACE, a serving system for ads generative retrieval that addresses both challenges. For eligibility, GRACE introduces Generative Target Matching (GTM), which extends catalog-valid constrained decoding with personalized filtering over Semantic ID (SID) prefixes using bitmask and Bloom filter matchers derived from targeting rules. SID-level GTM improves final ad-level target matching pass rate from 23.55% to 40.42% over constrained decoding alone. For compute-cost and latency, GRACE targets encoder-decoder Transformers, which are more lightweight than LLMs. It redesigns the decoder around the wide-beam, short-sequence regime, covering attention kernels, KV cache, and beam search optimizations. On NVIDIA GH200, compared with the faster of FlashAttention-2 and FlashAttention-3 baselines, GRACE improves cross-attention latency by 68.0 times and self-attention latency by 23.4-25.8 times across decode steps. Together, these changes reduce decoder latency by 11.1 times, keeping ads generative retrieval within latency and compute requirements.

</details>


### [198] [UniHEAR: Unified Heterogeneous-Source Attentive Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01147)
*Ganzhong Luo,Yang Ren,Hanyong Wang,Shuyu Zheng,Menglong Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniHEAR是一个统一的轻量级框架，用于异构来源的实体检索与重排序，通过粗检索描述符、检索引导的注意力模态门控和熵加权源融合，解决了KB-VQA中的单源检索瓶颈和检索源盲重排序问题，在E-VQA和InfoSeek上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA的检索增强系统存在两个关键局限：一是依赖单一检索模态造成单源检索瓶颈，无法获取仅存于互补来源中的真实实体；二是双塔逐点重排序器忽视检索来源和候选级检索先验，导致检索源盲重排序，造成模态依赖冗余。

Method: UniHEAR通过三个核心组件解决上述问题：1) 为每个候选实体构建粗检索描述符；2) 引入检索引导的注意力模态门控，根据描述符调节模态注意力权重；3) 采用熵加权源融合整合粗检索先验。训练上采用对比学习与辅助模态保持损失的混合策略，在单一模型中统一了实体级和篇章级检索。

Result: 在E-VQA和InfoSeek数据集上，UniHEAR在Recall@1上分别超过最强基线6.7和1.2个百分点，取得了SOTA的检索和VQA性能，同时保持了轻量级的重排序架构。

Conclusion: UniHEAR通过统一的轻量级框架有效解决了KB-VQA中异构来源实体检索的多源融合与重排序问题，并开源了代码和模型以供社区使用。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires retrieving relevant entity knowledge from external sources to answer visually grounded questions. Existing retrieval-augmented systems suffer from two critical limitations. First, relying on a single retrieval modality creates a Single-Source Retrieval Bottleneck, missing ground-truth entities that are only accessible through complementary sources. Second, dual-tower pointwise rerankers suffer from Retrieval-Source-Blind Reranking, as they overlook retrieval origins and candidate-level retrieval priors, leading to redundant modality reliance. To address these challenges, we propose UniHEAR, a unified lightweight framework for heterogeneous-source entity retrieval and reranking. UniHEAR constructs a Coarse Retrieval Descriptor for each candidate entity, and introduces Retrieval-Guided Attentive Modality Gating to condition modality attention weights on this descriptor, further complemented by Entropy-Weighted Source Fusion of coarse retrieval priors. A hybrid training strategy combining contrastive learning with an auxiliary modality-preserving loss unifies entity-level and section-level retrieval within a single model. Extensive experiments on E-VQA and InfoSeek demonstrate that UniHEAR achieves state-of-the-art retrieval and VQA performance, improving Recall@1 by 6.7 and 1.2 points over the strongest baselines while maintaining a lightweight reranking architecture. Code and model are available at https://github.com/iven-luo/UniHEAR.

</details>


### [199] [Auditing Semantic Gains in Sequential Recommendation: A Lightweight Recovery Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01260)
*Kong Wang,Zhongke He,Xiang Chen,Hongwei Zeng,Kai Deng,Long Wang,Kehua Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LIME-Rec，一种轻量级、可审计的恢复测试方法，用于厘清语义和生成式检索推荐系统性能提升的真正来源。LIME-Rec融合三个独立专家（SASRec序列、ItemCF共现、基于冻结bge嵌入的语义），通过分数级融合和历史校准实现，在Amazon三个数据集上以R@10 0.0996/0.1105/0.0593超越最强基线7%-12%，证明轻量级恢复测试应作为归因的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期语义和生成式检索推荐系统声称显著优于基于ID的序列基线，但这些提升究竟来源于语言模型推理、语义ID生成、端到端语义架构、更强的离线物品表示，还是语义与协同信号的互补，存在归因模糊性。作者希望厘清这一归因问题。

Method: LIME-Rec组合三个独立专家：SASRec序列专家、ItemCF共现专家和基于冻结BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5物品嵌入的语义专家。全目录分数按用户归一化，通过可审计的分数级融合和有界历史校准组合。融合门和校准头仅在验证集上拟合，服务时无需语言模型推理，且每个专家贡献可单独检查。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Toys和Sports上，LIME-Rec的R@10分别达到0.0996、0.1105和0.0593，超越最强对比基线7.0%-12.0%。三专家融合（无历史校准）持续优于校准后的SASRec，表明校准本身不能解释恢复效果。随机打乱物品文本嵌入与物品ID的对应关系使R@10降低13.6%-17.5%，证明提升依赖于真实的物品文本对应关系而非额外表征容量。

Conclusion: 在将性能提升归因于服务时语言建模、语义ID生成或更重的语义机制之前，应首先排除轻量级离线物品表征恢复和透明融合的解释。这些结果质疑了现有语义/生成式推荐系统归因声明的有效性。

Abstract: Recent semantic and generative-retrieval recommenders report substantial improvements over ID-only sequential baselines, but it remains unclear whether these gains arise from language-model reasoning, semantic-ID generation, end-to-end semantic architectures, stronger offline item representations, or complementary semantic and collaborative signals. We investigate this attribution ambiguity through LIME-Rec, a lightweight and auditable recovery test. LIME-Rec combines three independent experts: a SASRec sequential expert, an ItemCF co-occurrence expert, and a semantic expert based on frozen BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5 item embeddings. Their full-catalog scores are normalized per user and combined through auditable score-level fusion followed by bounded history calibration. The fusion gate and calibration head are fitted on validation data only, require no serving-time language-model inference, and keep each expert contribution separately inspectable. On Amazon Beauty, Toys, and Sports, LIME-Rec achieves R@10 scores of 0.0996, 0.1105, and 0.0593, outperforming the strongest comparison baseline by 7.0%-12.0%. Three-expert fusion without history calibration consistently outperforms calibrated SASRec, showing that calibration alone does not explain the recovery. Randomly permuting item-text embeddings across item IDs reduces R@10 by 13.6%-17.5%, indicating that the gains depend on genuine item-text correspondence rather than additional representation capacity. These results suggest that lightweight recovery from offline item representations and transparent fusion should be ruled out before improvements are attributed to serving-time language modeling, semantic-ID generation, or heavier semantic machinery.

</details>


### [200] [Collaborative Memory Augmentation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01315)
*Enze Liu,Zhen Tian,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出OMEGA框架，通过协作记忆增强生成式推荐系统，利用可学习查询令牌压缩用户行为为紧凑表示并构建协作记忆库，通过目标感知检索和门控交叉注意力机制融合全局协作信号与本地用户上下文，在多个真实数据集上显著超越现有先进GR模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐框架主要局限于建模个体用户序列，在受限的内部参数空间内运作，未能充分利用跨用户的协作信号，限制了推荐性能的提升空间。

Method: OMEGA包含三个核心模块：(1) 潜在上下文压缩方法，利用可学习查询令牌将序列化用户行为蒸馏为紧凑表示以降低存储开销；(2) 协作记忆库，聚合压缩表示形成全局行为模式的显式存储库；(3) 目标感知检索机制与上下文感知集成模块，通过门控交叉注意力自适应融合检索到的协作记忆与本地用户上下文，同时抑制噪声模式的干扰。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实证评估表明，OMEGA显著优于现有先进的生成式推荐模型，验证了外部记忆作为生成范式补充的有效性。

Conclusion: 外部显式协作记忆能够有效弥合生成式推荐中隐含参数知识与显式协作信号之间的差距，为生成式推荐范式提供了新的增强路径，是提升推荐性能的有效手段。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has exhibited great potential by modeling item transitions as a sequence-to-sequence task. Despite the success of GR, existing frameworks primarily focus on modeling individual user sequences within a constrained internal parametric space, failing to explicitly leverage cross-user collaborative signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{OMEGA}, a cOllaborative MEmory augmentation framework for Generative recommendAtion. OMEGA bridges the gap between implicit parametric knowledge and explicit collaborative signals. We first introduce a latent context compression method that utilizes learnable query tokens to distill sequential user behavior into compact representations, significantly reducing storage overhead. These compressed representations are aggregated into a collaborative memory bank, serving as an explicit repository of global behavioral patterns. To ensure precise knowledge acquisition, we design a lightweight and target-aware retrieval mechanism that identifies pertinent memories by considering both sequence-level and target-level similarities. Furthermore, a context-aware integration module, equipped with a gated cross-attention mechanism, is employed to adaptively fuse the retrieved collaborative memories with the local user context while mitigating the interference of noisy patterns. Empirical evaluations on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that OMEGA significantly outperforms existing advanced GR models, validating the potential of external memory as a complement to the generative paradigm.

</details>


### [201] [Real-Time Hybrid Retrieval in Hyperbolic Space for Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01450)
*Aradhya Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全在双曲几何洛伦兹模型中运行的混合文档检索系统，用于检索增强生成（RAG）。系统通过学习的HyTE-H变换将预训练词嵌入投影到双曲空间，采用BM25词法评分与洛伦兹内积相似度混合的两阶段检索管道，在BEIR基准的五个数据集上仅用词嵌入即取得有竞争力的NDCG@10成绩，且支持边缘设备上的实时索引和交互延迟查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集检索器受限于欧几里得空间，无法充分利用自然语言固有的层次化组织结构。双曲空间具有指数体积增长特性，更契合语言层级结构，因此作者希望探索在双曲几何中构建端到端的文档检索系统。

Method: 系统通过学习的HyTE-H变换将预训练词嵌入投影到洛伦兹模型的双曲空间，文档被切分为重叠块并以其洛伦兹嵌入建立索引。检索采用两阶段管道：第一阶段用BM25词法评分筛选候选，第二阶段用洛伦兹内积相似度重新排序。可调参数α用于混合BM25分数与双曲相似度分数。

Result: 在BEIR基准的五个数据集（SciFact、NFCorpus、ArguAna、SciDocs、FiQA）上，系统分别取得0.654、0.304、0.342、0.150和0.217的NDCG@10分数，且仅使用词嵌入，无需微调神经编码器或交叉注意力重排序器。系统支持实时索引和边缘设备上的交互延迟查询。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了纯双曲几何检索的可行性，在不依赖大规模微调模型的条件下即可在多个基准上取得有竞争力的效果，同时具备资源高效性和边缘设备部署能力，为RAG系统在低资源环境中的应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid document retrieval system designed for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that operates entirely within the Lorentz model of hyperbolic geometry. Unlike conventional dense retrievers confined to Euclidean space, this system projects pretrained word embeddings into hyperbolic space through a learned HyTE-H transformation, whose exponential volume growth suits the hierarchical organization of natural language. Documents are segmented into overlapping chunks, indexed by their Lorentz embeddings, and retrieved through a two-stage pipeline that first applies BM25 lexical scoring, then re-ranks candidates using Lorentzian inner-product similarity. A tunable parameter $α$ blends the BM25 score with the hyperbolic similarity score. The system was evaluated on five datasets from the BEIR benchmark suite, SciFact, NFCorpus, ArguAna, SciDocs, and FiQA, achieving NDCG@10 scores of 0.654, 0.304, 0.342, 0.150, and 0.217 respectively with word embeddings alone, without fine-tuned neural encoders or cross-attention rerankers.
