The system supports real-time indexing of user-supplied documents and resource-efficient querying over tens of thousands of moderately sized documents, so hyperbolic retrieval can run on edge devices at interactive latencies.

</details>


### [202] [Floor, Ceiling, and the Fusion Gap: How Much of Crowd Reading Attention Can Machines Predict?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01704)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个稀有任务（众包读者对网页文档中句子的高亮预测）的性能下界（朴素截断）和上界（分裂半数预言机），发现上下界之间存在0.2028 AP的语义差距。前缘语言模型零样本可达35-53%的差距，而五种前缘模型的跨厂商融合加上位置先验可达60%，其增益在多项消融和预注册复制中均得到验证。将该融合蒸馏到80亿参数的开源模型中可保留90%的边缘优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数若无下界（平凡方法）和上界（理论最优）的参照则毫无意义。本文旨在为一个罕见的众包高亮预测任务同时建立这两个边界，以结构化理解性能差距的构成，并评估当前模型的真实水平。

Method: 以朴素截断作为性能下限，以分裂半数预言机（半群众预测另一半）作为性能上限；通过特征分析（位置和长度）分解差距的语义来源；系统评估零样本前缘语言模型、经典基线和提示压缩器；构建跨厂商五种前缘模型排名的无加权融合并加位置先验；将该融合蒸馏为8B开源学生模型进行压缩验证。

Result: 上下界差距为+0.2028 AP；位置和长度特征仅能恢复5%的差距（表明差距本质上是语义性的）；前缘语言模型零样本达到35-53%差距，LLMLingua-2低于下界；跨厂商融合达到60%差距，优于最强单模型+0.0159 AP，该增益通过了多项消融和预注册复制确认；8B蒸馏学生保留90%融合优势，与最强单模型达到统计持平，而局部上下文学生仅保留63%。

Conclusion: 众包高亮信号主要存在于文档级结构而非局部上下文；目前已知最廉价的改进方法是融合多个不同厂商的模型并取平均；即使压缩到80亿参数，融合信号的大部分仍可保留，这为实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A benchmark score means nothing without knowing what a trivial method achieves and what the best possible method could achieve. We construct both bounds for a task with a rare kind of ground truth: predicting which sentences a crowd of readers -- highlighting for their own purposes, unpaid, uninstructed, and blind to each other -- marked in 120 web documents. The floor is naive truncation (lead); the ceiling is a split-half oracle: half the crowd predicting the other half. The gap between them is +0.2028 AP [+0.1698, +0.2342, domain-clustered], and three findings structure it. First, the gap is semantic: position and length features recover 5% of it. Second, frontier language models reach 35-53% of it zero-shot -- far above classical baselines, far below the crowd; a state-of-the-art prompt compressor (LLMLingua-2) lands below the floor, indistinguishable from random selection. Third, an unweighted cross-vendor fusion of five frontier rankings plus a position prior reaches 60%, beating the best single model by +0.0159 [+0.0044, +0.0269; Holm p=0.019] -- a gain that survives ablation of its best member, split-half arm selection, prompt paraphrase, and label, gate, and seed perturbations, and was CONFIRMED by a pre-registered replication on 217 independent documents (+0.0179, Holm p=0.042). Finally, the bracket compresses: distilling the fusion into one open-weight 8B student that reads the whole document retains 90% of the fusion's edge and reaches statistical parity with the strongest single frontier model (+0.0070 [-0.0068, +0.0200]), where a local-context student retains only 63% -- the crowd's signal lives in document-level structure, and the cheapest known improvement is to ask several different models and average.

</details>


### [203] [MODE: Mutual Optimality in Direct Effects of Reciprocal Recommendations in Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01731)
*Yoji Tomita*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MODE方法，用于匹配平台（如招聘和婚恋平台）的双向推荐系统，在考虑双方用户偏好和避免机会过度集中的同时，保证单个用户推荐列表的"直接效应最优性"，实现互惠最优推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配平台（如招聘和婚恋平台）需要设计合适的双向推荐系统（RRSs），既要考虑双方用户偏好、防止机会过度集中在少数热门用户上，又不能因为过度追求集中度缓解而损害个别用户的推荐质量，导致用户不满。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两方面。

Method: 首先形式化定义了在给定其他用户推荐的情况下，单个用户推荐列表的"直接效应最优性"概念；在此基础上提出MODE方法，计算在所有用户间实现直接效应上互惠最优的推荐结果。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，MODE在直接效应互惠最优性方面优于现有方法，处理速度更快，且能实现更高的预期匹配数量。

Conclusion: MODE方法能够在匹配平台双向推荐系统中同时实现用户个体推荐质量与整体公平性（避免机会过度集中）之间的平衡，并通过更快的计算速度和更高的匹配效率验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Matching platforms such as job posting services and online dating platforms have become widely used over the past decade. For a matching platform to be successful, it is crucial to design appropriate reciprocal recommendation systems (RRSs) that consider the preferences of users on both sides (job candidates and employers) and prevent opportunities from being concentrated too heavily on a few popular users. However, prioritizing concentration mitigation too much can lead to recommending undesirable results to some individual users, resulting in their dissatisfaction. In this paper, we formulate the concept of ``optimality of direct effects'' of the recommendation list for an individual user, given the recommendations to other users. Furthermore, we propose a novel method, MODE, that computes mutually optimal recommendations in direct effects. Experiments with synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MODE surpasses other existing methods in terms of mutual optimality of direct effects, exhibits faster processing speeds, and enables a higher expected number of matches.

</details>


### [204] [X-KGRank: A Knowledge Graph RAG Framework for Explainable Recommendations via Pattern Mining and LLM Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01732)
*Meenakshi Rajpurohit,Jainish Patel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为X-KGRank的框架，将知识图谱检索增强与协作过滤和LLM解释相结合，用于可解释的推荐系统。在MovieLens-1M数据集上构建异构知识图谱，使用LightGCN排序器和SBERT初始化进行训练，并采用流行度选择性路由策略来平衡长尾物品和流行物品的生成开销。实验结果显示在NDCG@10、Recall@10等指标上显著优于流行度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统产生的预测用户无法质询。协作过滤能捕获行为信号但无法提供推理过程，而LLM虽然能生成流畅的解释但会产生幻觉且缺乏用户历史数据的良好支撑。两种主流方法各有缺陷，需要一种能够统一两者优势的方法。

Method: 1) 从MovieLens-1M数据集构建包含9,762个节点和999,264条边的异构知识图谱（三种关系类型：RATED、HAS_GENRE、CO_RATED），存储在Neo4j中。2) 使用LightGCN排序器，配合SBERT内容感知初始化和加权BPR目标函数进行训练。3) 采用流行度选择性路由策略，对长尾物品（1,855个）通过知识图谱路径生成解释，对流行物品使用预训练知识，减少约50%的KG增强生成。

Result: 在MovieLens-1M测试集上（99样本协议），NDCG@10=0.2956，Recall@10=0.5371，较流行度基线在两个指标上提升17.1%；NDCG@20提升15.6%（0.3449 vs 0.2983）；MRR提升14.6%（0.2435 vs 0.2124）。跨三个LLM后端评估16个案例，Qwen2.5-1.5B与Mistral-7B在启发式解释质量上相当（0.97 vs 0.94），但较小模型更易产生事实性捏造。

Conclusion: X-KGRank通过知识图谱检索增强框架有效统一了结构化协作过滤与LLM解释推理的优势，在推荐准确性和解释质量上均取得了显著改进。但研究也揭示了一个重要发现：虽然小规模LLM在评分上能与大规模模型匹敌，但其定性分析显示更易产生事实性捏造，说明仅依赖启发式评估指标可能不够充分。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems produce predictions that users cannot interrogate. The two dominant improvements, collaborative filtering and LLM-based reasoning, each fall short: collaborative filtering captures behavioural signals but offers no reasoning, while large language models (LLMs) generate fluent explanations but hallucinate and are poorly grounded in a user's history. We present X-KGRank, a knowledge graph retrieval augmented framework that unifies structural collaborative filtering with LLM-based explanation. From the MovieLens-1M dataset (6,040 users, 3,704 items, 988,129 interactions) we construct a heterogeneous knowledge graph of 9,762 nodes and 999,264 edges spanning three relation types (RATED, HAS_GENRE, and CO_RATED) persisted in Neo4j. We train a LightGCN ranker with content-aware SBERT initialization and a rating weighted BPR objective, and apply a popularity selective routing strategy that grounds long-tail items (1,855 of 3,704) in knowledge-graph paths while serving popular items from pre-trained knowledge, reducing KG-augmented generations by roughly 50%. On the MovieLens-1M test set under a 99-sample protocol, X-KGRank achieves NDCG@10 = 0.2956 and Recall@10 = 0.5371, improving over a strong popularity baseline by 17.1% on both metrics, by 15.6% on NDCG@20 (0.3449 vs. 0.2983), and by 14.6% on MRR (0.2435 vs. 0.2124). Across three LLM backbones evaluated on 16 cases, a 1.5-billion-parameter model (Qwen2.5-1.5B) matches a 7-billion-parameter model (Mistral-7B) on heuristic explanation quality (0.97 vs. 0.94), yet qualitative analysis shows the smaller model is more prone to factual fabrication.

</details>


### [205] [SPEAR: Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval for Community Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01738)
*Wenbin Wu,Yuzhong Wu,Yufan Xu,Kuan Fang,Xing Xu,Cheng Ye,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR框架，解决电商搜索中查询改写与检索两阶段结构性错配的问题。通过双嵌入骨干、乘法门控聚合器和动态改写选择器三个组件，分别解决语义侵蚀、通用词捷径和偏好适配三个失败模式，在多电商平台离线评估和在线A/B测试中均显著提升改写质量与检索效果，已全面部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索中查询改写和检索分别优化导致两阶段结构错配；基于路径的端到端架构虽能统一二者，但其目标是个性化而非检索相关性约束，直接移植会导致模型学习"通用词支配效应"捷径——偏向通用改写而在路径上得分高却偏离用户查询意图，需要从根本上解决这一结构性错位。

Method: 提出SPEAR框架，包含三个组件：(1) 双嵌入骨干网络，通过辅助损失和梯度隔离保护召回侧语义不被CTR驱动的排序信号侵蚀；(2) 乘法门控聚合器，仅在改写置信度和商品相关性都强时给予高分，消除通用词捷径；(3) 动态改写选择器，联合生成请求特定改写权重及用户查询条件化的尺度和偏置项，使改写偏好和相关性校准适应每个请求。

Result: 在10万条工业搜索会话的离线评估中，改写语义相似度@10提升+18.2，点击召回@10提升+99.5。在线A/B测试中，查询视图CTR提升+0.259，平均阅读深度提升+0.733，证明改写选择改进可转化为更强的检索和更深的用户参与。

Conclusion: SPEAR通过三个针对性组件有效解决了端到端路径架构在电商搜索中的结构性错配问题，消除通用词捷径的同时保持了改写的查询意图忠实度，已在电商社区搜索平台2025年全面部署，代码已开源。

Abstract: Query reformulation bridges user intent and retrieval in e-commerce search, yet production systems optimize rewrite quality and retrieval effectiveness separately, leaving the two stages structurally misaligned. Path-based architectures unify them end-to-end but were designed for personalization, where relevance is not an explicit constraint-search additionally requires the rewrite to remain faithful to the user's stated query intent. Transplanted directly, these models learn a shortcut we term the generic-word dominance effect: they favor generic rewrites that score well on paths but drift from query intent. To address this, we propose SPEAR (Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval), which integrates three components that each target one failure mode: (1) a dual-embedding backbone with auxiliary loss and gradient isolation that shields recall-side semantics from being eroded by CTR-driven ranking signals; (2) a multiplicative gating aggregator that lets a rewrite score high only when both its confidence and item relevance are strong, eliminating the generic-word shortcut; (3) a Dynamic Rewrite Selector that jointly generates request-specific rewrite weights and user-query-conditioned scale and bias terms, allowing both rewrite preference and relevance calibration to adapt to each request. Offline evaluation on 100K held-out industrial search sessions shows that the proposed framework improves rewrite semantic similarity@10 by +18.2 and click recall@10 by +99.5 over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SPEAR achieves +0.259 in query-view CTR and +0.733 in average reading depth, confirming that improved rewrite selection translates into stronger retrieval and deeper user engagement. The proposed SPEAR system has been fully deployed in Dewu's community search platform since 2025. Our code is available at https://github.com/mallocagi1-cell/spear.

</details>


### [206] [HyperAgent4POI: Dynamic Semantic Message Passing on Multi-Agent Hypergraphs for Missing-Modality Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01846)
*Jinze Wang,Yuze Liu,Tiehua Zhang,Jiong Jin,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent4POI，一种用于POI推荐的方法，通过动态语义消息传递（DSMP）在超图各层内完成模态补全和软关联精化，解决缺失模态问题，并在三个真实LBSN数据集上超越15个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POI推荐依赖文本和视觉等语义内容，但这些模态在真实场景中常不完整。缺失模态会削弱POI表示，降低排序所需的语义证据，并影响高阶用户-POI交互建模的可靠性。

Method: 提出HyperAgent4POI框架，核心是DSMP模块：持久化节点代理共享冻结的Llama骨干网络，使用角色特定的适配器生成节点到超边的消息；由这些消息形成的语义超边模体用于指导软关联评分和模态补全；最终节点表示被缓存用于在线排序，无需LLM调用。

Result: 在三个真实LBSN数据集上，HyperAgent4POI在多种模态缺失率下均取得一致的排序性能提升，超越15个基线。在60%模态缺失率下，NDCG@20平均提升8.2%。缓存推理机制保证了实际的在线推理效率。

Conclusion: HyperAgent4POI通过超图内动态语义消息传递有效应对模态缺失问题，同时兼顾了模型性能和在线推理效率，为POI推荐中的多模态不完整性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation benefits from textual and visual content that describes venue semantics, yet such content is often incomplete in real-world services. Missing modalities weaken POI representations and reduce the semantic evidence available for ranking. The resulting representations also provide unreliable evidence for modeling higher-order user--POI interactions. We propose HyperAgent4POI, which uses Dynamic Semantic Message Passing (DSMP) to perform modality completion and soft incidence refinement within each hypergraph layer. Persistent node agents share a frozen Llama backbone and use role-specific adapters to produce node-to-hyperedge messages. Semantic hyperedge motifs formed from these messages guide soft incidence scoring and modality completion. Final node representations are cached for online ranking without LLM calls. Experiments on three real-world LBSN datasets show consistent ranking gains over 15 baselines across modality-missing rates, while cached inference provides practical online efficiency. Under a 60% modality-missing rate, HyperAgent4POI improves NDCG@20 over the strongest baseline by 8.2% on average across the three datasets.

</details>


### [207] [A Self-Triggered Agentic Push Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01949)
*Zhao-Yu Zhang,Qingying Chen,Chunyuan Zheng,Jing Zhou,Jian Sun,Siqi Chen,Leiying Chen,Chuan Zhou,Huiyou Jiang,Xin Tao,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了STEPS，一个自触发端到端智能推送推荐系统，已在抖音大规模部署（超10亿用户）。STEPS将推送推荐重构为自触发的智能体过程，系统不仅决定是否发送推送，还决定何时再次触发自身，形成实时性与效率兼顾的闭环。系统由规划智能体（调度下次系统调用）、执行智能体（决定是否发送推送）和轻量级过滤智能体（控制计算开销和防止不合理规划）组成。在线A/B测试显示用户活跃天数提升0.2843%，推送权限关闭率降低1.9089%，计算开销降低79.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推送系统存在两类被动范式：预规划频率方法（离线建模分配时间，缺乏实时适应性）和固定间隔触发方法（周期性轮询，在计算开销与最优时机捕捉之间存在严格两难）。此外，多阶段框架严重受限于局部最优解。因此需要一种既实时又高效的主动式推送系统。

Method: STEPS将推送推荐建模为自触发智能体过程，由一个规划智能体（基于决策Transformer，使用门控序数回归预测下一次系统调用时间）和一个执行智能体（基于轨迹奖励决定是否发送推送）组成，并引入轻量级过滤智能体控制计算开销并作为规划行为的保护机制。整个系统形成端到端闭环。

Result: 在线A/B测试显示STEPS显著提升用户活跃天数0.2843%，推送权限关闭率降低1.9089%，过滤智能体使计算开销降低79.42%。STEPS已在抖音全面部署。

Conclusion: STEPS通过自触发机制成功解决了推送系统中实时性与效率的矛盾，克服了多阶段框架的局部最优问题，并在超大规模平台（10亿+用户）上验证了其有效性，为推送推荐系统提供了一种新的主动式智能体范式。

Abstract: Push notification is a critical recommendation scenario on large-scale platforms, allowing the system to proactively reach users outside the application to improve long-term re-engagement. However, designing an optimal push system requires handling a complex action space for the "whether and when" delivery problem under strict system resource constraints. Existing solutions typically fall into two passive paradigms: pre-planned frequency methods that allocate delivery times via offline modeling, limiting real-time adaptability; and fixed-interval triggering methods that periodically poll the system, creating a strict dilemma between excessive computational overhead and diminished optimal timing capture. Furthermore, such multi-stage frameworks severely suffer from local optima. To overcome these limitations, in this paper, we propose STEPS, a proactive, Self-Triggered End-to-end Agentic Push Recommendation System, which is already fully deployed at Douyin with over 1 billion users. STEPS reformulates push recommendation as a self-triggered agentic process in which the system decides not only whether to send a push, but also when to invoke itself again, thereby forming a closed loop that balances real-time effectiveness and efficiency. Specifically, STEPS consists of two decision transformer-based agents: a planning agent that schedules the next system invocation using a gated ordinal regression method, and an execution agent that decides whether to send a push based on trajectory rewards. Furthermore, we introduce a lightweight filtering agent to both control computational overhead and act as a crucial safeguard against unreasonable planning behaviors. Online A/B testing demonstrates that STEPS significantly increases user active days by 0.2843% and reduces the push permission disablement rate by 1.9089%, while the filtering agent reduces computational overhead by 79.42%.

</details>


### [208] [SmartGR: Hierarchy and Beam-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02048)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yu Cui,Bohao Wang,Yong He,Chao Yu,Chuan Yuan,Wujie Sun,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SmartGR是一个针对生成式推荐系统的知识蒸馏框架，解决了现有蒸馏方法未考虑的两个GR特有问题：语义ID层级上的蒸馏难度不均衡，以及束搜索过程中的错误前缀剪枝。通过层级感知的SID蒸馏和束感知的排序蒸馏，在四个基准数据集上平均提升8.6%的性能并实现2.39倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型规模增大虽能提升性能，但推理成本也大幅增加。知识蒸馏是解决方案，但现有蒸馏方法未考虑GR特有的两个挑战：语义ID层级上蒸馏难度不均衡，以及束搜索中错误前缀剪枝问题。

Method: 提出SmartGR蒸馏框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation，跨语义ID层级传递教师的建模能力；2) Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation，在束搜索过程中蒸馏教师的排序偏好。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SmartGR平均提升推荐性能8.6%，同时实现2.39倍的推理加速，验证了其有效性和效率。

Conclusion: SmartGR通过针对GR特有挑战设计的知识蒸馏方法，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升轻量级GR模型的推荐性能，为生成式推荐模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for recommender systems. Scaling up GR models can improve recommendation performance, but it also substantially increases inference cost. Knowledge distillation provides a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a large GR model to a lightweight one. However, existing distillation methods do not account for two GR-specific challenges: imbalanced distillation difficulty across the semantic ID (SID) hierarchy and incorrect prefix pruning during beam search. To address these challenges, we propose SmartGR, a novel distillation framework that utilizes Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation to transfer the teacher's modeling capability across the hierarchy and leverages Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation to distill the teacher's ranking preferences during beam search. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SmartGR, improving the performance by 8.6% while achieving a 2.39$\times$ inference speedup on average.

</details>


### [209] [Douyin Multimodal Embedding Model Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02148)
*Haonan Chen,Chu Li,Zhicheng Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Yuanjiang Wang,Shaohua Jiang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DME是一种两阶段训练的多模态嵌入模型，结合了对比学习的效率和CoT式推理的细粒度判别能力，在MMEB-v2上达到SOTA，并在抖音生产环境中取得显著效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM嵌入模型难以同时满足大规模索引下的高效性和细粒度判别需求：对比模型效率高但依赖粗粒度的配对级监督，CoT模型判别力强但生成开销大、不便于在线服务。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段进行大规模对比预训练以建立统一的多模态嵌入空间；第二阶段通过两种机制补充语义充分性——证据引导的隐式类型化潜在推理（Evidence-Grounded Typed Latent Reasoning）和跨条件重建（Cross-Conditional Reconstruction），后者通过跨方向自回归重建强化对侧语义。两种机制仅在训练时生效，推理时仅有少量query侧开销。

Result: 在MMEB-v2上，DME的2B和9B变体分别达到74.8和78.4的SOTA结果，尤其在视频和视觉文档任务上表现突出。生产环境中，官方离线评估集上相对提升2.92%，已在抖音的生成式、图像和AI搜索等场景部署，在线A/B测试中搜索LT提升0.1%。

Conclusion: DME通过两阶段训练成功兼顾了大规模检索的效率和细粒度匹配的精度，验证了潜在推理机制可以在不牺牲在线服务效率的前提下提升嵌入的语义充分性，为工业级多模态嵌入提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning is a cornerstone of modern AI. By encoding multimodal queries and targets into vectors, it powers industrial search and recommendation and underpins modern agents. Real-world platforms with complex modalities and massive-scale content, such as Douyin, Xiaohongshu, and YouTube, demand both efficiency under billion-scale indexing and fine-grained discrimination for hard matching. Existing MLLM embedding models rarely satisfy both. Contrastive models are efficient but rely on pair-level supervision too coarse for fine-grained distinctions, while CoT-based models improve discrimination through explicit generation impractical to serve online. We present Douyin Multimodal Embedding (DME), a model trained in two stages to combine both strengths. Stage 1 performs large-scale contrastive pre-training that establishes a unified multimodal embedding space with broad modality and task coverage. Stage 2 supplements semantic sufficiency, the property that an embedding is grounded in retrieval-relevant evidence and preserves fine-grained counterpart-side semantics, via two mechanisms. Evidence-Grounded Typed Latent Reasoning organizes retrieval evidence through hidden-space latent reasoning, and Cross-Conditional Reconstruction enforces counterpart-side semantics through cross-directional autoregressive reconstruction. Both act only during training and add only marginal query-side overhead, so DME serves as efficiently as a standard contrastive encoder. On MMEB-v2, DME reaches state-of-the-art results at comparable scales for its 2B and 9B variants (74.8 and 78.4), with especially strong video and visual-document tasks. In production, DME delivers a 2.92% relative gain on Douyin's in-house offline evaluation set, is deployed across Douyin scenarios such as generative, image, and AI search, and yields a 0.1% Lifetime (LT) gain in online A/B testing on Douyin search.

</details>


### [210] [Disentangled Contrastive Learning for Zero-Shot Multilingual Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02189)
*Chao Huang,Yufeng Chen,Changhao Guan,Guang Yang,Dongze Chen,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多语言稠密检索的解耦对比学习方法（DCL），通过将多语言表示分离为语义子空间和语言子空间，减少语言特征对语义匹配的干扰，实现从英语监督到低资源语言的稳定零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言稠密检索中，现有的共享表示方法常将语义特征和语言特征纠缠在一起，这会干扰语义相关性的优化，尤其在低资源语言上缺乏标注数据时问题更为突出。已有方法多关注在这种纠缠状态下学习语言无关的语义特征，而非真正分离两者。

Method: 提出解耦对比学习（DCL）方法，将多语言表示分离为语义和语言两个子空间。设计基于层次语义对齐和语言去偏对比学习的解耦优化目标：在句子级和词元级对齐跨语言的检索相关语义，同时在语言子空间中捕获语言特定的变化，以减少语言诱导的干扰。联合检索目标共同优化以实现稳定的零样本迁移。

Result: 在mMARCO和MIRACL基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在多个强基线上持续取得更优表现，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离语义与语言子空间并进行解耦优化，可以有效提升多语言稠密检索的零样本迁移性能，为低资源语言检索提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Multilingual dense retrieval aims to handle queries and documents across different languages based on a unified retriever model. The challenge lies in enabling robust retrieval transfer to low-resource languages where annotated retrieval data is often scarce. Although previous studies transfer high-resource supervision to low-resource languages in multilingual semantic representation learning, the shared representation often entangles semantic and linguistic features, which may interfere with optimizing semantic relevance for retrieval. Different from existing methods that focus on learning language-agnostic semantic features under such entanglement, we propose a disentangled contrastive learning~(DCL) method for multilingual dense retrieval by separating multilingual representations into semantic and linguistic subspaces. Specifically, we design disentangled optimization objectives based on hierarchical semantic alignment and language debiasing contrastive learning. By aligning retrieval-relevant semantics across languages at both sentence and token levels while capturing language-specific variations in the linguistic subspace, these objectives reduce language-induced interference in semantic matching. We jointly optimize them with the retrieval objective to facilitate stable zero-shot transfer from English supervision to multilingual dense retrieval. Extensive experiments on mMARCO and MIRACL show that our method consistently outperforms several strong baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalization ability.

</details>


### [211] [Advancing Relevance Measurement with Vision-Language Models for Web-Scale Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02446)
*Han Wang,Alex Whitworth,Pak Ming Cheung,Zhenjie Zhang,Krishna Kamath,Xi Chen,Roberto Konow,Kurchi Subhra Hazra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Pinterest搜索中基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化相关性评估流水线，用于在线A/B实验。该方案验证了VLM判断与人工标注的一致性，在提升评估效率的同时显著降低了在线实验中的最小可检测效应（MDE），并支持更大规模的查询集扩展和采样优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人工标注方法在相关性评估中成本高昂且周期长，限制了其可扩展性，亟需一种高效且可靠的自动化评估方案来支撑个性化搜索系统中的在线实验。

Method: 构建了基于VLM的自动化相关性评估流水线，部署于Pinterest搜索的在线A/B实验中，通过严格验证VLM生成判断与人工标注之间的一致性来确保评估的可靠性，并利用VLM标注能力扩展查询集、优化采样设计。

Result: VLM能够提供与人工标注高度一致的相关性评估，大幅提升评估效率，带来更高质量的相关性指标，并显著降低在线实验测量中的最小可检测效应（MDEs）。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的自动化评估方案是人工标注的有效替代，能够在大规模个性化搜索场景中实现高效、可靠的在线实验结果评估，为搜索体验优化提供了更大规模的可评估空间。

Abstract: Relevance evaluation plays a crucial role in personalized search systems, serving as a guardrail alongside user engagement metrics to ensure that search results align with user queries and intent. While human annotation is the traditional method for relevance evaluation, its high cost and long turnaround time limit its scalability. In this work, we present a VLM-based automated relevance evaluation pipeline deployed within Pinterest Search for online A/B experiments. We rigorously validate the alignment between VLM-generated judgments and human annotations, demonstrating that VLMs can provide reliable relevance measurement for experiments while greatly improving the evaluation efficiency. Leveraging VLM-based labeling further unlocks opportunities to expand the query set, optimize sampling design, and efficiently assess a wider range of search experiences at scale. This approach leads to higher-quality relevance metrics and significantly reduces the Minimum Detectable Effects (MDEs) in online experiment measurements.

</details>


### [212] [Syntax Meets Semantics: Understanding Scientific Formulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02457)
*Yuni Susanti,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了科学公式的语法与语义两种模态之间的表征对应关系，发现它们的原生表征空间之间几乎不存在可观测的对应关系，但存在强潜在相关性。通过对比学习将基于图的语法编码器和基于文本的语义编码器对齐到共享表征空间后，跨模态检索性能得到显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学公式在学术交流中具有语法结构和语义的双重属性，但以往研究虽然表明联合建模语法和语义模态能提升检索性能，却未系统探究这两种模态底层表征之间的关系。

Method: 使用基于图的编码器表示公式的语法结构，使用基于文本的编码器表示语义信息，然后应用对比学习来诱导一个共享的表征空间，以评估表征不一致是否可以通过标准的表征学习和对齐技术得到缓解。

Result: 公式语法与语义的原生表征空间之间表现出极弱的可观测对应关系，但存在强潜在相关性；经过对比学习对齐后，跨模态检索性能获得显著提升，表明显式的表征学习能够恢复原生表征空间中缺失的对应关系。

Conclusion: 语法与语义模态之间确实存在显著的表征不匹配问题，但这一不匹配可以通过显式的表征学习和对齐技术得到有效解决，从而显著改善跨模态的学术信息检索性能。

Abstract: Scientific formulae are a fundamental component of scholarly communication, yet their dual nature -- as structured syntax and carriers of semantics -- remains underexplored in scholarly information retrieval. Although prior studies show that jointly modeling syntactic and semantic modalities improves retrieval performance, the relationship between their underlying representations has not been systematically investigated. In this work, we empirically study cross-modal correspondence between formula syntax and semantics. We find that their native representation spaces exhibit extremely weak observable correspondence despite strong latent correlation, indicating a substantial representation mismatch between the two modalities. We further evaluate whether this mismatch can be reduced using standard representation learning and alignment techniques. We represent syntactic structure using graph-based encoders and semantic information using text-based encoders, then apply contrastive learning to induce a shared representation space. Results show that the learned alignment substantially improves cross-modal retrieval, suggesting that explicit representation learning can recover correspondence absent from the original representation spaces.

</details>


### [213] [Unpaired Modality-Agnostic Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02477)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Yuqin Lan,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UnpairGR提出了一种统一的模态无关生成式推荐方法，能够在配对、仅图像和仅文本的混合观测数据上学习统一的语义ID空间，无需特征填补或模态专属码本，在完全观测和不完全观测场景下均提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成式推荐方法通常需要条目级别的配对观测数据，这限制了语义ID的构建只能在模态重叠的数据子集上进行；同时，加入非配对观测数据会导致表示偏移跨越量化边界，产生不兼容的标识符序列，这是一个难以解决的挑战。

Method: UnpairGR将模态专属处理限制在轻量级输入投影层中，共享后续的Transformer和残差码本；配对观测通过可靠性引导建立跨模态共识，单模态观测直接优化相同的表示和编码；学习完成后的分词器被固定，为单一的自回归推荐器提供稳定的目标。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，UnpairGR在完全观测和不完全观测两种设置下均持续提升了推荐性能。

Conclusion: UnpairGR通过模态无关的统一语义ID空间设计，有效利用了非配对的多模态观测数据，避免了特征填补、模态专属码本和回退映射等复杂处理，为多模态生成式推荐提供了更灵活且性能更优的方案。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation over discrete semantic identifiers (IDs). Although recent multimodal GR methods improve semantic ID construction with visual and textual information, they typically require item-level paired observations, restricting tokenization to the intersection of modality availability. Moreover, incorporating unpaired observations is nontrivial because small representation shifts may cross quantization boundaries and produce incompatible identifier sequences. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{Unpair}ed Modality-Agnostic \textbf{G}enerative \textbf{R}ecommendation (UnpairGR), which learns a unified semantic-ID space from paired, image-only, and text-only observations. UnpairGR confines modality-specific processing to lightweight input projections while sharing the subsequent Transformer and residual codebooks across all observation conditions. Paired observations establish a reliability-guided cross-modal consensus, whereas unimodal observations directly refine the same representations and codes. The learned tokenizer is then fixed to provide stationary targets for a single autoregressive recommender, without feature imputation, modality-specific codebooks, or fallback mappings. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that UnpairGR consistently improves recommendation performance under both fully observed and incomplete-observation settings.

</details>


### [214] [Requirement--Evidence Alignment for Compositional E-Commerce Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02500)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Yuqin Lan,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REAlign，一种面向组合式电商查询的需求-证据对齐重排序框架。它显式连接类型化查询需求与可见证据，区分满足/违反/不支持的三种条件状态，构建需求定向对比以暴露失败模式，并通过需求感知的组相对策略优化来优化无重复的部分排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的电商重排序模型将多重查询需求压缩为单一的相关性分数，导致在组合式查询中倾向于将"主题近似但不可行"的产品排在真正满足所有需求的产品之前，无法正确处理多约束需同时成立的场景。

Method: REAlign的核心方法包括：(1) 将查询分解为类型化需求，并显式关联到产品可见证据；(2) 区分满足、违反和不支持三种条件状态；(3) 构造需求定向的对比样本暴露失败模式；(4) 使用需求感知的组相对策略优化（Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization）优化无重复的部分排序；(5) 列表效用函数综合相关性、需求满足度、证据支持、重大违反和输出有效性。

Result: 在两个固定池的电商基准测试上，REAlign在相同训练预算下持续优于强监督基线和策略优化基线，在top-ranked候选中出现更少的违反，且在浅层排序位置上获得更大的提升。消融实验证实了需求建模、证据接地和分解优化的互补价值。

Conclusion: REAlign通过显式的需求-证据对齐和需求感知的优化策略，有效解决了组合式电商查询中多重约束联合满足的问题，为电商检索中的精细约束排序提供了可行的新框架。

Abstract: Compositional e-commerce queries express multiple requirements that must hold jointly, yet existing rerankers collapse these constraints into aggregate relevance and often promote topical near misses over feasible products. In this paper, we introduce REAlign, a novel requirement-evidence-aligned reranking framework that explicitly connects typed query requirements with visible evidence. REAlign distinguishes satisfied, violated, and unsupported conditions, constructs requirement-targeted contrasts that expose failure modes, and optimizes duplicate-free partial rankings through Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization. Its list utility preserves relevance while incorporating requirement satisfaction, evidence support, material violations, and output validity. Experiments on two fixed-pool e-commerce benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong supervised and policy-optimization baselines under matched training budgets, with fewer violations among top-ranked candidates and larger gains at shallow ranks. Controlled ablations confirm the complementary value of requirement modeling, evidence grounding, and decomposed optimization.

</details>


### [215] [Between-User Collapse Under Popularity-Biased Feedback: A Centered-Covariance Theorem and Computable Phase Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02548)
*Sahil Medepalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了流行度偏置的BPR训练如何重塑协同过滤嵌入的用户间几何结构。作者证明在流行度偏置反馈下，均值中心化的用户协方差矩阵收敛到与物品噪声协方差成正比的稳态，导致用户间分布向噪声底线坍缩。论文推导了可计算的相变边界，并在MovieLens-25M上验证了预测方向，但发现该效应在实际部署规模的正则化下很小，不影响推荐质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作主要关注嵌入的未中心化二阶矩，而本文提出需要研究均值中心化的用户协方差，因为后者才真正衡量用户之间的可区分性。理解流行度偏置训练如何影响用户几何结构，对于评估和改善推荐系统的实用性至关重要。

Method: 论文使用均值中心化用户协方差C = (1/n) U^T H U作为分析对象（而非前人工作中的未中心化二阶矩），通过理论分析证明在流行度偏置反馈和静态物品条件下C收敛到与物品噪声协方差Q成正比的稳态。推导出关于训练超参数(α, λ_neg, γ, d)的封闭形式相变边界，区分收缩与扩张区域，并在MovieLens-25M上验证。

Result: 实验表明：在部署规模的正则化下，理论预测的收缩是真实存在且由策略驱动的，但幅度很小，并未反映在任何测量的推荐级指标中。α驱动的各向异性坍缩机制仅在正则化强度损害推荐器时才起作用。基于理论推导的部署期恢复干预并未提升推荐质量。相变边界可以从已训练模型的嵌入、物品交互计数和训练超参数计算，但实验表明部署设置远离强收缩区域。

Conclusion: 虽然理论上流行度偏置会导致用户嵌入向噪声底线坍缩，但在实际部署参数下该效应微小且不影响推荐质量。相变边界可作为实践者检查系统是否处于强坍缩区域的工具，但常规部署设置不需要担心这一效应。

Abstract: We study how popularity-biased BPR training reshapes the between-user geometry of collaborative-filtering embeddings. We work with the mean-centered user covariance $C=\tfrac1n U^\top H U$, the object that measures how distinguishable users are from one another, as opposed to the uncentered second moment used in prior work. We prove that under popularity-biased feedback with stationary items, $C$ converges to a steady state proportional to the item-noise covariance $Q$. Thus between-user spread collapses toward a noise floor. We derive a closed-form, computable phase boundary in the training hyperparameters $(α,λ_{neg},γ,d)$ separating contraction from expansion, and validate both directional predictions on MovieLens-25M. We then examine the limits of the effect. At deployment-scale regularization the predicted contraction is real and policy-driven but small, and it is not reflected in any recommendation-level metric we measured. The $α$-driven anisotropic-collapse mechanism operates only at regularization strengths that degrade the recommender. A deployment-time restoration intervention derived from the theory does not improve recommendation quality. The boundary is computable from a trained model's embeddings, item interaction counts, and training hyperparameters, so a practitioner can check whether a deployed system sits in the strong-collapse regime without simulating the feedback loop. In our experiments the boundary places deployable settings far from that regime.

</details>


### [216] [Beyond the Final Prompt: Measuring the Effect of Within-Conversation Context on AI Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02556)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 这项研究直接检验了在多轮对话评估中，遗漏前文上下文是否会影响AI系统的回答。基于180个英文多轮对话样本，研究发现完整对话上下文与仅用最后一条消息的回答在44.7%的情况下存在实质性差异，而添加压缩前缀可将该比例降至30.8%，但压缩并不能完全替代完整对话上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统评估中，通常将孤立的最终用户消息直接作为查询，忽略了多轮对话中请求可能分布在前面的轮次之中。作者希望直接检验这种被遗漏的对话内上下文是否会实质性改变AI的回答。

Method: 从商业语料库和公开PRISM数据集中采样180个英文多轮对话，固定最终用户消息和回答模型不变，生成三种回答：完整角色标注对话、仅最终消息、最终消息加前置压缩重建（上限160词）。由独立评判模型在随机化标签下评估，主要终点为"可能改变用户行为的实质性差异"而非风格或细节差异，并采用逆概率加权和bootstrap置信区间进行统计分析，另设48个案例的顺序交换重复实验检验可靠性。

Result: 完整对话与仅最终消息的回答在44.7%的案例中存在实质性差异（95% CI 33.8%-56.1%）；完整对话回答在0-4分请求满意度量表上平均高出0.49分（CI 0.32-0.67）。添加压缩前缀后，实质性差异率降至30.8%（CI 20.2%-42.1%），绝对降低13.9个百分点，满意度差距降至0.01分（CI -0.12至0.13）。但仍有约三分之一的回答存在实质性差异。重复实验显示91.7%的一致性和kappa=0.83。

Conclusion: 研究证实省略对话上下文会显著影响AI系统评估结果，前文信息对正确理解用户请求至关重要。压缩前缀在多数情况下能恢复部分上下文信息，但不能完全替代完整对话语境。研究范围限于同一对话内的前序轮次，未涉及跨对话的持久记忆问题，提示评估协议设计需要考虑对话上下文的影响。

Abstract: An isolated final user message is often treated as the query in evaluations of AI systems. In a conversation, however, the actionable request may be distributed across preceding turns. We directly test whether that omitted within-conversation context changes answers. For each of 180 English multi-turn conversations sampled from a governed commercial corpus and the public PRISM dataset, we hold the final user message and requested answer model constant while generating three answers: one from the full role-labelled conversation, one from the final message alone, and one from the final message plus a prefix-only reconstruction capped at 160 words. A separately requested judge model evaluates answers under randomized labels. The prespecified primary endpoint is a material difference that could change what the user does, rather than a difference in style or detail. After inverse-probability weighting to the eligible cohorts, the full-conversation and isolated-final answers differ materially in 44.7% of cases (95% bootstrap CI 33.8% to 56.1%). Full-conversation answers score 0.49 points higher on a 0 to 4 request-satisfaction scale (0.32 to 0.67). Adding the compressed prefix reduces the material-difference rate to 30.8% (20.2% to 42.1%), a 13.9-point reduction (4.9% to 24.1%), and reduces the mean satisfaction gap to 0.01 points (-0.12 to 0.13). Yet compression is not equivalent to the complete dialogue context: almost one third of answers remain materially different. An order-swapped repeat on 48 cases yields 91.7% agreement and kappa = 0.83 for the primary decision. The study concerns preceding turns in the same conversation and does not test persistent memory across separate conversations.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对363个MCP任务进行SFT+RL两阶段后训练，训练了一个开源MoE模型（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B），在多个外部基准（Toolathlon、τ²-Bench、BFCL-V4、SWE-Bench Pro、Terminal-Bench 2）上均获得显著提升，表明跨基准迁移可验证智能体的通用能力而非环境特定投机取巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出自包含环境中RL策略可能通过利用环境特定规律（工具模式、解析器、任务模板）获取奖励而非真正的可迁移技能，分布内留出集无法区分这种投机行为。作者主张应以行为学视角（即训练后智能体如何行动）来判断，并认为跨基准迁移是验证的关键场所。

Method: 使用开放权重MoE模型（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B），在27个类别共363个长时域MCP任务上进行两阶段SFT-then-RL流水线后训练。训练过程中不引入任何外部基准任务或评分器，也不使用外部分数指导奖励、超参数、检查点选择或停止条件。随后在五个外部基准上评估贪婪pass@1性能，并进行配对轨迹分析。

Result: 训练模型在五个外部基准上均优于基座模型：Toolathlon +9.6pp、τ²-Bench +5.3pp、BFCL-V4 +3.5pp、SWE-Bench Pro +5.8pp、Terminal-Bench 2 +2.8pp。尽管训练集不含软件工程任务，两个软件工程基准仍获得提升。轨迹分析识别出四种可迁移的行为差异：更谨慎的局部目标形成、构建目标相关的工作状态、通过局部修复保持父目标稳定、以及验证完成情况。

Conclusion: 长时域多工具后训练可以改变智能体的工作方式，且这些行为改变能够迁移到训练域之外的场景。这为论证跨基准迁移是评估RL智能体是否获得真正可迁移技能的关键方法提供了描述性证据。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [218] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用预训练Transformer模型CodeBERT进行源代码作者归属（authorship attribution）的研究。在公开的Google Code Jam竞赛数据上，该模型达到92.6%的Top-1准确率（10位作者）和70.7%的Top-1（1000位作者）；但在大学课程作业数据上，同一流程的准确率仅与随机基线相当（0.2%和0.06%），表明GCJ基准高估了该任务在教育场景中的实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码作者归属旨在通过编程风格识别程序片段的作者。现有研究多基于Google Code Jam竞赛数据评估模型性能，但该评估范式能否迁移到教育场景（如课程作业抄袭检测）缺乏验证，需要检验预训练模型在真实教学环境中的适用性。

Method: 使用预训练Transformer模型CodeBERT，在三类数据源上进行微调：(1)公开的Kaggle GCJ提交数据；(2)精挑细选的GCJ归档数据；(3)某技术大学收集的课程作业数据集。此外还构建了一个多模型基准，在多种数据集配置下比较不同模型家族的跨模型表现。

Result: 在GCJ数据上，CodeBERT在10位作者场景达到92.6% Top-1准确率，1000位作者时仍保持70.7% Top-1（88.2% Top-10）。但在课程作业数据集上性能急剧下降：封闭式作业（690位作者）仅0.2% Top-1，开放式作业（812位作者）仅0.06% Top-1，与随机基线持平。多模型基准进一步验证了这一差异并非CodeBERT特有，而是跨模型族普遍存在。

Conclusion: GCJ基准显著高估了代码作者归属在教育环境中的实际效果。除非在目标课程作业场景中进行针对性验证，否则基于GCJ的评估结果不能可靠反映该任务在真实教育场景中的实用性。研究分析了造成这一差距的数据集与任务属性，为后续研究提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [219] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DroidGraph是一个结合静态分析与高效系统性探索测试的框架，用于生成Android应用的全面控制流模型，支持自动化测试生成。在19个应用上的实验表明，相比随机探索，该方法能以更少的交互覆盖更多应用内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用开发节奏快、发布频繁，需要自动化测试生成。基于模型的测试生成是最常见有效的方法，但Android平台的基于事件的特性和多样化的功能实现机制给静态分析带来困难，而动态随机测试生成器效率低且缺乏应用知识。

Method: 提出DroidGraph框架，使用传统静态分析与高效系统性探索测试相结合的方式，生成Android应用的全面控制流模型。模型涵盖从底层方法语句到高层用户界面结构的详细信息，可支撑自动化测试生成。

Result: 在19个多样化应用上的实验显示，DroidGraph的高效探索测试平均比常用随机探索多交互18%的应用内容，且少用345次交互。动态分析结果补充静态分析，平均多发现51个组件和49%更多的接口回调链接。

Conclusion: DroidGraph通过静态与动态分析相结合的方式，能够生成更全面、更准确的Android应用模型，从而提升自动化测试生成的效果，弥补了传统方法在Android平台上的不足。

Abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [220] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PADRAIG，一个基于模型的Android自动化测试输入生成框架，利用详细的控制流模型生成测试，在19个F-Droid应用上的实验表明，相比现有最先进工具，平均行覆盖率提高16%，测试生成运行时间减少84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android自动化测试输入生成已研究十余年，但现有方法各有缺陷：随机输入生成易于维护但效率低下；系统化和基于搜索的方法能产生有效测试但生成运行时间过长；基于模型的方法虽有建模开销但生成更快。因此需要一种能同时实现高覆盖率和低生成运行时间的测试输入生成方法。

Method: PADRAIG采用基于模型的方法，通过构建被测应用（AUT）的详细控制流模型来指导测试输入生成，从而实现更高的行覆盖率并降低测试生成运行时间。

Result: 在19个随机选取的F-Droid应用上进行实验，与3个使用不同测试输入生成技术的现有最先进工具对比，PADRAIG平均比现有最先进工具多覆盖16%的代码行，且测试生成运行时间平均减少84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG证明了基于详细控制流模型的测试输入生成方法能够在覆盖率和生成效率两方面都优于现有技术，是Android自动化测试生成领域的一个重要进展。

Abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [221] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够表示Android应用程序代码及交互元素的控制流结构，通过给Exerciser Monkey工具提供测试环境知识，显著提升了其测试覆盖率和效率。基于该结构的深度优先搜索方法（Monkey++）比随机测试快一个数量级，并实现用户交互元素的完全覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Android移动应用测试工具（如Exerciser Monkey）随机生成用户输入事件，缺乏对应用底层结构的了解，导致测试速度慢且难以发现bug，亟需一种能提供测试环境知识的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种新型控制流结构来表示Android应用的代码及所有交互元素，并将该结构与Exerciser Monkey结合形成Monkey++，采用深度优先搜索（DFS）遍历控制流结构来生成测试序列。

Result: 与随机性质的Exerciser Monkey相比，基于DFS的测试套件速度快一个数量级，并且实现了用户交互元素的完全覆盖；同时通过去除重复/冗余测试提升了效率。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将控制流结构知识注入测试工具可以显著提升Android自动化测试的有效性和效率，并为未来更精准的基于搜索的自动化测试技术奠定基础。

Abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [222] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向CRM原生消息管理包的合规同意记录架构，解决了TCPA等法规对企业消息系统中明确同意获取和退出请求响应的严格要求，同时应对多租户托管包无法修改安装组织schema的架构约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法规（如TCPA）要求企业消息系统必须获取并记录明确的用户同意，并及时响应退订请求，但CRM平台虽然提供了丰富的联系人和线索数据模型，却未将同意状态作为一等记录类型原生建模。这一缺口在多租户托管包场景下尤为显著，因此需要一种不依赖安装组织schema的通用同意记录架构。

Method: 论文基于CRM原生消息管理包的生产实践，提出了同意记录架构，涵盖：数据模型设计（将同意状态作为一等记录）、基于关键词的同意捕获机制、基于哈希的唯一性去重策略、发送时的抑制执行机制，并分析了多租户约束对设计决策的影响。

Result: 该架构已在覆盖医疗、金融服务和销售运营等多个独立企业组织的生产环境中得到验证，证明了在多租户托管包场景下无需修改安装组织schema即可实现合规同意管理的可行性。

Conclusion: 论文证明了一种通用、可扩展的同意记录架构可以嵌入CRM消息管理托管包中，在满足TCPA等法规合规要求的同时，克服多租户环境下的schema不可修改约束，为同类企业消息系统提供了可复用的架构模式。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [223] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LOOPSBENCH，一个用于编码代理"循环工程"评估的长时程基准。包含112个来自真实来源的任务，覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域，每个任务以依赖DAG组织可测试开发单元。评测发现最强配置（Opus-4.7 + Claude Code + 外层续接）仅解决25%的任务，表明当前编码代理在长时程软件开发中仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理基准多聚焦于局部任务或最终状态结果，无法为持续长时程软件开发提供充分洞察。随着编码代理从"脚手架工程"转向"循环工程"，需要一个新的基准来评估代理在长时间、多步骤开发过程中的持续执行能力。

Method: 构建LOOPSBENCH基准：1) 每个任务构建为依赖DAG，节点为可独立测试的开发单元，边为有源码证据的前置依赖；2) 包含112个来自真实来源的任务，覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域；3) 采用流程感知运行时，沿就绪前沿释放测试，并保留已完成节点作为回归测试义务。

Result: 在评估前沿编码代理与常用循环实现组合后发现：最强配置Opus-4.7搭配Claude Code和外部续接机制仅解决25.00%的任务；记录的计划只能恢复部分源码恢复的前置依赖DAG；回归事件在评估的循环配置中仍然可见。

Conclusion: 当前编码代理在长时程软件开发任务上表现有限，LOOPSBENCH揭示了现有循环实现的不足，并通过开源基准数据和代码（含5300+开发单元及可执行测试）为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Coding agent infrastructure is shifting from harness engineering toward loop engineering as coding agents are deployed for sustained long-horizon software development. Existing benchmarks often center on localized tasks or end-state outcomes, offering limited insight into sustained execution. We introduce LOOPSBENCH, a long-horizon benchmark for loop engineering in coding agent evaluation. Each task is a dependency DAG over separately testable development units with source-evidenced prerequisite edges. LOOPSBENCH comprises 112 tasks from authentic sources spanning 8 programming languages and 9 domains. Its flow-aware runtime releases tests along the ready frontier and retains completed nodes as regression obligations. We evaluate frontier coding agents paired with widely used loop implementations. The strongest configuration, Opus-4.7 with Claude Code and outer continuation, resolves 25.00% of tasks. Recorded plans recover only part of the source-recovered prerequisite DAG, and regression events remain visible across the evaluated loop profiles. We open source the benchmark data and code, including all tasks, more than 5,300 development units, and executable tests, at microsoft/Loopsbench.

</details>


### [224] [OrEdge: Efficient Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection in Distributed Software Systems via Orthogonal-Domain Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00309)
*Amr M. Zaki,Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Honggeun Ji,Komal Sarda,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出OrEdge，一个轻量级的多模态分布式软件系统实时异常检测框架，利用正交域时间表示替代昂贵的注意力/图架构，以极小的模型规模（≤9.6K参数）在边缘设备上实现亚秒级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法依赖计算昂贵的注意力机制和图神经网络架构，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上实时部署，需要更轻量高效的替代方案来联合分析日志、指标和追踪等多模态数据。

Method: OrEdge采用正交域时间表示，核心组件OrEdgeCore是一个轻量级正交域重建模块，通过捕获周期性时间模式并抑制瞬态变化来实现异常检测，同时联合分析日志、指标和追踪三种异构数据。

Result: 在三个真实微服务数据集（MSDS、SN、TT）上取得与现有方法相当的检测性能，模型参数从20K-143K降至最多9.6K；在Raspberry Pi上推理延迟降低一个数量级以上，实现亚秒级推理。

Conclusion: 正交域时间建模可作为注意力/图架构的有效替代方案，在检测精度和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，适合边缘环境下的实时多模态异常检测。

Abstract: We introduce Orthogonal-Edge (OrEdge), a lightweight framework for real-time anomaly detection in multi-modal distributed software systems. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive attention- and graph-based architectures, OrEdge leverages orthogonal-domain temporal representations to achieve accurate anomaly detection with substantially lower computational complexity and model size. It jointly analyzes heterogeneous monitoring data, including logs, metrics, and traces, to identify abnormal software behavior, capture temporal dependencies, and reduce redundancy across observability signals. At its core, OrEdge incorporates OrEdgeCore, a lightweight orthogonal-domain reconstruction module that captures recurring temporal patterns while suppressing transient variations. Evaluated on three real-world microservice datasets (MSDS, SN, and TT), OrEdge achieves competitive detection performance while reducing the reconstruction model size to at most 9.6K parameters, compared with 20K--143K parameters in existing methods. This compact design enables efficient deployment on resource-constrained edge devices: on Raspberry Pi platforms, OrEdge achieves sub-second inference and reduces inference latency by over an order of magnitude compared with existing approaches. Extensive ablation studies, sensitivity analyses, orthogonal basis evaluations, and qualitative case studies further validate the effectiveness of each design component. Overall, OrEdge demonstrates that orthogonal-domain temporal modeling provides an effective alternative to computationally intensive attention- and graph-based architectures, achieving a favorable balance between detection accuracy and computational efficiency for real-time multi-modal anomaly detection in edge environments. The code is available at https://github.com/theamrzaki/MicroService_Twin_Original.

</details>


### [225] [Artificial Intelligence and Modeling & Simulation: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00366)
*Niclas Feldkamp,Philippe J. Giabbanelli,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本报告系统梳理了人工智能（AI）与建模与仿真（M&S）之间双向交叉融合的现状。AI可支持、增强甚至替代仿真研究中的各环节组件，而仿真也可为AI提供数据生成、训练环境和评估平台。报告按M&S流程各阶段（模型规范、输入建模、执行、实验设计、验证与确认、输出分析）组织论述，并结合大语言模型等技术的典型案例，指出当前局限与开放挑战，为读者提供概念性路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与M&S两个领域的研究需求日益趋同，加之生成式AI等技术快速进步以及数据和计算资源不断丰富，亟需一个结构化框架来梳理两者之间的交叉关系，帮助研究者理解这一快速演变的生态。

Method: 按M&S生命周期各阶段（模型规范、输入建模、执行、实验、验证与确认、输出分析）进行结构化综述，选取各阶段的代表性研究，重点展示LLM等AI技术如何重塑仿真实践，并指出局限与开放挑战，同时提供概念性路线图。

Result: 成功构建了AI与M&S双向关系的结构化全景图：一方面总结AI在各仿真阶段的应用价值，另一方面阐明仿真作为AI数据源、训练场和评估平台的作用；通过案例展示了LLM等新技术对仿真实践的影响，并识别了当前的技术局限与待解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: AI与M&S的融合是双向且深层次的，两者相互赋能、协同演进。该报告提供的结构化路线图为研究者和实践者在快速变化的交叉领域中导航提供了重要参考，同时也揭示了该方向仍存在诸多开放挑战待攻克。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and Modeling & Simulation (M&S) are increasingly intertwined, reflecting converging research needs across both communities, rapid technological advances such as the rise of generative AI, and the growing availability of data and computational resources. This report provides a structured overview of the intersections of AI and M&S. The relationship goes both ways: AI can support, augment, or even replace components of simulation studies, while simulations can serve as data generators, training environments, and evaluation platforms for AI. We organize this landscape along the stages of M&S from model specification and input modeling to execution, experimentation, verification and validation, and output analysis. Selected studies at each stage illustrates how techniques such as Large Language Models have reshaped simulation practices, while highlighting limitations and open challenges. This report also provides a conceptual roadmap that helps readers navigate a rapidly changing ecosystem.

</details>


### [226] [CASPER-Change-Aware Slice Prioritization for Efficient Regression Testing of LLM-based systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00378)
*Biruk Asmare Muse,Lionel Briand,Yiwei Lu,Keheliya Gallaba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CASPER，一个基于变更感知的切片优先级排序框架，用于LLM系统的回归测试。CASPER通过进化式切片识别方法定位语义相关且性能一致的回归切片，并根据执行日志中的行为信息对切片进行优先级排序。实验表明CASPER生成的切片一致性更好，回归切片优先级排序效果优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统的回归测试面临独特挑战：单个回归实例信息有限，无法反映系统级回归或诊断受影响行为；而仅依赖整体系统性能变化又过于粗粒度，无法识别具体受影响的用户行为。因此需要在中间层级（测试套件切片）分析回归实例。

Method: CASPER框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 使用进化式切片识别方法识别包含语义相关实例且具有一致性能特征的回归切片；(2) 针对LLM应用的变更，利用执行日志中提取的行为信息，对切片按其回归可能性进行优先级排序。

Result: 在软件问题解决领域实例化CASPER，与基于聚类的基线和随机排序基线比较：CASPER生成的切片一致性更好，同时保持可比或更好的语义连贯性；在不同LLM系统变更和测试预算下，回归切片优先级排序效果均有提升。

Conclusion: CASPER有效解决了LLM系统回归测试中"实例级过于细粒度、系统级过于粗粒度"的中间层级分析问题，为LLM系统的高效回归测试提供了可行方案，并在切片质量和回归优先级排序方面优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Regression testing for LLM-based systems poses unique challenges because individual regression instances provide limited information about system-level regressions. A failure in a single instance does not necessarily indicate a meaningful regression or provide sufficient information to diagnose affected behaviors. Conversely, detecting regressions based only on overall system performance changes is too coarse-grained, as it does not identify which behaviors are affected. This motivates analyzing regression instances at an intermediate level through test suite slices.
  To address this challenge, we propose CASPER, a change-aware slice prioritization framework for efficient regression testing of prompt-level and model-level changes in LLM-based systems. CASPER first identifies regression slices containing semantically related instances with consistent performance characteristics using an evolutionary slice identification approach. Given a change to an LLM-based application, CASPER prioritizes slices according to their likelihood of regression using behavioral information extracted from execution logs. We instantiate CASPER in the software issue resolution domain and evaluate slice identification against clustering-based baselines and regressed slice prioritization against a random ranking baseline. Results show that CASPER generates more consistent slices while maintaining comparable or better semantic coherence and improves regressed slice prioritization across different LLM-based system changes and testing budgets.

</details>


### [227] [Distilling Reasoning Traces into Advisory Prompts for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00437)
*Faizan Faisal,Prem Devanbu,Toufique Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外训练即可减少LLM代码生成错误的方法：通过让低资源LLM在"思考模式"下识别错误，再由更大的LLM生成解释和总结，最终提炼为简短的"咨询提示词"（advisory prompts），帮助模型在不开推理模式的情况下避免常见编码错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码处理中常出错，但训练数据本身来自人类编写的、不完美的代码，难以获得完全无缺陷的大规模语料库。推理模式虽能减少错误但消耗额外资源，因此需要一种无需始终启用推理模式就能提升性能的方法。

Method: 受人类编程学习过程的启发（识别错误、反思认知失误、提炼规则、内化教训），本文采用三步流程：(1) 在低资源LLM中识别"思考模式"能避免的错误示例；(2) 由更大的LLM检查这些错误及思考模式下的规避方式，生成总结性解释；(3) 再由大型LLM将这些解释提炼为简短的咨询提示词，供模型在不开启推理模式时使用。

Result: 该方法在多种中等规模模型上有效，在某些情况下学到的咨询提示词还能迁移到其他模型。论文还提供了对LLM编码错误本质的调查研究，以及该方法的适用条件特征化。

Conclusion: 通过从"思考模式"中蒸馏出可复用的咨询提示词，可以在不持续付出推理成本的情况下降低LLM编码错误率，并具有跨模型迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Language models are widely used for generating and otherwise processing code (e.g., identifying code hallucinations, possible inputs, or predicting outputs); however, LLMs can make mistakes, which can be serious. One key issue is that models are trained on (still) largely human-written, and thus imperfect, code; it's not easy to find sufficiently large code corpora that are entirely free of bugs. Thus, other inference-time ways of reducing LLM errors, without additional training, are desirable. "Reasoning" or "thinking" modes, exposed as a togglable feature by hybrid reasoning models, do reduce errors; however, reasoning consumes additional resources. This paper asks if better performance can be achieved without always incurring the cost of reasoning. Human students of programming learn to avoid mistakes by (a) identifying them, (b) reflecting upon the cognitive lapses that led to them (essentially, "thinking through" the errors), (c) inferring general rules or lessons from these reflections, and (d) internalizing these lessons into rules. In tutorial sessions with an instructor, this is a common Socratic interaction. Examples of such internalizable rules might include the nugget "Before coding, restate the requirements to clarify them." Inspired by this process, this paper describes an approach where we first identify examples in which "thinking mode" in a (low-resource) LLM avoids errors. These errors, and their avoidance via "thinking" in the same LLM, are then examined by a bigger LLM to generate summary explanations; these are then summarized by a large LLM into brief advisory prompts. This approach works on many modest-sized models; in some cases, the "advisory prompts" thus learned can also be gainfully transferred to other models. We also present investigations into the nature of coding errors that language models make, and a characterization of when this approach can be helpful.

</details>


### [228] [AiFlow: Token-Native Reactive Orchestration with Bounded Backpressure for Streaming LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00558)
*Qunhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AiFlow 是一种 token 原生的响应式编排模型，将 LLM 提供商的数据流差异归一化为类型化的 Context<T> 事件，通过有向流式图传播，并由 Node Guardian 管理队列边界、并发、排序、背压和重试。实验表明相比聚合方式，应用端 TTFPT 降低 70.9-94.7%，且运行时队列深度始终保持在声明边界内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 应用框架虽能暴露提供商的增量数据，但工作流节点通常将生成视为粗粒度的请求-响应步骤，队列管理、工作者分配、排序和背压等依赖临时的回调代码，缺乏系统化的编排方案。

Method: 提出 token 原生的响应式编排模型 AiFlow：将提供商增量归一化为类型化 Context<T> 事件并通过有向流式图传播；每个节点由 Node Guardian 管理，声明并强制局部队列边界、工作者并发、排序、溢出策略、取消传播和重试纪律；形式化有界内存属性，提供从紧凑 DSL 和 JSON 图形式的编译，以及类型安全、状态并发和注入兼容性的静态验证。

Result: 受控微基准、DeepSeek 轨迹回放（30 次）、在线运行、LangGraph 基线对比、流式 RAG 工作负载以及 Ollama 本地后端检查显示：不改变提供商端 Model TTFT，但应用端 TTFPT 降低 70.9-94.7%，运行时拥有的队列深度保持在声明边界内（相比无界策略 MaxQ 降低 93.7-96.5%）。

Conclusion: AiFlow 在保持提供商端首 token 延迟不变的同时，显著降低了应用端端到端延迟并将队列深度控制在声明的有界范围内，证明了 token 原生响应式编排在 LLM 流式工作流中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly operate as streaming workflows combining retrieval, tool calls, safety filters, and multi-agent coordination. Although contemporary frameworks expose provider deltas, workflow nodes often treat generation as coarse request-response steps, leaving queue management, worker allocation, ordering, and backpressure to ad hoc callback code. This paper presents AiFlow, a token-native reactive orchestration model that normalizes provider deltas into typed Context<T> events propagated through a directed streaming graph. Each node is managed by a Node Guardian that declares and enforces local queue bounds, worker concurrency, ordering, overflow policy, cancellation propagation, and retry discipline. We formalize the bounded-memory property, present the compilation from a compact DSL and JSON graph form, and provide static validation for type safety, state concurrency, and injection compatibility. Controlled microbenchmarks, captured DeepSeek trace replay (30 runs), descriptive online runs, LangGraph baselines, a streaming RAG workload, and an Ollama local-backend check show that AiFlow does not alter provider-side Model TTFT but reduces Application TTFPT by 70.9-94.7\% versus aggregation and keeps runtime-owned queue depth within declared bounds (93.7-96.5\% MaxQ reduction versus unbounded policies). The supplementary artifact contains scripts, raw traces, machine-readable tables, checksums, and an API-free smoke test; the public implementation is available through the FIT Framework repository.

</details>


### [229] [Less Is More: Tuning Configurable Systems with Imperfect Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00759)
*Yulong Ye,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MFTune配置调优器，利用"不完美保真度"环境（相似但测量成本更低）进行部分调优，通过探索超过10^4种可能的保真度设置来生成高质量种子，从而显著提升配置调优的预算利用效率。实验显示在83.33%的案例中优于10个现有调优器，最高提升19.34%，并节省大量时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置调优对高度可配置系统至关重要，但面临两个核心挑战：可调选项众多且配置测量成本昂贵。作者发现了"少即是多"的现象，即在不完美保真度（更便宜的类似环境）下进行部分调优，反而能实现更好的预算利用和调优效果。

Method: 作者首先为可配置系统建立了"保真度"的概念框架，然后提出MFTune调优器。MFTune主动在超过10^4种可能的保真度设置空间中探索，以逼近一个有用的设置，从而追求调优的广度。生成的种子用于完美保真度环境进行深度调优，实现广度与深度的结合。

Result: 在10个最先进的调优器对比实验中，使用多个真实世界系统进行19个月24×7的测试，MFTune在83.33%的案例中表现更好，最高提升19.34%的性能，并普遍节省数小时的预算。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在不完美保真度环境下进行部分调优可以显著提升配置调优的效果和效率，MFTune通过巧妙利用保真度空间的探索，实现了更好的预算利用和调优性能。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is essential for optimizing the performance of highly configurable systems, e.g., throughput or runtime, under a given environment. Yet, this is a challenging process as there can be many options to tune, and configuration measurement is often highly expensive. In this paper, we demonstrate the phenomenon of ``less can be more'': system configuration tuning can be greatly improved with much superior budget utilization by partially tuning under the imperfect-fidelity---an environment that is similar, but cheaper to measure, compared with the concerned perfect-fidelity of environment under which the system should be tuned. We codify a conceptual framework of fidelity for configurable systems, drawing on which allows us to propose MFTune, a tuner that proactively explores in the space of $>10^4$ possible imperfect-fidelity settings to approximate a useful one, which strikes for the wideness of tuning. This creates high-quality seeds for the perfect-fidelity, which in turn ensures the tuning depth. Experiment results against $10$ state-of-the-art tuners, obtained from running diverse real-world systems for $19$ months $24 \times 7$, show that MFTune performs considerably better on $83.33$\% cases with up to $19.34\%$ improvement while achieving hours of budget saving in general.

</details>


### [230] [Can Perplexity Serve as a Cognitive Signal for Code Understandability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00624)
*Xiaokai Rong,Mohammadali Sefidi Esfahani,Aashish Yadavally,Rigby Peter,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多项人类标注数据集实证研究发现，虽然token级困惑度能与代码理解的局部困惑对齐，但简单的片段级困惑度聚合（如均值、中位数、峰值）并不能可靠地反映代码可理解性，并揭示了三个原因：token困惑度分布高度偏斜且重尾、人类理解标注缺乏共识、不同模型和分词器的困惑度分布差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已表明大语言模型的token级困惑度可以对齐人类在代码理解过程中的局部困惑，这自然引发一个问题：困惑度能否作为代码片段（snippet）级别的可理解性信号？作者希望通过实证研究来检验这一假设。

Method: 作者在多个人类标注数据集上进行实证研究，包括方法级可理解性判断、输出预测任务和已接受的可理解性改进补丁。首先检验简单困惑度聚合方法的有效性，然后从三个维度分析其失效原因：token困惑度的分布特征、人类标注的一致性、以及不同模型和分词器的影响。

Result: 研究发现简单的片段级困惑度聚合（平均、中位数、峰值）与人类可理解性之间没有可靠的相关性。原因是：token困惑度高度偏斜且重尾分布，极端值不仅来自语义重要结构，还来自标识符、字面量、类型、分隔符和分词伪影；人类理解标注缺乏共识导致目标本身噪声大；困惑度分布及其与人类难度的对齐在不同模型和分词器间差异显著。

Conclusion: 困惑度是有前景但需谨慎使用的认知信号：适合局部代码困惑的检测，但要支持可靠的代码可理解性度量，需要代码感知的聚合方法、共识感知的评估以及模型敏感性分析。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that token-level perplexity from large language models can align with localized human confusion during code comprehension. This raises a natural question: can perplexity also serve as a snippet-level signal for code understandability? We conduct an empirical study of this question across multiple human-grounded datasets, including method-level understandability judgments, output-prediction tasks, and accepted understandability-improvement patches. Despite prior token-level evidence, we find that simple snippet-level aggregations of token perplexity, such as average, median, or peak perplexity, do not reliably correlate with human understandability. We then investigate why this happens. First, token perplexity is highly skewed and heavy-tailed across code structures; extreme spikes arise not only from semantically meaningful constructs, but also from identifiers, literals, types, separators, and tokenization artifacts. Second, human understandability labels often lack consensus, making whole-snippet difficulty a noisy target. Third, perplexity distributions and their alignment with human difficulty vary substantially across models and tokenizers. These findings explain why prior token-level perplexity--confusion alignment does not directly transfer to snippet-level understandability. Overall, our study positions perplexity as a promising but delicate cognitive signal: useful for localized code confusion, but requiring code-aware aggregation, consensus-aware evaluation, and model-sensitivity analysis before it can support reliable code-understandability measurement.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding Online Failure Prediction in Linux Through Complementary Multi-View Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00651)
*Diogo Dória,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Linux操作系统中构建和评估可解释的在线故障预测（OFP）管道的实践经验。该方法结合基于共识的特征选择、时间onset分析、子系统级因果分析及互补诊断机制，在严格跨工作负载条件下实现91-94%的检测率和低于1%的误报率，但诊断能力对工作负载变化更敏感。研究发现检测泛化能力强于诊断，预警时间因故障模式从38秒到215秒不等，未见过的故障模式在LOMO评估下诊断准确率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在线故障预测（OFP）在OS环境中已被证明可行，但仅有预测而缺乏诊断洞察力不足以支持实际部署。操作员需要信任告警并做出响应决策，同时需要弄清高精度模型捕捉的是有意义的故障过程还是仅利用了遥测中的工作负载特定噪声和偶然相关性。

Method: 构建可解释OFP管道，采用四个关键组件：(1) 基于共识的特征选择用于故障检测；(2) 时间onset分析用于预警；(3) 子系统级因果分析用于故障解释；(4) 互补的诊断机制。在严格的跨工作负载条件下使用冻结的训练工件进行评估。

Result: 在未见工作负载上无需重训练即可实现91-94%的检测率，误报率低于1%。但故障模式诊断对工作负载偏移更敏感，部分诊断机制对特定故障类型效果有限。预警能力从38秒到215秒不等，LOMO评估下未见故障模式的诊断准确率为0%。

Conclusion: 检测能力比诊断能力在跨工作负载变化中更具鲁棒性；预警能力高度依赖故障模式；仅靠相关训练模式无法可靠诊断未见过的故障模式。互补的可解释性机制对于解释准确故障预测至关重要，能够揭示预测信号何时反映可迁移的故障结构，以及诊断泛化在何时会因工作负载变化而失效。

Abstract: Accurate Online Failure Prediction (OFP) has been shown to be feasible in Operating Systems (OSs) settings, but prediction alone is not sufficient for practical adoption. Without diagnostic insight, operators have limited basis to trust alerts or decide how to respond. Moreover, even when predictive accuracy is high, it is often unclear whether models are capturing meaningful failure processes or merely exploiting workload-specific noise and incidental correlations in telemetry. This paper reports a practical experience building and evaluating an explainable OFP pipeline for Linux OSs. We combine consensus-based feature selection for detection with temporal onset analysis, subsystemlevel causal analysis, and complementary diagnostic mechanisms to support failure interpretation. Evaluated under strict crossworkload conditions with frozen training artifacts, it achieved 91-94% detection on unseen workloads without retraining, while maintaining false alarm rates below 1%. However, failure mode diagnosis proved substantially more sensitive to workload shift, and several diagnostics mechanisms showed limited effectiveness for specific failure types. Our experience highlights three main lessons: i) detection generalizes more robustly than diagnosis across workload changes; ii) early-warning capability depends strongly on the failure mode, ranging from 38 to 215 seconds in our study; and iii) unseen failure modes are not reliably diagnosable from related training modes alone, providing 0% accuracy under Leave-One-Mode-Out (LOMO) evaluation. Taken together, these results show the value of complementary explainability mechanisms for interpreting accurate failure predictions, revealing when predictive signals reflect transferable failure structure and when diagnostic generalization breaks down under workload variation.

</details>


### [232] [Unreliable in Practice? A Comprehensive Study of Errors in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00661)
*Rodrigo Pato Nogueira,Marco Vieira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了由7个LLM在4种编译语言中生成的86,726个包含编译或运行时错误的代码样本，对其错误类型进行了分类和比较分析，发现错误类型在不同语言和模型间差异显著，即使最大的模型也常犯简单错误，且生成的代码常缺乏输入验证和内存安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码在复杂任务中仍存在错误，但已有研究范围有限（单一语言、少量问题或模型），缺乏对LLM生成代码错误的全面理解，因此需要系统性地识别和表征这些错误，以设定合理预期并设计缓解策略。

Method: 收集了7个LLM在4种编译语言中生成的86,726个含错误的代码样本，使用LLM对错误按根本原因进行分类，人工验证分类结果，并进行跨模型、跨语言、跨问题难度的比较分析。

Result: 错误类型在不同语言和模型间差异显著；即使最大的模型也频繁犯简单错误；生成的代码常省略基本输入验证或内存安全检查，可能导致溢出、资源耗尽等可靠性/安全性问题。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM生成代码错误的大规模、多维度分析框架，揭示了普遍存在的简单错误和安全性缺失问题，为改进LLM代码生成能力和设计针对性缓解策略提供了数据基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being widely used for coding, with reports indicating that AI now generates an increasing share of production code. Studies show that LLMs can significantly improve developer productivity, yet they still struggle with more complex coding tasks. Just as understanding error modes in human-written code has been central to improving software quality, identifying and characterizing the errors in LLM-generated code is critical for setting realistic expectations and designing mitigation strategies. Prior research has been limited in scope, often focusing on a single language, a small number of problems, or a limited selection of models. As a result, there is still no comprehensive understanding of which errors are common and which are specific to certain models or languages. To address these gaps and develop a deeper understanding of the quality of LLM-generated code, we analyzed a corpus of 86,726 code samples that contained compilation or runtime errors. These samples were generated by seven LLMs across four compiled languages. We classified errors by their underlying causes using an LLM, manually validated these classifications, and performed a comparative analysis. This labeled data is then used to measure error prevalence by model, language, and problem difficulty, to identify common error patterns. Results show that, although error types vary strongly across languages and models, even the largest models frequently make simple mistakes. We also observe that generated code often omits basic input validation or memory-safety checks, which can lead to overflows, resource exhaustion, or other reliability/security issues.

</details>


### [233] [Vul4Py: Benchmarking Automated Vulnerability Repair in Python with Paired Exploit and Functional Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00692)
*Tan Bui,Ting Zhang,Ferdian Thung,Yunpeng Xiong,Penghao Jiang,Xin Zhou,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Vul4Py是一个包含100个真实Python漏洞的基准测试集，每个漏洞都配有双重测试标准（exploit测试和功能回归测试）。实验表明，软件工程智能体在自动漏洞修复中表现最佳，OpenHands修复了41/100个漏洞，远优于直接提示LLM（4个）和专用修复工具（2个），且双重测试标准能有效过滤掉会产生功能回归的错误补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Python漏洞修复基准测试存在缺陷：要么仅依赖概念验证漏洞利用来验证补丁，要么只在部分项目上运行功能测试，两者都会遗漏"补丁修复了漏洞但破坏了其他功能"的回归问题。需要一个可信的、可验证的Python自动漏洞修复基准来公平比较不同方法。

Method: 构建Vul4Py基准测试集，包含100个来自60个开源项目的真实漏洞（覆盖60种CWE，时间跨度2017-2025年）。每个漏洞配有配对的测试标准：漏洞利用测试（必须在修复前失败、修复后通过）和项目自带的pytest功能测试（必须在两方面都通过）。使用该基准比较了六种方法，涵盖三类：专用修复工具、直接提示的LLM、软件工程智能体。

Result: 软件工程智能体表现最佳：OpenHands修复了100个漏洞中的41个，而最强的直接提示LLM仅修复4个，专用工具仅修复2个（三者共享相同的底层模型）。配对测试标准拒绝了119个补丁中15个会被漏洞利用单独验证通过的补丁，且104个被接受的补丁中有98个被人工确认为与开发者的补丁语义等价。

Conclusion: Vul4Py提供了更严格可信的Python漏洞修复评测框架，证明软件工程智能体在自动漏洞修复中显著优于其他方法，同时强调双重测试标准（不仅验证漏洞被修复，还要确保功能不退化）对于评估自动修复工具的必要性。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has advanced rapidly across program analysis, machine learning, and Large Language Models (LLMs), but a verifiable, head-to-head comparison of AVR approaches on Python is still missing. Python underpins critical web, data, and machine-learning infrastructure, yet existing Python benchmarks accept a patch on the strength of a proof-of-concept exploit alone, or apply a functional test only on the subset of entries whose upstream project happens to ship one. Both therefore miss functional regressions, in which a patch defeats the exploit but breaks unrelated behavior. We present Vul4Py, a Python AVR benchmark in which every entry carries a paired oracle: an exploit oracle that must fail on the vulnerable revision and pass on the fixed one, together with a project-native pytest functional oracle that must pass on both. Vul4Py comprises 100 real vulnerabilities from 60 open-source projects, spanning 60 distinct CWEs and the years 2017 to 2025, each packaged with a pinned, reproducible per-instance environment. Using Vul4Py, we compare six approaches in three categories: a specialized vulnerability repair tool, directly prompted LLMs, and software engineering agents. The agents dominate: OpenHands repairs 41 of 100 vulnerabilities, against 4 for the strongest directly prompted LLM and 2 for the specialized tool, despite all three sharing the same backbone model. The paired oracle is what makes these counts trustworthy: it rejects 15 of the 119 patches that an exploit-only oracle would accept, and 98 of the 104 patches it admits are manually confirmed to be semantically equivalent to the developer's patches

</details>


### [234] [EduPluginBench: Executable Assurance for AI-Generated Educational Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00739)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了EduPluginBench，一个面向受管软件生态系统中生成插件的可执行基准与分阶段准入方法。实验表明P0-P4相比P0-P2将发布阻断缺陷召回率提升74.7个百分点，且对120个干净参考无拒绝；但对600个未修改的当前代码模型生成结果，300个能解析但无一通过P0或P0-P4合规，迁移至Moodle的评估也全部失败，揭示生成模型的合规差距显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型能够生成可执行组件，但编译和功能测试不足以验证最小权限、遥测同意、来源、特权写权限、生命周期约束或故障有界性等安全与治理属性。现有评估缺乏针对受管生态系统中安全治理合规性的可执行基准。

Method: 构建EduPluginBench基准，包含30个规范生成的1,440个经过激活检查的一阶突变体；提出P0-P4分阶段准入方法；采用规范聚类的95%置信区间评估召回率提升；进行冻结迁移研究（600个未修改生成物）、独立标记的Moodle研究（16个漏洞/修复对）、以及540个生成的诊断性有界修复实验；附带完整协议、来源、原始数据与复现说明。

Result: P0-P4将发布阻断缺陷召回率比P0-P2提升74.7个百分点（规范聚类95% CI 73.4-75.8），120个干净参考中无拒绝（Wilson上限3.1%）。冻结迁移研究中300/600可解析但无一通过P0或P0-P4合规（95%上限0.64%），下游保证估计未定义。Moodle研究中冻结的通用PHP检测器未发现任何易受攻击版本。540生成诊断实验中有界修复产生112个P0通过但全部不合规，召回率从13.4%升至100%。

Conclusion: EduPluginBench展示了扩展准入检查的价值，但负面的迁移结果说明当前模型生成的插件在治理合规方面仍严重不足，且基准上的表现不能直接等同于真实缺陷检测有效性。有界修复虽能提高通过率但无法保证合规性，需要更严格的保障措施。

Abstract: Code-generation models can produce executable components, but compilation and functional tests do not establish compliance with least privilege, telemetry consent, provenance, privileged-write authority, lifecycle constraints, or bounded failure. We introduce EduPluginBench, an executable benchmark and staged admission method for generated plugins in governed software ecosystems. Across 1,440 activation-checked first-order mutants from 30 specifications, P0-P4 increased release-blocking-defect recall by 74.7 percentage points (specification-clustered 95% CI 73.4-75.8) over P0-P2, with no observed rejection among 120 clean references (95% Wilson upper bound 3.1%). A frozen transfer study of 600 unmodified generations from two current coding models found that 300/600 parsed, but none passed P0 or achieved P0-P4 conformance (95% upper bound 0.64%); downstream assurance estimands were undefined. An independently labelled Moodle study retained 16 vulnerable/fixed pairs; the frozen generic PHP detector found no vulnerable revisions. These negative transfer results prevent controlled contract consistency from being read as independent real-defect effectiveness. An earlier 540-generation diagnostic found that post-hoc bounded repair yielded 112 P0 passes, all nonconforming, with recall increasing from 13.4% to 100%. The artifact retains protocols, public-source provenance, raw generations, row-level decisions, audits, analysis code, and reproduction instructions.

</details>


### [235] [Turning Interaction History into Execution State: A Runtime Layer for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00808)
*Zehao Wang,Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Chao Peng,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE
