TL;DR: 本文提出Ledger，一个确定性运行时层，通过显式维护执行账本（记录观察、修改、尝试过的操作），在模型行动前注入运行时状态、在命令执行前进行校验，从而解决长时程编码代理因隐式推断执行状态不准确而导致的操作过时或重复执行问题。在SWE-bench Verified上，Ledger将GPT-5 mini的Pass@1从56.2%提升至64.2%，将MiniMax M2.5从75.8%提升至81.0%，同时降低28.9%和31.8%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程编码代理在轨迹中积累数百个操作和观察，但没有任何记录能表明哪些观察仍然描述了当前仓储状态。模型必须在每次决策前从原始历史中隐式推断执行状态，当推断不足时，代理会基于过时的文件内容行动或重复执行结果仍然有效的操作。

Method: Ledger是一个确定性运行时层，将代理的已完成交互蒸馏为显式执行状态（观察了什么、修改了什么、尝试了什么），并在线维护执行账本。它在每个步骤的两个边界应用此状态：模型行动前的inform路径将紧凑的运行时状态视图附加到提示中；命令运行前的govern路径根据账本检查命令，返回仍然有效的早期结果以替代重复执行，并标记可能冗余的重复操作。该层不添加任何语言模型调用，直接包装未修改的代理。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上，Ledger将GPT-5 mini的Pass@1从56.2%提升至64.2%，将MiniMax M2.5从75.8%提升至81.0%，同时将总成本降低28.9%和31.8%。附加到OpenAI Codex时，Pass@1增加3.4个百分点且成本降低24.4%。消融实验表明，govern路径贡献了大部分解决率提升，inform路径贡献了大部分效率提升，两者组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 长时程代理缺乏的不是更短的历史视角，而是对其自身执行状态的显式记录。通过为代理提供明确、实时、确定性的执行状态账本，可以显著提升任务解决率和执行效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents accumulate hundreds of actions and observations in their trajectories, yet nothing in this record indicates which observations still describe the repository as it currently stands. Before every decision, the model must implicitly infer the execution status from raw history, and when this inference falls short, the agent acts on outdated file contents or re-executes work whose results are still valid. We propose Ledger, a deterministic runtime layer that distills an agent's completed interactions into an explicit execution state: what has been observed, what has been modified, and what has been attempted. Ledger keeps this state in an online execution ledger and applies it at two boundaries of every step. Before the model acts, an inform path appends a compact runtime state view to the prompt; before a proposed command runs, a govern path checks it against the ledger, returning still-valid earlier results in place of re-execution and flagging likely-redundant repetition. The layer adds no language-model calls and wraps an otherwise unmodified agent. Across all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, Ledger raises Pass@1 from 56.2% to 64.2% with GPT-5 mini and from 75.8% to 81.0% with MiniMax M2.5, while cutting total cost by 28.9% and 31.8%. Attached to OpenAI Codex, it adds 3.4 percentage points of Pass@1 at 24.4% lower cost. Ablations attribute most of the resolution gain to govern and most of the efficiency gain to inform, with their combination performing best. What long-horizon agents lack, we conclude, is not a shorter view of their history but an explicit account of their own execution state.

</details>


### [236] [Structure-Aware Semantic Chunking with Title-Chain Prefixes: A 1600-Query Evaluation and the Measurement Trap in Text-Transform Ablations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00824)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个零额外LLM调用的三段式纯块侧语义分块流水线（标题拆分、语义合并、标题链前缀），在1600查询的生产Markdown知识库评估中将MRR@5提升23.8%（全量）和11.7%（可回答子集）。论文还报告了多次失败的尝试，并揭示了核心方法论发现：相同池中前缀开关消融实验中，双标注者一致性从kappa 0.45骤降至0.04，说明标题链上下文是标注者达成相关性共识的关键信号，这否定了分块研究中常见的评估实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块是RAG流程中最关键的第一步，所有下游检索决策都继承分块边界。现有语义分块方法通常依赖额外的LLM调用生成摘要或总结，成本高昂。作者希望设计一种零额外LLM调用的高效语义分块方案，同时揭示现有分块评估实践中存在的测量陷阱。

Method: 提出三阶段分块流水线：(1) header-split：利用文档自身的标题层级进行初步拆分；(2) semantic merge：对块进行语义合并优化；(3) title-chain prefixing：复用文档标题层级作为前缀信息，而非生成摘要。全程无需额外LLM调用。评估采用1600查询分层抽样，双标注者标注16000个分数对。

Result: 全量集MRR@5从0.374提升至0.463（+23.8%），可回答子集（n=563）从0.828提升至0.925（+11.7%），双标注者Cohen's kappa为0.45。同时报告了多种失败尝试：三个查询侧或架构级方案是设计死胡同，一个测量到失败的方案（前缀权重衰减），以及一个协议级失败——同池前缀开关消融中kappa从0.45崩溃至0.04。

Conclusion: 标题链前缀通过复用文档自身的层级结构，在不增加LLM调用成本的前提下显著提升RAG检索质量。核心方法论发现是：前缀对标注者相关性判断的消歧作用极为关键，移除前缀会导致标注一致性崩溃（kappa从0.45降至0.04），因此分块研究中常见的同池前缀开关评估实践是无效的，应转向检索时逐候选前缀评估。

Abstract: Chunking is the first and most consequential step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): every downstream retrieval decision inherits the chunk boundaries. We present a three-stage, chunk-side-only semantic chunking pipeline---header-split, semantic merge, and title-chain prefixing---that costs zero additional LLM calls: the title chain reuses the document's own header hierarchy instead of a generated summary. On a 1600-query stratified evaluation over a production Markdown knowledge base, the pipeline improves MRR@5 from 0.374 to 0.463 (+23.8%) on the full set and from 0.828 to 0.925 (+11.7%) on the answerable subset (n=563), with dual-annotator Cohen's kappa 0.45 (unweighted, 16,000 score pairs). We then report what we tried and what failed: three query-side or architecture-level follow-ups are design dead ends (unevaluated---no comparable run artifacts), one measured failure (prefix weight decay), and one protocol-level failure that is the paper's central methodological finding. In a same-pool prefix on/off ablation, dual-annotator agreement collapsed from kappa 0.45 to 0.04 under identical prompts---stripping the title-chain context strips the disambiguation signal annotators need to agree on relevance. This measurement trap invalidates a common evaluation practice in chunking research and motivates retrieval-time per-candidate prefix evaluation, the direction we recommend from all our evidence.

</details>


### [237] [Documentation vs. Code Patterns: What Drives LLM-Based Exception Oracle Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00884)
*Soneya Binta Hossain,Matthew B. Dwyer,Tasfia Tasnim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模干预实验（删除Javadoc @throws子句及归因引导的替换消融）调查了基于LLM的测试预言生成（TOG）系统预测异常预言时所依赖的证据，发现结构化异常文档并非主要驱动因素，模型的准确率可能源于捷径信号而非真正的异常语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based TOG方法在异常预言生成上报告了高准确率，但缺乏对其预测依据的深入理解——即模型究竟是依赖显式的异常行为文档（如Javadoc @throws子句），还是依赖测试、代码和文档中的重复模式。这挑战了"高准确率等于鲁棒理解异常语义"的假设。

Method: 采用干预式研究方法，对三个TOG系统（涵盖分类器架构和生成式架构，模型规模从约1.1亿到70亿参数）进行大规模实验。首先移除Javadoc @throws子句观察准确率变化；随后应用归因引导的替换消融（attribution-guided substitution ablations）来识别预测所依赖的信号特征。评估基于三个真实世界基准，包括两个生成测试数据集和一个开发者手写测试的新基准。

Result: 移除Javadoc @throws子句后，准确率仅发生微小变化（最大下降不足一个百分点），表明结构化异常文档并非主要预测驱动因素。归因消融实验发现高准确率可能由捷径信号驱动：部分模型对少量结构标记高度敏感，而其他模型则将依赖分散在众多词法线索上。

Conclusion: 强异常预言准确率并不能反映模型对异常语义的鲁棒理解。未来TOG系统的评估不仅应关注是否预测了正确的预言类型，还应检验其预测是否基于有意义的异常触发证据。

Abstract: LLM-based test oracle generation (TOG) methods report high accuracy on exception oracle generation, but it remains unclear what evidence drives these predictions. In particular, do models use explicit exceptional-behavior documentation such as Javadoc @throws clauses, or do they rely on recurring patterns in tests, code, and documentation?
  We investigate this question through a large-scale intervention-based study of three TOG systems spanning classifier-based and generative architectures and model sizes from roughly 110M to 7B parameters, evaluated on three real-world benchmarks comprising two generated-test datasets and a new benchmark of developer-written tests. We first remove Javadoc @throws clauses and find that accuracy changes only marginally, with the largest drop below one percentage point. This indicates that structured exception documentation is not the primary driver of exception-oracle prediction. We then apply attribution-guided substitution ablations to identify the signals that predictions depend on. The results show that high accuracy can be driven by shortcut signals: some models are highly sensitive to a small number of structural tokens, while others distribute reliance across many lexical cues. These findings challenge the assumption that strong exception-oracle accuracy reflects robust use of exception semantics. Future TOG systems should therefore be evaluated not only by whether they predict the correct oracle type, but also by whether their predictions are grounded in meaningful exception-triggering evidence.

</details>


### [238] [Joint Optimization of Human Headcount and Stochastic AI Resource Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00886)
*Marco Montes de Oca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定预算下，组织如何在人类员工与生成式AI token容量之间进行最优分配，提出了一种结合认知摩擦调整的生产函数与机会约束随机预算前沿的联合分配模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已成为编码和商业运营的标准工具，但其不可预测且往往高昂的成本给组织带来严重财务压力。标准确定性规划忽略了token消耗的重尾波动性以及审计AI输出的认知成本，需要更精确的随机模型来指导预算分配决策。

Method: 将认知摩擦调整的生产函数与机会约束随机预算前沿相交，构建联合分配模型。每位工程师的token使用量假设为i.i.d.、非负、右偏随机变量，仅需均值、方差和偏度三个统计量。通过中心极限定理结合Cornish-Fisher偏度修正，将机会约束转化为确定性等价形式。求解拉格朗日函数得到闭式最优员工数、每人均token强度的超越条件，以及bordered-Hessian二阶条件。

Result: 比较静态分析显示：个人token波动率上升会提高最优人均token强度同时缩减员工数——虽然生产上人力和token是互补品，但在随机预算约束下它们在边际上是替代品。个体使用偏度作为纯无谓损失税收缩预算但不变替代动态。若使用离散度与均值成比例缩放，替代关系反转，形成可实证检验的命题。

Conclusion: 研究为组织在固定预算下分配人类与生成式AI资源提供了理论基础，揭示了波动率和偏度对最优配置的关键影响，并指出了两种不同的离散度假设将导致截然不同的替代关系，这是一个尖锐且可实证检验的问题。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are rapidly becoming standard for coding and other business operations. However, while these tools can drive productivity, their unpredictable, and often significant, costs are putting severe financial strain on organizations. The leadership of such organizations must split a fixed budget between human headcount and generative-AI token capacity, yet standard deterministic planning ignores both the heavy-tailed volatility of token consumption and the cognitive cost of auditing AI-generated output. We model the joint allocation by intersecting a cognitive-friction-adjusted production function with a chance-constrained stochastic budget frontier. Per-engineer token usage is treated as i.i.d., non-negative, and right-skewed; no parametric family is assumed, as only its mean, variance, and skewness enter the analysis. The chance constraint is reduced to a deterministic equivalent via the Central Limit Theorem with a Cornish--Fisher skewness correction. Solving the resulting Lagrangian yields a closed-form optimal headcount, a transcendental condition for optimal per-capita token intensity, and a bordered-Hessian second-order condition that holds for any reasonable volatility-to-headcount ratio. Comparative statics show that rising individual token volatility raises optimal per-capita token intensity while contracting headcount: although human labor and tokens are complements in production, the stochastic budget makes them substitutes at the margin. Individual usage skewness acts as a pure deadweight tax that shrinks the budget without altering the substitution dynamics. Both conclusions presuppose that usage dispersion is invariant to the planned mean allocation. In the case where dispersion instead scales proportionally with the mean, the substitution reverses, so which regime applies is a sharp, empirically testable question.

</details>


### [239] [RefactorAssist: Agentic Refinement for Reliable Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00924)
*Jonathan Cordeiro,Shayan Noei,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RefactorAssist智能体，通过静态修复和基于测试日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索与代码差异的迭代修复机制，将LLM生成的代码重构失败率大幅降低，最佳配置下累计通过率达94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码重构常引入细微错误，导致功能行为改变和单元测试失败，限制了其实际应用。作者希望分析LLM重构失败的根本原因，并开发工具提高LLM生成重构的功能正确性。

Method: 使用10个开源Java项目及其原生测试套件，手动分析LLM重构失败原因并分类。设计RefactorAssist智能体，采用两阶段修复策略：先进行无需LLM的静态修复（缺失导入、括号不平衡、编译错误），对剩余失败再结合单元测试日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索和代码差异进行智能体驱动的迭代修复。

Result: 识别出8类主要失败原因（如上下文误解/幻觉占24.3%、错误或不一致重命名占15.3%等）；RefactorAssist在剩余失败上达到最高70.8%的修复率，最佳配置下累计通过率达94.2%。

Conclusion: 静态检查和测试引导、上下文感知的智能体修复可以显著提高LLM生成重构的可靠性，使其更接近实际开发者工作流中的集成应用。

Abstract: Code refactoring aims to enhance the internal structure of source code without affecting its functional behavior. The recent advancements of Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential for automating software engineering tasks, such as code refactoring. However, the refactorings produced by LLMs often introduce subtle errors, leading to functional behavior changes and failed unit tests, which limit their practical adoption. To address the limitations of LLM-generated refactorings, we analyze the root causes of their failures and develop the RefactorAssist agent to improve the functional correctness of LLM-generated refactorings. To this end, we use 10 open-source Java projects with their native test suites and manually evaluate why LLM-generated refactorings fail unit tests. We then design an agentic approach that leverages unit-test logs, error explanations, project context retrieval, and code diffs to guide the iterative refactoring. Our findings show that the main reasons for failure are context misunderstanding/hallucination (24.3%), incorrect or inconsistent renaming (15.3%), adding new functionality or variables (13.7%), code incompleteness (11.3%), syntax and structural errors (9.7%), edge cases not handled (9%), improper type handling (8.7%), and variables outside scope (8%). To make our approach cost-effective, RefactorAssist first applies a static repair step for missing imports, unbalanced brackets, and compilation errors without LLMs. For remaining failures, RefactorAssist incorporates error logs and code diffs, achieving up to a 70.8% repair rate on the remaining failures and a 94.2% cumulative pass rate under the best-performing configuration. These results indicate that static checks and test-guided, context-aware agentic repair can increase the reliability of LLM-generated refactorings, bringing them closer to practical integration within developer workflows.

</details>


### [240] [Claim Plane: Reliability Gains and the Limits of Selective Concurrency for Parallel Coding Agents: A 30-Pair, Three-Seed Confirmatory Study of Deterministic Pre-Write Admission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00947)
*Maxim Nikolaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了Claim Plane系统的验证性研究：通过对30对CooperBench特征（15对冲突、15对干净）进行360次完整执行，测试了基于版本化变更意图的确定性写入前准入机制。静态准入将配对通过率从23.3%提升至50.0%，但以序列化96.7%执行为代价；动态准入选择性更强但46/90次执行因未声明范围而失败关闭，导致通过率仅22.2%。研究支持写入前准入作为可靠性机制，但未实现有效的墙钟并行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行编码智能体可能产生各自局部有效但组合后失败的变更。研究动机在于验证Claim Plane系统能否通过确定性写入前准入机制解决这一并行编码中的组合失败问题，并评估其实际可靠性收益与并行加速代价。

Method: 在30对冻结的CooperBench特征（15冲突/15干净）上进行确认性研究，使用3个编码种子、4种协调策略，共360次完整执行。DeepSeek V4 Pro生成60份特性级规划声明并冻结，DeepSeek V4 Flash执行编码。比较无约束并行、静态准入和动态准入三种策略的表现。

Result: 静态Claim Plane将配对通过率从23.3%提升至50.0%（+26.7个百分点，95% bootstrap CI 9.6-60.0），集成成功率从65.6%提升至96.7%，冲突配对通过率从6.7%提升至60.0%；但静态准入序列化了96.7%的执行。动态准入仅序列化66.7%冲突和13.3%干净案例，但46/90次执行失败关闭（其中45次针对声明中已有的文件），通过率降至22.2%。

Conclusion: 写入前准入机制确实能作为提升并行编码可靠性的有效机制，但当前实现主要靠序列化来恢复可靠性，未建立有用的墙钟并行加速。区域覆盖不足和修订处理机制缺陷是动态准入的主要瓶颈，需进一步改进声明范围和修订处理以释放真正的并行潜力。

Abstract: Parallel coding agents can produce locally valid changes that fail when combined. Claim Plane addresses this failure mode as deterministic pre-write admission over versioned change intents. This paper reports a confirmatory study on 30 frozen CooperBench feature pairs, balanced between 15 conflict and 15 clean labels, with three coder seeds, four coordination arms, and 360 completed executions. DeepSeek V4 Pro generated 60 feature-level planner declarations once; the declarations were frozen across all arms and coder seeds, while DeepSeek V4 Flash performed the coding work. Static Claim Plane raised pair pass from 23.3% under unconstrained parallel execution to 50.0%, a paired task-cluster difference of +26.7 percentage points (95% bootstrap CI 9.6 to 60.0), and raised integration success from 65.6% to 96.7%. On conflict-labeled pairs, pair pass rose from 6.7% to 60.0%. However, static admission serialized 96.7% of executions, including 93.3% of clean cases, and therefore recovered reliability largely by collapsing toward serial execution. Dynamic admission was more selective, serializing 66.7% of conflict cases and 13.3% of clean cases, but 46 of 90 executions failed closed on undeclared scope, reducing pair pass to 22.2%. Forty-five of those 46 blocks targeted files already present in the frozen declarations, indicating region undercoverage and insufficient amendment handling rather than wholly unknown files. The results support pre-write admission as a reliability mechanism, but they do not establish useful wall-clock parallel speedup: provider calls were physically sequential, and the conservative policy largely serialized the workload. The complete study artifacts, hashes, and clustered bootstrap analysis are publicly released for reproduction.

</details>


### [241] [AgenTag: Attribution of AI Coding Agents from Behavioral Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00966)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenTag，一个用于开放世界AI编程代理归因的多模态框架，在包含33,580个AI代理PR和6,618个人类PR的AIDev数据集上评估。AgenTag在加权F1达到0.96、区分AI与人类PR的平衡F1为0.89，并能检测未知代理（AUC 0.84）。研究发现PR描述和提交信息提供了几乎全部的归因信号，代码差异贡献甚微，且行为指纹在去除自我披露标记后依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理在开发者账户下提交PR，模糊了代码变更的实际生产者，这对仓库治理、AI辅助软件开发实证研究以及AI编程代理影响度量都至关重要。现有工作仅聚焦于已知代理的闭集识别，开放世界场景下AI代理归因的实际边界尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出AgenTag多模态框架，利用文本、行为和代码三种模态表示PR，对比传统分类与监督对比学习方法，用于开放集识别和少样本注册新代理。构建AIDev数据集，包含来自5个AI编程代理的33,580个PR和6,618个人类编写的PR，通过消融实验分析各模态的贡献。

Result: AgenTag在代理识别上加权F1为0.96（宏F1为0.84），AI与人类PR区分上平衡F1为0.89，检测未见代理的AUC为0.84。PR描述和提交信息几乎涵盖全部归因信号，代码差异贡献极小；去除显式自我披露标记后行为指纹依然存在。

Conclusion: 可靠归因AI编程代理是可行的，AI代理主要通过沟通变更的方式而非生成代码的差异被区分，属性主要依赖潜在的风格特征而非显式披露，这澄清了归因准确性与所需信息之间的实际权衡。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly author pull requests (PRs), often under developers' own accounts, obscuring who actually produced a change. Reliable attribution is important for repository governance, empirical studies of AI-assisted software development, and measuring the impact of AI coding agents. Existing work focuses on closed-set identification of known agents, leaving the practical limits of open-world AI coding agent attribution largely unexplored. In this paper, we present AgenTag, a multimodal framework for open-world AI coding agent attribution, evaluated on AIDev, comprising 33,580 PRs from five AI coding agents and 6,618 human-authored PRs. We represent each PR using textual, behavioral, and code-based modalities, and compare conventional classification with supervised contrastive learning for open-set recognition and few-shot enrollment of previously unseen agents. AgenTag identifies authoring agents with a weighted F1 of 0.96 (macro F1 of 0.84), distinguishes AI- from human-authored PRs with a balanced F1 of 0.89, and detects previously unseen agents with an AUC of 0.84. We further show that PR descriptions and commit messages provide nearly all of the attribution signal, whereas code diffs contribute little across multiple representations, indicating that coding agents are distinguished primarily by how they communicate changes rather than by the code they generate. Moreover, these behavioral fingerprints persist after removing explicit self-disclosed markers, demonstrating that attribution relies largely on latent stylistic characteristics. These findings show that reliable attribution of AI coding agents is feasible and clarify the practical trade-offs between attribution accuracy and the information required to achieve it.

</details>


### [242] [Registry Descriptions Go Stale Unevenly: An 89-Day Measurement of Model Context Protocol Drift, and Why Drift-Ranked Re-Auditing Under-Covers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00997)
*Gautam Bharti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对MCP官方注册表进行了88.6天、120次观测的纵向研究（覆盖19,099个服务器），发现基于历史漂移排序的重新审计策略只能捕捉约20%的描述变更服务器（top-5%预算下），远低于描述符漂移（~27%）。核心结论是：内容绑定（哈希变化时立即重新验证）加定期全量扫描才是有效的审计控制，历史漂移排序最多是部分且对新增服务器盲目的控制手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP生态安全研究均为单一时间点的注册表审计，但没有研究评估审计所依据的注册表描述文本的时效性。如果一个描述的保质期很短，那么基于该描述的审计发现很快就会过时，这是一个必要但不充分的条件。作者旨在量化描述级审计发现的有效期限。

Method: 在88.6天内对官方MCP注册表进行120次连续观测，重建了从3,510增长到18,966个服务器的19,099个不同服务器的面板数据。计算描述变更的分布、寿命，并评估不同再审计策略的覆盖率：基于历史漂移排序、内容绑定（哈希触发）和定期全量扫描。

Result: 只有8.6%的服务器会重写描述（对比描述符为24.8%）；top-5%历史漂移排名只能捕获~20%的描述变更服务器（对比~27%随机描述符漂移），仅覆盖~10%的所有描述变更者。约一半的描述变更发生在新增服务器上，历史排名无法触达。跨至少10个观测区间的服务器中，四分之三从未变更，最活跃的5%产生了61%的变更事件。

Conclusion: 基于历史漂移的再审计排名不是有效的描述级审计控制；内容绑定（描述哈希变化时立即重新验证）配合定期全量扫描才是适合的扫描卫生方案。该结论适用于扫描层面的描述级审计者，而非运行时信任信号。研究发布了面板数据、图表生成器和分析代码供复现。

Abstract: Security studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem share a design: each audits a registry at a single point in time. None reports how long the registry descriptions those audits judged stay current - a necessary condition for any description-level finding to still apply, though not a sufficient one: we measure the shelf-life of the audited text, not the validity of a security finding itself (Sec. 7.1). We reconstruct 120 observations of the official MCP registry over 88.6 days, covering 19,099 distinct servers as it grew from 3,510 to 18,966. Our central result is a policy one: you cannot keep description-level findings current by re-auditing the servers that drift most. At a top-5% re-audit budget, ranking by prior drift catches only ~20% of the previously-seen servers whose description changes in a held-out window - versus ~27% for descriptor drift overall - and only ~10% of all description changers. The limit is not unpredictability: the ranking still buys ~4x lift. It is that the description surface is sparse - 8.6% of servers ever rewrite one, against 24.8% for descriptors - and that roughly half of all description changes land on new arrivals a history ranking cannot reach, so the same lift buys far less coverage. The control that fits is content-binding - revalidate the moment a description's hash moves - plus a sized periodic full-catalog sweep; a drift-history ranking is at best a partial, blind-to-new-arrivals control. This is scanner hygiene for a description-level auditor, not a runtime trust signal. Of servers observed across at least ten intervals, three-quarters never change, the most active 5% generate 61% of all change events, and only 11.9% of a cohort's descriptors change within 30 days; naive compounding predicts 35.8% at 30 days (73% at 89), a heavy-tail overestimate we use only as a diagnostic. We release the panel, figure generator, and analysis code.

</details>


### [243] [What Could the Agent See at 19:05? Generating Temporal Enterprise Scenarios from Real Research and Replaying Them to Evaluate Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01042)
*Tezan Sahu,Himani Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个企业AI Agent评估系统，通过生成随时间演化的企业世界数据，并在任意历史时刻进行"回放"评估，解决了传统离线评估只能基于单一静态快照、无法覆盖不同时间点多种情境的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI Agent面对的是持续变化的数据，答案的正确性取决于提问时刻的数据状态和可见性。传统离线评估仅基于单一静态快照（即片段末端），只能评估一个最终情境，无法覆盖片段中每个时刻产生的不同问题及其正确答案。若为每个时刻单独重建租户快照则成本过高，且单一快照会泄露记录中的未来状态，也无法反映多应用、时间有序的真实工作方式。

Method: 系统通过两个环节解决上述问题：(1) 基于真实研究生成一个拟人驱动的、随时间演化的企业世界；(2) 在任意选定时刻回放该世界以评估任意可插拔Agent。具体地，利用模式推断出的时间描述，通过"确定性+LLM"方式重建每条记录的历史状态；由于可查询时刻是有限的，所有重建结果被预计算并压缩为紧凑的差异缓存，使评估成为快速、可复现的查找操作，无需在评估路径中运行模型。

Result: 论文描述了系统设计、覆盖两个流程（生成与回放）的架构，以及评估企业Agent的初步实践经验，但未给出具体量化实验数据。

Conclusion: 该系统同时弥合了两个差距：既能从真实研究中生成拟人驱动、随时间演变的企业世界，又能在任意时间点回放该世界以评估任意Agent，使企业Agent评估从单快照模式升级为多时刻、多情境的快速可复现评估。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents act across many apps whose data changes continuously, so an answer is correct only relative to what data existed and who could see it at the moment it was asked. Offline evaluation today grades against a single static snapshot, effectively the end of the episode. So, it can only evaluate one situation, the final one, even though every earlier moment of the episode is a different situation that invites its own realistic questions with its own correct answers. Recreating each of those moments as a separate snapshot would mean re-provisioning a whole tenant per instant, which is prohibitively costly; and even a single snapshot leaks future state hidden inside records and cannot represent the multi-app, time-ordered way real work happens. Our system closes two gaps at once: it generates a realistic, persona-driven, temporally-evolving enterprise world from real research, and replays that world at any chosen moment to evaluate any pluggable agent. A schema-inferred temporal description drives a deterministic-plus-LLM rebuild of each record's past state; because the queryable moments are finite, all rebuilds are precomputed into a compact difference cache, making evaluation a fast, reproducible lookup with no model in the path. We describe the design, an architecture spanning both flows, and early experience evaluating enterprise agents.

</details>


### [244] [MADE: Belief-Driven Dual-Agent Coordination for Autonomous Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01189)
*Yicheng Liu,Bolin Zhang,Weiran Liu,Yakun Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MADE，一个双智能体协调系统，用于自动化将开源AI模型部署为可直接调用的API，并引入M2ABench基准（含122个真实模型）进行评测，部署成功率达68.85%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体虽具备强大的通用能力，但在领域特定任务上仍有不足，需要集成外部工具。开源社区有大量异构AI模型，但将其转化为可调用的API成本高且劳动密集，自动化模型部署是弥合模型资源与工具可用性之间差距的关键，但这一长周期多阶段任务尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MADE（Model Automated Deployment Engine）双智能体协调系统。给定模型资源后，MADE迭代地构建和验证部署工件，基于执行反馈更新部署信念，并重新审视无效的上游工件，直到模型成功作为可调用API被服务。同时引入M2ABench基准，包含122个真实世界模型及标准化测试用例。

Result: MADE在M2ABench上达到68.85%的部署成功率，分别超越SWE-agent和OpenHands 13.93和44.26个百分点。代码和数据集已公开。

Conclusion: MADE通过双智能体协调、迭代验证与信念更新的机制，有效解决了从模型资源到可调用API的自动化部署难题，验证了其在真实世界模型上的有效性，为LLM智能体扩展领域能力提供了实用的技术路径。

Abstract: LLM-based agents now have strong general capabilities. However, they still struggle with domain-specific tasks, motivating the integration of external tools to broaden their capabilities. The open-source community offers a vast array of AI models typically released as heterogeneous research artifacts, whereas transforming them into ready-to-call APIs is costly and labor-intensive. Automated model deployment is therefore essential for bridging the gap between model resources and tool usability, yet it remains a long-horizon, multi-stage task that has not been sufficiently explored. To tackle this challenge, we introduce Model Automated Deployment Engine (MADE), a dual-agent coordination system. Specifically, given a model resource, MADE iteratively constructs and validates the deployment artifacts, updates its deployment belief based on execution feedback, and revisits invalid upstream artifacts until the model is successfully served as a ready-to-call API that can then be used by other agents. We further introduce M2ABench, a benchmark for the task of transforming Models to ready-to-call APIs. M2ABench comprises 122 real-world models with standardized test cases for evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that MADE achieves a deployment success rate of 68.85%, outperforming SWE-agent and OpenHands by 13.93 and 44.26 percentage points, respectively. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/HITDiSC/MADE.

</details>


### [245] [Doc2CI: A Multi-Service Study of CI Configuration Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01451)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了DOC2CI基准（包含4个CI服务的3,363个描述到YAML配置对），对14个7B-34B开源模型及GPT-4o/GPT-4.1进行了大规模实证研究，发现LLM生成的CI配置参考精确率极低（≤3.1%），但多数输出可解析为YAML（97%）且仅71%满足服务schema，并提出训练-free的schema引导修复方法可将schema有效性提升至94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采用持续集成（CI）通常需要编写易出错且难以维护的YAML配置。尽管LLM在软件工程中应用日益增多，但其从自然语言生成CI配置的能力在不同服务和模型家族间的表现尚不清楚，缺乏系统的实证研究。

Method: 构建DOC2CI基准（从4个CI服务的官方文档收集3,363个描述到YAML配置对），评估14个7B-34B参数的开源模型与GPT-4o和GPT-4.1，生成超过53,000个配置；同时评估参考对齐和schema有效性，并基于385个配置的手动分析开发失败分类法，研究模型分歧原因；最后提出训练-free的schema引导修复方法和微调方法进行比较。

Result: 参考精确匹配率从未超过3.1%；97%输出可解析为YAML但仅71%满足服务schema；更大模型提升结构有效性，但代码专用模型相比通用模型无一致优势；模型差异主要源于输出完整性（部分模型生成预期片段，部分生成完整工作流）；训练-free的schema引导修复方法将schema有效性提升至94%，而微调提升文档相似性但降低独立有效性。

Conclusion: 相似性与有效性是CI生成的独立目标，当前LLM在CI配置生成上仍难以精确复现参考配置，但通过schema感知的修复方法可显著提升结构有效性。这激励了未来在LLM配置生成中采用schema感知的评估和工具开发。

Abstract: Adopting Continuous Integration (CI) often requires writing YAML configurations that are error-prone and challenging to maintain. Despite increasing LLM use in software engineering, their ability to generate CI configurations from natural language across services and model families remains unclear. This paper presents a large empirical study on using LLMs to generate CI configurations. We introduce DOC2CI, a benchmark of 3,363 description-to-YAML pairs collected from the official documentation of four CI services, and evaluate 14 open-weight models from 7B-34B parameters together with GPT-4o and GPT-4.1, producing over 53,000 configurations. We assess both reference alignment and schema validity to determine whether the generated configurations are structurally valid. We further develop a failure taxonomy from a manual analysis of 385 configurations and examine why LLMs disagree. Across models and services, exact reference reproduction never exceeds 3.1%, and while 97% of outputs parse as YAML, only 71% satisfy service schemas. Larger models improve structural validity, but code specialization provides no consistent advantage over comparable general models. Model differences are driven largely by output completeness: for the same request, some models generate the expected fragment while others produce a full workflow. Finally, a training-free schema-guided repair method improves schema validity to 94%, while fine-tuning improves similarity to documentation but reduces standalone validity. This suggests that similarity and validity are distinct objectives for CI generation and motivate schema-aware evaluation and tooling for LLM-based configuration generation.

</details>


### [246] [An Empirical Investigation of Soft Skills and Corresponding Challenges in International Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01469)
*Sajid Ibrahim Hashmi,Jouni Markkula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对两个焦点小组的实证研究，探讨了国际软件工程教育中团队合作所需的软技能，识别了学生在课程作业中应学习的软技能及在国际环境中面临的挑战，为大学教师和教学专家规划学生小组工作提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发工作需要技术技能和专业实践的结合，而团队合作等软技能在专业软件开发环境中至关重要。软件工程教育中的小组作业为学生提供了学习和实践这些软技能的机会，尤其是在国际化的教育环境中。

Method: 本研究采用实证研究方法，通过两个焦点小组（focus groups）收集数据，重点关注国际软件工程高等教育中团队合作所需的软技能，特别是学生之间的协作情况。

Result: 研究识别了学生在团队实践过程中应学习的多种软技能，以及在国际化环境中开展小组合作所面临的挑战。这些发现为规划和改进学生小组工作提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 研究结果为大学教师和教学法专家有效规划学生小组工作提供了指导方针，有助于在软件工程教育中更好地培养学生的团队合作软技能。

Abstract: Joint software development work requires the application of technical skills and professional practices. Soft skills, such as teamwork, remain essential to succeeding in a professional software development environment. The phenomenon is reflected by group work in SE (software engineering) education, where students can learn and practice teamwork-related soft skills. Therefore, SE education requires students to practice those skills so that they can actively embark on their professional careers in the software industry. In this research study, empirical findings from two focus groups we conducted on group work are presented, with emphasis on the soft skills necessary for teamwork in international software engineering education, specifically student collaboration in higher education. There are several soft skills students should learn as they practice teamwork in their course-related assignments and exercises, as well as the challenges that come with it in international settings. The findings serve as a guideline to university teachers and pedagogy experts to plan student group work more effectively.

</details>


### [247] [Deep Agentic Search for Repository-Level Code Question Answering: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01507)
*Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei,Bora Ilci,Alperen Kayim,Mehmet Egemen Uzun,Berat Can,Kaan Emre Kara,Ozan Orhan,Mehmet S. Aktas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了代码代理在仓库代码定位方面的两种主流方法：基于向量索引的语义搜索和深度代理搜索（即由规划代理委派子代理进行探索的方式）。在SWE-QA基准测试上，语义搜索以65.2%的正确率显著优于深度代理搜索的46.2%，且成本不到后者一半。失败模式分析显示，深度代理搜索的41.8%失败发生在规划者与子代理的交接环节，且这些失败通常是"无声的"——即输出流畅自信但错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度代理搜索（grep-search by subagent）已被Claude Code、Codex、Antigravity等主流代码代理快速采用，被视为良好的上下文工程实践，用以保护主代理免受上下文污染。然而，缺乏充分证据表明该方法确实能产生更优的答案，本文旨在填补这一实证空白。

Method: 在SWE-QA（仓库级代码问答基准）上对比语义搜索与深度代理搜索两种方法的表现，并对每次失败运行进行分类编码，构建失败模式的分类体系，以定位两种方法的差距根源。

Result: 语义搜索正确回答了65.2%的问题，深度代理搜索仅正确回答46.2%；语义搜索每个正确答案的成本不到深度代理搜索的一半。失败分类显示深度代理搜索并未消除失败，反而引入了新型失败——41.8%的失败发生在规划者与子代理之间的交接（hand-off）环节，且这些失败通常是静默的，最终以流畅自信但错误的答案告终。

Conclusion: 深度代理搜索虽然解决了一个真实问题（上下文污染），但其保护并非没有代价。对于可被索引的仓库中的只读问答任务，检索（语义搜索）是更强且更便宜的选择。

Abstract: Code agents spend much of their effort simply locating the right code inside a repository. Two approaches dominate current practice. In Semantic Search, the agent retrieves code blocks from a vector index built from the repository in advance. In Deep Agentic Search (also known as grep-search by subagent), a planning agent delegates the exploration to a separate subagent that works in an isolated context window and returns only a condensed result. The second design, which is considered good context engineering practice, exists to protect the main agent from context pollution (also known as context rot), the loss of accuracy that occurs as unrelated material accumulates in the context window. Recent code agents (such as Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, etc) have adopted it quickly, but there is little evidence on whether it produces better answers. We compare the two approaches on SWE-QA, a benchmark for repository-level code question answering. Semantic search answered 65.2% of questions correctly against 46.2% for deep agentic search, and it produced each correct answer at less than half the cost. To explain the gap, we then coded every failed run into a taxonomy of failure modes. The taxonomy shows that deep agentic search did not remove failures but introduced a new class of them: the single largest share of its failures, 41.8%, occurred at the hand-off between the planner and its sub-agent, and these were usually silent, ending in a fluent and confident answer that was wrong. Deep agentic search addresses a real problem and is now the preferred design in many code agents. However, our results show that the protection it offers may not be free, and that for read-only questions over a repository that can be indexed, retrieval was the stronger and cheaper option.

</details>


### [248] [Algorithm-driven Development: A Proactive Approach to Improving Software Quality and Reducing Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01533)
*Philippe Jawish,Pierre Evrard,Alexandre Lemerle,Adrian Genin,Layal Dergham,Séverin Lanfranchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了算法驱动开发（ADD）方法论，将需求转化为算法流程图并从中系统性地推导验收测试，在达索系统为期四年的工业项目中验证了其效果，实现了95%以上的代码覆盖率、低缺陷密度和稳定的交付节奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程实践中长期面临保证软件质量与满足交付期限、适应需求变化之间的持续挑战，需求到可靠、可测试、可维护软件行为的转化过程存在反复出现的难题。

Method: ADD将需求转化为算法流程图，流程图既作为规范工件又作为技术蓝图，支持利益相关者与开发者之间的共同理解；从流程图中系统性地推导验收测试，将需求建模与自动化测试生成联系起来，从一开始就提供功能场景（包括边界情况）的系统性覆盖。

Result: 在达索系统为期四年的工业项目中，涉及两个开发团队（团队1有22,444行生产代码，团队2分析了157个API），结合纵向质量/交付指标与ADD、TDD和测试后开发实践在不同复杂度API函数上的比较分析。实证数据表明ADD支持持续95%以上的代码覆盖率、QA和发布后阶段均保持低缺陷密度，以及稳定的交付节奏。

Conclusion: ADD有潜力加强工业软件开发环境中需求、测试与实现之间的联系，为软件质量保障提供了一条从需求建模到自动化测试生成的有效路径。

Abstract: Ensuring software quality while meeting deadlines and adapting to evolving requirements is a persistent challenge in software engineering practice. This paper introduces Algorithm-Driven Development (ADD), a methodology developed from industrial practice to address recurring challenges in translating requirements into reliable, testable, and maintainable software behavior. ADD translates requirements into algorithmic flowcharts from which acceptance tests are systematically derived. These flowcharts serve both as specification artifacts and as technical blueprints, supporting shared understanding between stakeholders and developers. By linking requirement modeling with automated test generation, ADD provides systematic coverage of functional scenarios, including edge cases, from the outset of development. The approach was evaluated over a four-year period within an industrial project at Dassault Systèmes, involving two development teams, 22,444 lines of production code for Team 1, and 157 APIs analyzed for Team 2. The evaluation combined longitudinal quality and delivery indicators with a comparative analysis of ADD, TDD, and test-last development practices across API functions of different complexity levels. Empirical data collected from internal lifecycle management and CI/CD systems show that ADD supported sustained code coverage above 95%, low defect density in both QA and post-release phases, and a stable delivery cadence. These findings provide evidence of ADD's potential to strengthen the connection between requirements, testing, and implementation in industrial software development contexts.

</details>


### [249] [How Well Do LLMs Generate Taxonomies in the SE Domain? A Multi-perspective Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01592)
*Sota Nakashima,Yuta Ishimoto,Masanari Kondo,Tao Xiao,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对自动化分类法生成方法在软件工程（SE）领域的应用进行了全面实证评估，比较了TnT-LLM和CLIMB两种方法与五种最新大语言模型，发现TnT-LLM生成的质量与人类专家相当但成本高且复杂，而CLIMB速度更快、成本更低但质量略逊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工构建SE领域的分类法需要大量劳动和领域专家，虽然LLM在其他领域催生了自动化分类法生成方法，但这些方法在技术复杂度高的SE工件上的适用性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 收集7篇SE论文及其公开工件和人类定义分类法，构建多角度评估框架（包括分类法质量、与人类专家分类法的一致性、独立标注可靠性、效率），使用TnT-LLM和CLIMB两种自动化方法与五种SOTA LLM进行对比实验。

Result: TnT-LLM生成的高质量分类法与人类定义相当，但成本更高、运行时间更长且倾向于生成过度复杂的分类法；CLIMB比TnT-LLM快15-40倍、便宜8-49倍，但在需要超越表面相似性的技术推理场景中质量得分较低。

Conclusion: 两种自动化方法在SE领域均有实际应用价值，但研究人员应先用目标数据的子集评估生成分类法的复杂性和成本，再决定采用自动化方法还是人类专家，为SE领域自动化分类法生成提供了系统性理解和可操作的建议。

Abstract: Taxonomies provide a shared conceptual framework for organizing heterogeneous observations in software engineering (SE) research. Manually constructing such taxonomies is labor-intensive and requires annotators with expertise in the SE domain. While advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of automated taxonomy generation methods outside the SE domain, their applicability to technically complex SE artifacts remains unclear. In this experience paper, we present the first comprehensive empirical evaluation of how state-of-the-art automated methods perform on SE artifacts through a multi-perspective evaluation framework, including taxonomy quality, alignment with taxonomies defined by human experts, reliability under independent annotation, and efficiency. To support this evaluation, we systematically collect seven SE papers with publicly available artifacts and human-defined taxonomies, and conduct experiments using two automated methods (TnT-LLM and CLIMB) with five state-of-the-art LLMs. Our evaluation reveals a clear trade-off: TnT-LLM constructs high-quality taxonomies comparable to human-defined ones but incurs substantially higher cost and runtime and tends to generate overly complex taxonomies, whereas CLIMB is 15--40$\times$ faster and 8--49$\times$ cheaper but tends to score lower on quality when technical inference beyond surface-level similarity is required. These findings suggest that TnT-LLM and CLIMB can be used in practical situations in the SE domain, while researchers should first assess the complexity of the generated taxonomies and their cost using a subset of the target data to decide whether to use automated methods or human experts. Our work represents a first step toward a systematic understanding of automated taxonomy generation in SE, offering actionable insights for future research and practice.

</details>


### [250] [AI-assisted Script Management for Requirements Elicitation Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01640)
*Anmol Singhal,Paulo Carvalho,Travis Breaux*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI辅助的需求获取工作流，将理论驱动的脚本生成与实时主题覆盖跟踪、按需追问问题生成相结合。准实验研究表明，与仅培训无AI的条件相比，AI辅助能生成更高质量的脚本、更深入地追问每个主题、产生更精细的目标模型，尽管覆盖的主题数量较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈要求访谈者在实时响应利益相关者的同时，平衡主题覆盖、主动倾听和自适应追问。虽然已有工作探索了AI对孤立访谈任务（如脚本生成、追问问题生成）的支持，但关于集成式支持如何影响访谈过程以及产生何种需求工件的研究仍然不足，且脚本管理（帮助访谈者实时追踪主题覆盖并决定何时深入追问）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种AI辅助的需求获取工作流，结合三部分：(1) 基于业务目标的理论引导脚本生成；(2) 实时主题覆盖追踪支持；(3) 按需追问问题生成。采用组间准实验设计，比较无训练且AI辅助的条件与有训练但无AI辅助的条件，并使用基于需求获取最佳实践制定的评价量规进行评估。

Result: AI生成的脚本得分显著高于纯培训脚本（92.8 vs 74.8，满分100）。AI辅助访谈覆盖更少主题（9.6 vs 14.5），但覆盖更多脚本化问题（86% vs 69%），每个主题的追问更多（3.43 vs 1.15），产生更精细的目标模型（最低层目标比例0.653 vs 0.598）。参与者普遍认为脚本管理有用，其中主题追踪被评为最有用的功能（86%认同）。

Conclusion: AI辅助条件与仅培训条件在访谈轨迹和产生的需求工件上存在显著差异。AI辅助工作流可被定位为需求获取的支撑脚手架，为未来研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews require interviewers to balance topic coverage, active listening, and adaptive probing while responding to stakeholders in real time. Although prior work has explored AI support for isolated interviewing tasks, such as script generation and follow-up question generation, little is known about how integrated support affects the interview and what requirements artifacts emerge. Furthermore, script management---which helps the interviewer track topic coverage in real time and decide when to probe further---remains underexplored. This paper presents an AI-assisted elicitation workflow that combines theory-guided script generation grounded in business goals with live support for topic coverage tracking and on-demand follow-up question generation. We evaluate the workflow in a between-subjects quasi-experimental study comparing a no-training, AI-assisted condition with a training, AI-unassisted condition. Based on a rubric derived from elicitation best practices, the AI-generated scripts score higher than training-only scripts (92.8 vs. 74.8 out of 100). AI-assisted interviews cover fewer topics (9.6 vs. 14.5), cover more scripted questions (86% vs. 69%), ask more follow-ups per topic (3.43 vs. 1.15), and produce more refined goal models (lowest-level goal fraction 0.653 vs. 0.598). Participants find script management useful, rating topic tracking as the most useful workflow feature (86% agreement). Collectively, these results show that the AI-assisted condition is associated with a different interview trajectory and different elicited requirements than a training-only condition, positioning AI-assisted workflows as elicitation scaffolds for future studies.

</details>


### [251] [Coding Agents as Test-Suite Auditors: Finding What Official Suites Miss While Approaching What They Catch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01715)
*Shuyang Xie,Shuxiao Xie,Feng Zhu,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用现成编码代理作为测试套件审计器的方法，通过构建对抗性测试套件来识别官方评测套件遗漏的有缺陷提交。该方法在AtCoder上发现906个"通过官方评测但实际有bug"的提交，并在无官方套件的Codeforces问题上优于所有复现基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 官方在线评测的判定结果和基于其构建的数据集被视为代码大语言模型评估和训练的"金标准"，但先前审计发现官方测试套件会接受有缺陷的提交。然而这些审计仅止于发出警告，没有提供实际可行的补救方案。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 使用现成编码代理作为测试套件审计器，构建对抗性测试套件来暴露官方套件遗漏的bug，并在无官方套件时提供测试套件；(2) 认证链用于在不依赖官方判题器的情况下确定每个代理标记的提交是否真正有bug：多个独立编写的已接受解决方案在每次测试上输出一致，暴力解法解决分歧，逐题验证器确认每个失败输入合法。

Result: 在AtCoder的20,375个已审计接受提交中，一个代理识别出589个经认证的"接受但有bug"的提交；扩展到五个代理后得到906个这样的提交的下限。五个代理在官方套件捕获的逻辑bug上覆盖率差距均在1.7个百分点以内。在无官方套件的Codeforces问题上，该方法在所有测试输入预算下均优于五个复现基线。

Conclusion: 当存在官方套件时，代理可审计套件的充分性而非默认为可靠的；当不存在官方套件时，代理生成的套件能捕获最多的有bug提交。该方法为官方评测套件不可靠的问题提供了实际可行的补救手段。

Abstract: Online-judge verdicts and the datasets and benchmarks built on them are treated as ground truth for evaluating and training large language models for code. Yet prior audits have sounded a warning: official suites accept buggy submissions. These audits, however, stop at the warning and offer no practical remedy. Our remedy has two parts: an off-the-shelf coding agent, serving as a test-suite auditor, both builds adversarial test suites to expose what official suites miss and supplies these suites where no official suite exists; a certification chain determines whether each agent-flagged submission is genuinely buggy without relying on the official judge: multiple independently written accepted solutions agree on the expected output for every test, brute-force solutions settle disagreements, and a per-problem validator certifies each failing input legal. One such agent identifies 589 verified accepted-but-buggy submissions among AtCoder's 20,375 audited accepted submissions; extending the same certification to all five agents yields a union floor of 906 such submissions. Five agents, scored separately, each stay within 1.7pp of official-suite coverage on logic bugs those suites catch. On post-cutoff Codeforces problems with no available official suites, the same test-building method leads all five reproduced baselines at every tested input budget. Where an official suite exists, the agent audits suite adequacy instead of assuming it; where none exists, agent suites catch the most buggy submissions among methods we reproduced and tested.

</details>


### [252] [Effective and Efficient Context Retrieval via Partial Dependency Graph for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01927)
*Zhongxin Liu,Zhonghao Jiang,Zhifan Ye,Haoye Wang,Jiakun Liu,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DyRetriever，一种基于部分依赖图的仓库级代码生成上下文检索方法。它利用LLM选择入口函数并在代码依赖图上进行多跳推理，通过语义理解验证函数的帮助性，按需构建部分图而非静态全局图。集成后形成DyCoder，在CoderEval和DevEval上分别取得25.63%和59.73%的相对Pass@1提升，且比静态图方法快7.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的仓库级代码生成方法存在两大问题：相似度检索难以获取目标函数依赖的代码片段，而图检索虽能建模依赖但依赖手工规则和静态全局图，构建维护成本高且灵活性差。

Method: DyRetriever首先利用LLM选择一组入口函数，然后在代码依赖图上进行多跳推理；推理过程中利用LLM的语义理解验证函数是否对生成目标函数有帮助，避免手工规则。按需构建部分依赖图并在使用后丢弃，降低构建和维护成本。

Result: 将DyRetriever与相似度检索器集成形成DyCoder，在CoderEval和DevEval上相比现有RAG方法分别获得25.63%和59.73%的相对Pass@1提升，且比基于静态依赖图构建的基线快7.4倍。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类开发者隐式构建部分依赖图并迭代检索的行为，DyRetriever在检索效果、灵活性和效率方面均优于现有方法，验证了按需构建部分依赖图的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based repository-level code generation aims to generate code using the context available in a software repository, requiring LLMs to reason over complex code dependencies. Due to limited context windows and insufficient repository-specific understanding, LLMs typically rely on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate relevant code. Early RAG approaches primarily employ similarity-based retrieval, which often fails to retrieve code snippets that the target function depends on. Recent work introduces graph-based retrieval to model such dependencies, but typically relies on manually designed rules and static global graphs, leading to limited flexibility and high construction and maintenance costs. In contrast, human developers collect helpful context by implicitly constructing a partial dependency graph and iteratively inspecting along it. Inspired by this behavior, we propose DyRetriever, an efficient context retrieval method via partial dependency graphs. DyRetriever uses an LLM to first select a set of entry-point functions and then perform multi-hop reasoning along the code dependency graph. During multi-hop reasoning, it uses the LLM's semantic understanding to validate whether a function can help generate the target function, eliminating manually designed rules and enabling flexibility across scenarios. Instead of statically constructing a global dependency graph, DyRetriever builds a partial graph on demand and discards it after use, reducing construction and maintenance costs. We integrate DyRetriever with a similarity-based code retriever to build DyCoder and evaluate it on CoderEval and DevEval. Experimental results show that DyCoder achieves relative Pass@1 improvements of 25.63% and 59.73% on CoderEval and DevEval, respectively, compared with existing RAG-based methods, while being 7.4x faster than baselines based on static dependency graph construction.

</details>


### [253] [TELLER: Non-intrusive Cross-Layer Root-Cause Analysis for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01975)
*Ruilin Xu,Junyi Li,Pengfei Chen,Zongxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TELLER是一个面向LLM推理系统的非侵入式根因分析框架，通过收集NVTX/CUPTI迹线和服务日志，重构每个请求的调用链树并对齐日志与执行步骤，结合依赖感知的因果上下文切片和Trace Pair Encoding（TPE）分词器进行压缩，最终利用多模态根因模型联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释。实验表明适度的TPE词表可将迹线长度压缩80%以上同时保持最佳诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理从离线工作负载转变为持续运行的软件服务，但根因分析困难，因为单个请求跨越推理引擎、Python/C++后端、主机CUDA API、GPU内核和分布式通信等多个层次。现有分析器仅暴露原始时间线，而基于日志的诊断往往缺失跨层执行语义和请求级结构。

Method: TELLER首先非侵入式地收集NVTX/CUPTI迹线和服务日志，重构逐请求的调用链树并对齐日志与执行步骤；引入依赖感知的因果上下文切片保留父子结构、时间顺序和通信关系；提出Trace Pair Encoding (TPE)分词器将切片压缩为带父节点、深度和时长属性的结构token序列；结合数值候选定位与多模态根因模型，联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释。

Result: 在多节点GPU推理负载上，适度的TPE词表可将每步迹线长度减少80%以上，同时在横向（跨节点通信）和纵向（节点内执行栈）视图上达到最佳整体性能；过度的压缩会显著降低诊断质量。在低故障先验、强化基线、模态消融、解释质量检查和追踪开销等进一步分析中表现出色。

Conclusion: TELLER提供了一种实用的LLM推理根因分析的分诊和证据定位基础平台，在迹线压缩效率与诊断质量之间存在明确的权衡，适度的压缩策略可在显著减少数据量的同时保持最佳诊断性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has evolved from an offline workload into a continuously operated software service, yet root-cause analysis remains difficult because a single request spans the inference engine, Python/C++ backend, host CUDA APIs, GPU kernels, and distributed communication. Existing profilers expose raw timelines, while log-based diagnosis often misses cross-layer execution semantics and request-level structure. We present TELLER, a non-intrusive Trace- and Log-aware LLM inference Root-cause analysis framework. TELLER first collects NVTX/CUPTI traces and service logs without modifying model binaries, then reconstructs per-request call-chain trees and aligns log lines with the corresponding execution steps. We introduce a dependency-aware causal-context slice that preserves parent-child structure, temporal order, and communication relations, and a Trace Pair Encoding (TPE) tokenizer that compresses such slices into compact structural token sequences with parent, depth, and duration attributes. On top of these representations, TELLER combines numeric candidate localization with a multimodal root-cause model that jointly predicts abnormal steps, localizes suspicious operators, and generates natural-language explanations. Experiments on multi-node GPU inference workloads show a clear compression-accuracy trade-off: a moderate TPE vocabulary reduces per-step trace length by more than 80% while achieving the best overall performance on both horizontal (cross-node communication) and vertical (within-node execution stack) views, whereas more aggressive compression substantially degrades diagnosis quality. Further analyses under low-fault priors, strengthened baselines, modality ablations, explanation-quality checks, and tracing overhead show that TELLER provides a practical triage and evidence-localization substrate for LLM inference RCA.

</details>


### [254] [VulnGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02001)
*Kexing Ji,Jiachen Liu,Enze Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Keke Lian,Yongheng Liu,Lei Zhang,Tian Dong,Hao Chen,Wang Bin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VulnGym，一个面向编码智能体的仓库级漏洞检测基准，包含184个公告、408个漏洞条目和23个仓库，并带有行级漏洞追踪标注，用于端到端评估和局限性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞检测基准主要针对预选代码片段进行分类评估，无法评估编码智能体在仓库级场景下的自主探索和漏洞定位能力；同时缺乏细粒度的漏洞追踪标注，难以探究检测过程中的能力瓶颈。

Method: VulnGym将对齐的GitHub安全公告与对应漏洞版本仓库相结合，构建了包含408个漏洞条目的数据集，每个条目标注了行级入口点、关键操作和漏洞追踪。定义了端到端检测任务和三个基于oracle的子任务，用于联合评估漏洞检测能力并诊断代码定位和证据构建中的局限。

Result: 评估表明当前编码智能体在端到端仓库级漏洞检测和精确支持追踪构建方面均表现有限。

Conclusion: VulnGym揭示了现有编码智能体在仓库级漏洞检测中的能力瓶颈，为后续改进提供了细粒度的评估基准和诊断框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-based vulnerability detection have shown promising results, while coding agents further extend this capability from isolated code snippets to complete repositories. This shift requires agents to autonomously explore repositories and locate vulnerability-relevant code, instead of performing detection on preselected functions. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on vulnerability classification over preselected code snippets, limiting their ability to evaluate coding agents in repository-level vulnerability detection. Moreover, without fine-grained vulnerability trace annotations, the capability limitations underlying the detection process remain difficult to explore. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{VulnGym}, a real-world repository-level benchmark for evaluating vulnerability detection by coding agents. VulnGym aligns reviewed GitHub advisories with their corresponding vulnerable version repositories. It contains 184 advisories and 408 vulnerability entries across 23 repositories, with each entry annotated with line-level entry points, critical operations, and vulnerability traces. Using this fine-grained ground truth, VulnGym defines an end-to-end detection task and three oracle-based subtasks to jointly evaluate vulnerability detection and diagnose limitations in code localization and evidence construction. Our evaluation indicates that current coding agents remain limited in both end-to-end repository-level vulnerability detection and the construction of accurate supporting traces.

</details>


### [255] [Pretraining on Call Graphs: When Binary Analysis Tasks Profit From Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02084)
*Samuel Valenzuela,Johannes Kinder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究剖析了基于调用图的嵌入优化对二进制函数嵌入模型的影响，发现其在二进制代码相似性检测（BCSD）上的改进并不能泛化到下游任务，且语义相似性优化与句法任务性能呈负相关，调用图上下文在命名空间相关函数和模型初始表现不佳的场景中最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明利用调用图信息可以改进二进制函数嵌入，但对其在不同下游任务中的泛化能力及具体影响机制缺乏系统性剖析。作者希望深入理解这些嵌入优化的实际利弊，揭示其在语义和句法任务上的不同表现。

Method: 作者使用多个基于图的模型，对两个最先进的二进制函数嵌入模型生成的嵌入进行实验。通过集成过程间上下文信息，评估BCSD及多种语义和句法下游任务的性能，并对数据集进行解释性分析。

Result: 调用图增强在BCSD上有改进，但无法泛化到下游任务；语义相似性优化与句法任务性能呈负相关；调用图增强显著提升嵌入鲁棒性，尤其在初始模型表现不佳时；对命名空间相关函数的增益大于独立逻辑函数。

Conclusion: 调用图嵌入优化并非普遍适用，其价值高度依赖具体任务场景。在下游语义或句法任务中，BCSD上的改进可能以牺牲其他任务性能为代价。调用图上下文最适合上下文依赖型场景（如命名空间相关函数），而在模型本身表现良好的情况下收益有限。

Abstract: Binary function embedding models are trained to encode the semantics of binary code in such a way that they can be generalized to a variety of reverse engineering tasks, such as binary code search, vulnerability detection, or malware classification. While many models only take the function in question as contextual input, there have been successful attempts to improve function embeddings by leveraging information from the call graph. In this study, we dissect the implications of these embedding refinements. We conduct experiments using a range of graph-based models on the embeddings generated by two state-of-the-art binary function embedding models. Integrating inter-procedural context, we show that improvements on binary code similarity detection (BCSD) will not necessarily generalize to downstream tasks, neither of semantic nor of syntactic nature. More generally, we find that optimizing for semantic similarity tasks correlates with worse performance on syntactic tasks. By conducting an explanatory analysis on the dataset, we find that the call graph-based enhancements significantly enhance the robustness of embeddings, particularly in scenarios where the initial models struggle. Furthermore, we observe that the added context is more beneficial for namespace-related functions than for those focused on individual logic, confirming that the call graph can be leveraged most effectively in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [256] [Lossless Tensor Compression as Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02162)
*Jieke Shi,Junda He,Wenjia Jiang,Weifeng Sun,Shidong Pan,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,Zhou Yang,Thong Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Brevis是一种将无损张量压缩建模为程序合成的方法，通过类型化领域专用语言（DSL）捕捉张量中的重复结构和浮点字段模式，用可逆算子实现比特级精确重建。在10个公开模型检查点上，Brevis将2.13 TB数据压缩至1.41 TB（降低33.93%），比zstd、gzip等四种通用压缩器以及ZipNN、DFloat11等专用压缩器产生更小的归档，同时实现3.60 GB/s压缩和6.61 GB/s解压速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型检查点在数量和规模上持续增长，导致归档、传输和部署成本越来越高。通用压缩器忽略张量结构信息，而现有的张量专用压缩器依赖于固定且格式特定的流程，缺乏灵活性和通用性。因此需要一种能同时利用张量结构化特征且具备泛化能力的无损压缩方法。

Method: Brevis将无损张量压缩形式化为程序合成问题。设计了一个类型化DSL，通过一组可逆算子捕获重复区域和浮点字段等常见张量结构。给定一个张量，Brevis合成一个自包含的DSL程序以比特级精确重建该张量。利用从少量代表性张量样本中学习的检查点专用先验知识，指导有界A*搜索合成紧凑程序，压缩后的程序可以直接执行以实现比特级精确解压。

Result: 在10个覆盖语言、音频和图像生成模型的公开检查点上，Brevis将2.13 TB检查点数据减少至1.41 TB，实现33.93%的存储压缩。其产生的归档比zstd、gzip等四种通用压缩器小最多30.87%，也比张量专用压缩器ZipNN和DFloat11产生的归档更小。在实用并发配置下，压缩吞吐达3.60 GB/s，解压吞吐达6.61 GB/s，且保持每个源字节完整无损。

Conclusion: Brevis通过程序合成的思路统一了张量压缩中的结构利用与通用性需求，在压缩率、速度和无损保证方面均优于现有方法，为大规模模型检查点的高效存储与传输提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Model checkpoints are growing in both number and size, which makes archival, transfer, and deployment increasingly costly. General-purpose compressors can reduce storage requirements but ignore tensor structure, whereas existing tensor-specific compressors rely on fixed and format-specific pipelines. We present Brevis, which formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis. We design a typed domain-specific language (DSL) that captures recurring tensor structures, such as repeated regions and floating-point fields, through a set of reversible operators. Given a tensor, Brevis synthesizes a self-contained DSL program that reconstructs it bit-exactly. A checkpoint-specific production prior, learned from a small representative sample of tensors, guides a bounded A* search to synthesize compact programs, which can later be executed directly for bit-exact decompression. On 10 public checkpoints spanning language, audio, and image generation models, Brevis reduces 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB, a 33.93% storage reduction. It produces archives up to 30.87% smaller than those of four general-purpose compressors, including zstd and gzip, and smaller archives than the tensor-specific compressors ZipNN and DFloat11. Under a practical concurrency configuration, Brevis achieves 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

</details>


### [257] [ECLAIR: A Causally-Grounded AI Framework for Scientific Discovery in Empirical Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02323)
*Alejandro Velasco,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Dipin Khati,David N. Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ECLAIR框架，一个基于因果推理的AI框架，将大语言模型（LLMs）作为科学代理集成到软件工程实证研究的全过程中（从假设生成到分析解释），并通过人机协作设计保障推理的稳健性。案例研究表明，在两种LLM中，指令风格、更长few-shot和签名增强提示对代码生成准确率产生小幅负面因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的实证研究长期遵循科学方法，但现代软件系统的复杂性常常阻碍其系统性应用。作者旨在将LLMs以因果推理为原则、以人机协作为保障的方式嵌入科学流程，解决自动化推理不稳健的风险，并为软件现象的因果解释提供原则性基础。

Method: 提出ECLAIR框架，将LLMs视为遵循因果推断原则的主动科学代理，覆盖从假设生成到分析与解释的科学过程各阶段；采用人机协作（human-in-the-loop）设计来防范自动化推理的风险。通过案例研究验证框架，考察提示设计对两种LLM代码生成准确率的影响。

Result: 在两种LLM模型上均发现：指令风格、更长few-shot以及签名增强的提示对代码生成准确率产生小幅负面因果效应，展示了因果推理如何为软件现象提供解释性基础。该研究是首个将LLMs嵌入SE科学方法的结构化因果框架。

Conclusion: ECLAIR为软件工程中AI辅助实证研究提供了首个基于因果推理的结构化方法论，围绕实证SE研究的认识论需求设计，为严格的AI辅助研究建立了基础。

Abstract: The scientific method has long guided empirical research in Software Engineering (SE), but the complexity of modern software systems often hinders its systematic application. This paper introduces _ECLAIR_, a causally grounded AI framework that integrates Large Language Models (_LLMs_) into every stage of the scientific process, from hypothesis generation to analysis and interpretation. _ECLAIR_ treats _LLMs_ as active **scientific agents** operating under the principles of causal inference, within a human-in-the-loop design that safeguards against the risks of unsound automated reasoning. We demonstrate the framework through a case study examining how prompt design influences code generation accuracy in two _LLMs_. Results show that, for both models, instruction-style, longer few-shot, and signature-augmented prompts yield small negative causal effects on accuracy, illustrating how causal reasoning provides a principled foundation for explaining _why_ software phenomena occur. This study presents the first causally grounded structured methodology for embedding _LLMs_ within the scientific method in SE, designed around the epistemological demands of empirical SE research, establishing a basis for rigorous AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [258] [On Automated and Explainable Provenance of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02329)
*Alejandro Velasco,Nathan Wintersgill,Trevor Stalnaker,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个研究愿景，主张下一代代码生成AI工具必须建立在可解释溯源的基础上，即通过自动化的、事后可追溯的技术，将生成的代码与其提示组件、训练数据实例、全局数据特征和内部模型组件联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成AI在提升软件开发效率的同时带来了透明度危机：AI生成代码的起源对开发者、组织和合规专业人员而言不透明。现有缓解措施仅在事后标记问题输出，却无法解释模型为何产生这些输出，也无法指导未来的改进。

Method: 基于美国NSF资助的研究基金，通过四维度可追溯性（提示组件、训练数据实例、全局数据特征、内部模型组件）来刻画问题；结合大规模实证研究与事后因果分析和可解释性技术；并从软件开发人员、模型用户和合规/法律专业人员三个群体收集经验证据。

Result: 实证研究表明，溯源信息是实际需求，而当前工具无法提供；论文从四个可追溯性维度刻画了问题，勾勒了研究计划，并确定了社区需解决的关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 下一代代码生成AI工具必须将可解释溯源作为基础设计原则，而非事后补救措施；这需要学术界和产业界共同应对该领域的关键开放挑战以实现这一愿景。

Abstract: Generative AI for code generation has transformed software development, but it has also introduced a critical transparency problem: the origins of AI-generated code are opaque to the developers who use it, the organizations that deploy it, and the compliance professionals responsible for ensuring its legal and quality standards. Existing mitigations flag problematic outputs after the fact without explaining why a model produced them or how future generation could be improved. We present a research vision, grounded in a U.S. NSF-funded research grant, that argues that the next generation of CodeGenAI tools must be built on a foundation of explainable provenance: automated, post-hoc traceability that links generated code back to the prompt components, training data instances, global data features, and internal model components that caused its generation. We grounded this vision in empirical evidence from studies of software developers, model users, and compliance/legal professionals, which show that provenance information is a practical necessity that current tools do not provide. We characterize the problem across four traceability dimensions, outline a research program combining large-scale empirical studies with post-hoc causal and interpretability techniques, and identify the key open challenges that the community must address to realize this vision.

</details>


### [259] [Profitability of Open-Source Software Product Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02398)
*Shivendu P. Singh,Narayan Ramasubbu,Chris F. Kemerer,M. Zia Hydari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2001至2025年间977家美国高科技企业，发现开源软件采纳平均使企业毛利率提高4-5%，其收益主要通过劳动生产率的提升实现。但只有当外部贡献者承担约35%的工作量时收益才会显现，且需要与企业资源禀赋相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科技企业在GitHub等平台上开放产品开发，邀请外部开发者参与贡献。但这种产品开发的开放性是否能为企业带来经济回报，尚缺乏系统性实证证据，本文旨在回答这一关键问题。

Method: 基于2001至2025年间977家美国高科技企业的数据进行分析，考察开源采纳与企业毛利率之间的关系，并探究劳动生产率和知识整合的作用机制，同时使用配置分析（如QCA类方法）识别高盈利路径的必要条件。

Result: 开源采纳平均使企业毛利率提高4-5%；收益主要源于更高的劳动生产率，企业将外部贡献者的多样化知识融入内部工作流程，在不显著增加劳动力成本的情况下拓宽了组织知识基础；但收益仅在外部志愿者承担约35%工作量时显现，且持续的内部研发投入是所有高盈利配置中的核心必要条件。

Conclusion: 管理者应将开源视为与企业资源相匹配的知识整合战略，而非仅仅是降低外部劳动成本的工具；政策制定者应重视开源生态系统的经济价值，并制定政策培育熟练的志愿者社区和公私合作的开源基础设施。

Abstract: Many technology firms now build software products in the open, inviting outside developers to contribute alongside their employees on platforms like GitHub. Does this openness in product development pay off? Analyzing 977 U.S. high-tech firms from 2001 to 2025, this study finds that open-source adoption raised firms' gross margins by 4-5% on average. These gains flow largely through higher labor productivity, as firms integrate external contributors' diverse knowledge into internal workflows, broadening the organizational knowledge base without a commensurate rise in labor costs. However, the payoff emerges only when outside volunteers supply a meaningful share of the work (around 35% in this sample), and hinges on the firm's resource configuration. While there are multiple pathways to profitability, pairing open-source product development with sustained internal R&D is a core condition present in all high-profitability configurations. For managers, the lesson is to treat open source as a knowledge-integration strategy matched to the firm's resources, not merely a means of sourcing external effort at lower cost. For policymakers, the findings underscore the broad economic value of open-source ecosystems and the need for policies that foster skilled volunteer communities and public-private open-source infrastructure.

</details>


### [260] [SWE-Touch: Benchmarking Coding Agents When Users Touch the Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02499)
*Yuqiao Tan,Jinxiang Meng,Fangyu Lei,Minzheng Wang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Touch框架，通过在共享工作区注入与任务冲突的"反向编辑"来压力测试编码代理对代码变更的感知与响应能力。实验表明，冲突编辑使九个编码模型在SWE-bench Verified上的平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，且退化在更长周期的基准测试中持续存在，揭示了当前编码代理缺乏共享工作区协作所需的状态感知和自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实软件开发中，编码代理需要在用户可能随时检查和修改代码的共享工作区中运行，但现有仓库级基准测试通常让代理独立工作或仅允许用户通过消息参与，难以评估代理对代码变化的感知与响应能力。

Method: 提出SWE-Touch框架：(1)从多条修复轨迹中挖掘任务关键区域；(2)使用独立的用户补丁生成器构造与任务完成冲突但合理的编辑（Counter-Edits）；(3)当代理触及相关代码时注入带上下文的用户消息。在SWE-bench Verified上评估九个编码模型，并在SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE上进行更长周期任务实验。

Result: Counter-Edits使SWE-bench Verified的平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，退化同样出现在两个更长周期基准上。轨迹分析表明，代理可能保留冲突代码或替换代码但未充分重新检查仓库、未用针对性测试验证修改后的代码。

Conclusion: 当前编码代理的强自主性能尚不能保证共享工作区协作所需的状态感知和自适应行为，未来优化应聚焦于检测工作区变化、协调冲突编辑与任务目标、以及验证受影响行为三个关键能力。

Abstract: Real-world software development requires coding agents to operate in shared workspaces where users may inspect and modify code during an ongoing task, yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate agents working alone or restrict user participation to messages. This leads us to ask: how do coding agents understand and respond to code changes in a shared workspace? We introduce SWE-Touch, a framework that stress-tests this setting through validated Counter-Edits: plausible edits to task-relevant code that conflict with task completion. SWE-Touch mines task-critical regions from multiple repair trajectories, uses a separate User Patch Generator to construct the edits, and injects them with contextual user messages when agents reach the relevant code. We evaluate nine coding models on SWE-bench Verified, with additional experiments on longer-horizon tasks from SWE-Bench Pro and DeepSWE. Counter-Edit lowers average resolve rate by 7.7 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, with degradation also persisting on both longer-horizon benchmarks. Trajectory analysis links these failures to limited awareness of the evolving workspace: agents may retain conflicting code or replace it without sufficiently re-inspecting the repository and validating the revised code with targeted tests. These findings show that strong autonomous performance does not yet ensure the state awareness and adaptive behavior needed for shared-workspace collaboration, and point to detecting workspace changes, reconciling conflicting edits with the task, and verifying the affected behavior as key capabilities for future optimization.

</details>


### [261] [Decomposing the Doer Effect in Programming Practice: Code Writing Stands Out Among Active Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02541)
*Arun Balajiee Lekshmi Narayanan,Gillian Gold,Jordan Barria-Pineda,Quinn K Wolter,Peter Brusilovsky,Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨"行动者效应"是否适用于计算机科学教学，并分析不同形式的主动练习类型是否效果不同。基于334名学生、11个学期的Java教学日志数据，研究发现主动练习活动与学习成果的关联是被动活动的3.2倍，其中代码编写与后测成绩提升的关联最强，而其他活动类型无显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"行动者效应"文献将"做"仅定义为主动练习，但未区分不同形式的主动练习之间可能存在的效果差异。作者希望探究在计算机科学教学中，行动者效应是否成立，以及某些主动练习形式（如代码编写）是否比其他形式更有效。

Method: 分析334名学生在11个学期中学习入门及中级Java课程时使用的互动练习系统日志数据。该系统包含五种内容类型：代码编写、代码追踪、代码补全、代码可视化和代码解释。通过对比主动练习与被动活动的学习成果，并进一步比较不同主动练习类型的关联强度。

Result: 主动练习活动与学习成果的关联是被动活动的3.2倍。在各类主动练习中，代码编写与前测到后测成绩提升的关联最为显著，而其他主动练习类型（代码追踪、代码补全、代码可视化、代码解释）均未表现出可比的关联强度。

Conclusion: 研究证实行动者效应在计算机科学教育中成立，同时强调具有挑战性且提供反馈支持的练习活动（如代码编写问题）对学习效果尤为重要，为教学实践和练习系统设计提供了启示。

Abstract: The "doer effect" suggests that actively doing practice activities is more strongly associated with learning outcomes than passively viewing content. In the doer effect literature, "doing" refers specifically to active practice. However, this categorization treats different forms of active practice as equivalent, leaving open whether some types of active practice are more effective than others. In this paper, we investigate whether the doer effect extends to computer science instruction and whether some forms of doing stand out compared to other forms. We analyze log data from 334 students across 11 semesters of introductory and intermediate Java who used an interactive practice system with five content types: Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, and Code Explanations. Consistent with prior doer effect work, we find that active practice activities were associated with 3.2 times better learning outcomes than passive activities. Interestingly, among the active practice, code writing was the most strongly associated with improved posttest performance, while no other activity type showed a comparable association. These results highlight the importance of challenging, feedback-supported practice activities, such as code writing problems.

</details>


### [262] [ACEM: A Cost Estimation Model for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02582)
*Mohammad El-Ramly*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ACEM（Agentic成本估算模型），将智能体软件开发成本分解为LLM、HITL和基础设施三个可加维度，并引入Revision Factor、Context Factor和HITL Intensity Score三个新构造来建模智能体开发的非确定性成本动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件成本估算模型（如COCOMO II、功能点、故事点）假设开发工作量主要由人类劳动驱动，但智能体软件工程中AI代理承担了大量实现工作，引入了token消耗、人类监督和基础设施等新的非确定性成本维度，现有模型无法适应这一范式转变。

Method: ACEM将总成本分解为LLM成本、HITL成本和基础设施成本三个维度；引入Revision Factor（RF）建模输出拒绝和重试带来的token开销、Context Factor（CF）捕捉上下文累积导致的token消耗增长、HITL Intensity Score（HIS）作为四级监督分类方案；并将Use Case Points、Story Points和Function Points映射为估算的token消耗量。

Result: 论文提出的是一个完整的模型结构和校准方法论，模型常数以符号形式保留待经验数据填充。作为早期提案，作者邀请研究社区通过真实项目数据对模型进行校准、测试和扩展。

Conclusion: ACEM提供了一个连接传统规模度量指标与智能体开发成本结构的桥梁框架，使组织能够复用现有的项目范围设定数据进行智能体成本预测，但模型仍需经验数据验证和校准才能实际应用。

Abstract: Traditional software cost estimation models, such as COCOMO II, Function Points, and Story Points, assume that development effort is primarily driven by human labor in design, coding, and testing. Agentic software engineering, where autonomous AI agents perform substantial implementation work and humans focus on planning, specification, and validation, challenges this assumption. New cost dimensions arise: large language model (LLM) token consumption across agent actions, Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) oversight effort, and infrastructure costs for agent orchestration and tooling. These costs are nondeterministic: identical tasks may consume different tokens, follow divergent reasoning paths, and require varying human correction, phenomena absent in traditional development. A new framework is needed to bridge standard sizing metrics with this cost structure.
  This paper proposes ACEM (Agentic Cost Estimation Model), which decomposes total agentic development cost into three additive dimensions: LLM, HITL, and infrastructure cost. ACEM introduces three constructs for agentic dynamics: the Revision Factor (RF), modeling token overhead from output rejection and retries; the Context Factor (CF), capturing rising token consumption as context accumulates; and the HITL Intensity Score (HIS), a four-level oversight classification scheme. It further maps Use Case Points, Story Points, and Function Points to estimated token consumption, enabling organizations to reuse existing project-scoping data for agentic cost forecasting.
  ACEM is presented as a fully specified model structure and calibration methodology, with constants left symbolic pending empirical grounding. As an early-stage proposal, it invites the research community to calibrate, test, and extend the model through real project data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [CITBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Interactive Tabular Data Processing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00018)
*Zihan Nan,Yang Gu,Wei Liu,Xi Yan,Zhou Liu,Hao Liang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了CITBench，一个用于评估LLM在交互式表格数据处理任务中能力的综合基准，涵盖4大类、18种任务类型和1,296个实例，支持离线和在线两种评估模式，揭示了当前LLM在复杂表格、规则依赖和多轮交互场景中的性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基准主要聚焦于单轮、完全明确的指令下的表格推理，未充分覆盖多轮交互、用户需求不断演变的复杂表格处理场景，因此需要一个新的基准来弥补这一空白。

Method: 构建CITBench基准：包括表格匹配、清洗、增强和转换四大类共18种任务类型和1,296个实例；同时支持离线评估和在线评估（模拟多轮交互、受限操作流程和结构化任务脚本）。

Result: 对多种开源和闭源LLM进行评估后发现：当前模型在简单表格和规则上表现良好，但随着表格复杂度增加、规则依赖更紧密以及多轮交互模拟出现噪声时，模型性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM在扩展交互式数据处理场景中仍存在理解、规划和表格结构感知方面的持续性挑战，表明这些方面是未来需要重点改进的方向。

Abstract: Tabular data processing is central to data work, and LLM-based assistants have recently shown promising capabilities in supporting such tasks. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on table reasoning under single-turn, fully specified instructions, underrepresenting complex table processing that unfolds through multi-turn interactions with evolving user requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce CITBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLMs on interactive tabular data processing. CITBench features a comprehensive taxonomy across four high-level categories--table matching, cleaning, augmentation, and transformation--spanning 18 task types and 1,296 instances curated from datasets across diverse domains. The benchmark supports both offline and online evaluation, where the online setting models multi-turn interactions under constrained operation procedures and structured task scripts, capturing key potential behavioral characteristics of user-in-the-loop tabular data processing. We evaluate a broad suite of open-source and closed-source LLMs on CITBench, revealing a consistent trend: while current models perform well on simple tables and rules, their performance degrades significantly with increasing table complexity, tighter rule dependencies, and noisy multi-turn interaction simulations. These results highlight persistent challenges in understanding, planning, and table-structure awareness for LLMs in extended interactive data processing scenarios.

</details>


### [264] [Machine-Checked Dual-Write Recovery from a Committed Log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00501)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文在Isabelle/HOL中开发了一个机器校验的理论，精确刻画了崩溃恢复场景中"恰好一次"投递保证的边界条件。核心发现是一个信息界限：崩溃侧持久化状态与sink端实际接受状态之间的信息不对称，使得仅依赖本地持久化知识无法同时避免重复投递和遗漏投递。通过读取sink端的接受记录可以突破该界限，但需要额外的防护机制（fencing）来应对过期请求和并发恢复者，且保证本身有生命周期限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事务性outbox和CDC（变更数据捕获）解决了应用层双写问题，但中继层引入了新的持久化决策点：投递和记录进度是两个独立持久化操作。从业者已用重试、检查点、幂等键和fencing等方案解决了十年，但缺乏对"何时有效"的精确描述——保证指向的确切事件、所需的证据以及证据的有效期都未有严格刻画。

Method: 作者使用Isabelle/HOL定理证明器构建机器校验的理论。首先提出一个信息界限定理：两个可达的崩溃后状态可以在崩溃侧持久化知识上完全一致，但sink端接受状态不同，因此任何基于该侧状态计算的恢复决策必然导致重复投递或遗漏投递。然后证明读取sink端接受记录可以精确突破该界限，并针对过期请求和竞争恢复者分别给出fencing证明。最后证明有界去重内存和截断的源历史会以可证明的方式使保证失效。

Result: 得到了一个简洁的检验标准来评估任何"恰好一次"恢复声明的有效性：sink端接受了什么、什么还能改变这个答案、证据能存活多久。论文证明了确定性deliver-then-checkpoint协议即使将自身持久化游标纳入恢复读取，仍会因崩溃时序而失败，从而排除了"簿记不严谨"的简单解释。

Conclusion: 本文为已实践十年的恢复边界问题提供了理论基础，将经验性的操作建议升华为经过机器证明的精确理论。"恰好一次"不是某种单一协议可以实现的无条件保证，而是有明确前提、代价和生命周期的有条件保证。每个fence的成本本身也是一个定理，强调了保证不是免费的。

Abstract: After a crash, a delivery process faces a question its own database cannot answer: did the other side already receive the effect? Transactional outboxes and change data capture remove the application's dual write, but the relay they introduce delivers and records its own progress as two separate durable acts, so the same decision reappears one stage later.
  Practitioners have handled this boundary for a decade with retries, checkpoints, idempotency keys, and fencing, and the operational advice is largely sound. What has been missing is a precise account of when it works: the exact event the guarantees refer to, the evidence they require, and how long that evidence lasts. This paper supplies the missing account as a machine-checked theory, developed in Isabelle/HOL.
  At its heart is an information bound. Two reachable post-crash states can agree on everything the crashed side durably knows and still differ in what the sink accepted, so any recovery decision computed from that side must duplicate a delivery or leave one owed. This is not a story about sloppy bookkeeping: a single deterministic deliver-then-checkpoint protocol, its own durable cursor included in what recovery reads, is defeated by crash timing alone. Reading the sink's accepted record escapes the bound exactly, under stated premises. The answer can then go stale -- an old request still in flight, a second recoverer racing the first -- and each hazard has its own proved fence at the sink's acceptance boundary, the cost of fencing itself a theorem. Finally, the guarantee has a lifetime: bounded deduplication memory and truncated source history each void it in a proved way. The resulting test for any exactly-once recovery claim is short: what did the sink accept, what can still change that answer, and how long will the evidence survive?

</details>


### [265] [TEngineDB-V: An OLAP-Native Vector Search System for Large-$k$ Workloads at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00650)
*Xufei Wu,Pengcheng Zhang,Yitong Song,Xiaobo Zhang,Anqi Liang,Kai Wang,Jijun Du,Yidi Xiong,Guangxu Cheng,Zhe Chen,Peng Chen,Guoliang Li,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TEngineDB-V，一种OLAP原生的向量搜索系统，通过将全局段解耦索引物化为关系表、将IVFPQ搜索分解为关系算子，并引入DPPQ量化技术，在大k值向量搜索工作负载上相比StarRocks等竞品实现最高145倍加速，在生产环境中实现52倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大k值分析型向量搜索（k=10^3到10^5）日益重要（如LLM数据管理、腾讯广告分析），但现有系统存在不足：专用向量数据库对k值有限制且分析支持有限，OLAP系统将向量索引作为黑盒嵌入导致读/计算放大且无法进行原生查询优化。

Method: TEngineDB-V将向量搜索作为腾讯OLAP引擎中的一等分析原语。核心方法包括：(1) 全局段解耦索引以关系表形式物化，消除分散-收集执行；(2) 将IVFPQ搜索分解为关系算子并集成OLAP优化；(3) 提出DPPQ技术，结合方向感知量化与层次残差细化提升召回率同时保持关系效率；(4) 引入索引感知的查询重写和分布式感知成本模型。

Result: 实验表明，TEngineDB-V相比StarRocks等竞争系统实现最高145倍加速，在百亿规模生产部署中实现最高52倍性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将向量搜索深度集成到OLAP引擎中而非作为黑盒嵌入，可以有效解决大k值分析型向量搜索的性能瓶颈，实现数量级加速。

Abstract: Vector search systems are essential infrastructure for modern data-driven applications. Large-$k$ analytical vector search, which retrieves $k=10^3$--$10^5$ results for analytics (e.g., aggregation, filtering, joins), is increasingly important for emerging workloads, including LLM data management and advertising analysis at Tencent. Existing systems remain inadequate: specialized vector databases often cap $k$ (e.g., $k \leq 10^4$) to satisfy tail-latency constraints and offer limited analytical support, while OLAP systems typically embed per-segment vector indexes as black boxes, causing severe read/compute amplification and preventing native query optimization.
  This paper presents TEngineDB-V, an OLAP-native vector search system for large-$k$ workloads. TEngineDB-V makes vector search a first-class analytical primitive in Tencent's OLAP engine through a global segment-decoupled index materialized as relational tables, eliminating scatter-gather execution, reducing amplification, and enabling native storage optimizations. It decomposes IVFPQ-based search into relational operators, integrates OLAP optimizations, and introduces DPPQ, which combines direction-aware quantization with hierarchical residual refinement to improve recall while preserving relational efficiency. TEngineDB-V further incorporates index-aware query rewriting and a distributed-aware cost model for efficient distributed execution. Experiments show that TEngineDB-V achieves up to a $145\times$ speedup over competitive systems such as StarRocks, and up to a $52\times$ improvement in 10-billion-scale production deployments.

</details>


### [266] [Using Lower-Bound Representations for Trajectory Similarity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01039)
*Liwei Deng,Haotian Meng,Yupu Zhang,Yan Zhao,Torben Bach Pedersen,Kai Zheng,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出LB-TrajRep，一种不依赖深度神经嵌入的统一的轨迹相似度下界表示框架，通过构建单向量表示，为DTW、Hausdorff距离和离散Fréchet距离提供可容许且可解释的下界，并在多个真实轨迹数据集上显著优于最先进的神经轨迹嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的轨迹相似度方法依赖于嵌入训练来近似距离或排名，但缺乏对原始距离的保证，在不同距离度量下性能不稳定，且训练成本高。作者希望从下界表示的角度重新审视轨迹相似度学习，以解决上述问题。

Method: 提出LB-TrajRep框架，从一组下界分量构建单向量表示，实现多种经典轨迹距离（DTW、Hausdorff距离、DFD）的可容许且可解释的下界。框架中实例化了点-枢轴（point-pivot）分量，同时支持度量和非度量距离，并兼容标准向量检索流水线。此外，设计了两种数据驱动的枢轴选择策略，分别优化下界紧致度和优先处理困难近邻轨迹对。

Result: 在真实轨迹数据集上的大量实验表明，该下界表示在多种距离度量下一致优于最先进的神经轨迹嵌入方法，在Hausdorff距离和DFD上top-k排序准确率提升达20%-60%，在DTW上提升15%-40%。

Conclusion: LB-TrajRep验证了从下界表示视角进行轨迹相似度学习的有效性，不仅避免了深度神经嵌入的高昂训练成本，还提供了可解释性和理论保证，为高效轨迹检索提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Trajectory similarity learning is fundamental to efficient trajectory retrieval under complex distance measures.
