Existing learning-based methods typically rely on embeddings trained to approximate trajectory distances or rankings, but they often lack guarantees with respect to the original distances, exhibit unstable performance across distance measures, and incur substantial training costs. We revisit trajectory similarity learning from a lower-bound representation perspective and propose LB-TrajRep, a unified lower-bound representation framework independent of deep neural embeddings. This framework constructs single-vector representations from a set of lower-bound components, enabling admissible and interpretable lower bounds for multiple classical trajectory distances, including Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), Hausdorff distance, and Discrete Fréchet Distance (DFD). Within this framework, we instantiate point-pivot components, which naturally support both metric and non-metric distances and remain compatible with standard vector-based retrieval pipelines. To improve ranking quality, we develop two data-driven pivot selection strategies that explicitly optimize lower-bound tightness and prioritize hard near-neighbor trajectory pairs, respectively. Extensive experiments on real-world trajectory datasets show that the proposed lower-bound representations are able to consistently outperform state-of-the-art neural trajectory embeddings across diverse distance measures, improving top-$k$ ranking accuracy by up to 20\%--60\% on the Hausdorff distance and DFD and by 15\%--40\% on DTW.

</details>


### [267] [Join Indices for Search Engines: a Prunable Parallel Semijoin over Lucene Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01173)
*Mikhail Khludnev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出在Lucene的LSM风格段存储中实现Valduriez的join-index技术，通过物化追加式的序数到序数join索引列，构建无需全局屏障的并行半连接算法，在三层剪枝下将查询延迟平均降低5.4倍，在高并发下优势扩大至8.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索引擎中join操作是"二等公民"：Lucene现有的查询时join实现要么性能受限（单一索引内快速join），要么能力受限（跨独立索引较慢），迫使使用者在性能和功能之间做出取舍。

Method: 将关系系统中的Valduriez join索引技术引入Lucene的基于flush的LSM-style段存储：对每对父子段物化一个只追加、序数到序数的join索引列J[c]=p，避免查询时翻译外部变长键。在此基础上构建按父段并行计算的半连接算法，无全局障碍，三级剪枝（段级、先验min/max、文档级两阶段确认配合懒累积半读并集），可与任意引擎查询组合。

Result: 原型在Apache Solr查询解析器上实现，用100万产品join 1000万SKU基准测试，平均查询延迟降低5.4倍（359.8ms vs 1934.6ms），优势随负载单调扩大，并发为8时达到8.3倍；在4 vCPU上基线在1.18 queries/s后吞吐下降，而join索引仍在增长，达到基线最优的6.8倍。

Conclusion: 通过物化join索引和并行半连接算法，显著提升了跨段join查询的性能和可扩展性，验证了将传统关系型数据库的join索引思想迁移到LSM式搜索引擎存储架构中的有效性，且高并发下性能优势更加明显。

Abstract: Joins are second-class citizens in search engines: existing query-time join implementations in Lucene are limited either in performance or in capability, forcing a choice between fast joins scoped to a single index and slower joins that span independently managed indices. We carry Valduriez's join-index technique from relational systems to Lucene's flush-based (LSM-style) segment storage: for every pair of a parent and a child segment we materialize an append-only, ordinal-to-ordinal join-index column J[c]=p, avoiding any query-time translation of external variable-length keys. On top of this structure we build a semijoin algorithm that is computed per parent segment, in parallel, without a global barrier between stages; it prunes at three levels (segment-level, the first of which comes free from per-segment execution; a-priori min/max; and document-level two-phase confirmation with a lazily accumulated half-read union) so that it composes with arbitrary engine queries instead of wasting computation on matches that a sibling filter would later discard. A prototype implemented as an Apache Solr query parser, benchmarked on 1M products joined against 10M skus, cuts average query latency 5.4 times (359.8,ms vs. 1934.6,ms) relative to Solr's built-in query-time join, and the advantage widens monotonically with load, reaching 8.3 times at a concurrency of eight: on 4 vCPUs the baseline peaks at 1.18 queries/s and then loses throughput, while the join index is still gaining, at 8.04 - 6.8times the baseline's best.

</details>


### [268] [RaG-Tree: Combining R-Tree and HNSW for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01255)
*Jiawei Liu,Xiang Zhang,Chao Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RaG-Tree，一种将R-tree与分区感知的HNSW图耦合的统一索引结构，用于解决多属性范围过滤的近似最近邻搜索（MR-ANNS）问题，在三个真实数据集上实现了优于现有方法的查询性能，同时具备轻量级索引和快速增量更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MR-ANNS索引要么利用单一属性进行范围定位，要么沿单个属性递归划分对象，难以充分利用属性间的相关性进行有效的范围剪枝，也无法利用属性-向量之间的相关性来实现高效的最近邻搜索。

Method: RaG-Tree将R-tree与分区感知的HNSW图相结合：利用R-tree的层次化分区进行有效的范围剪枝，并根据每个分区内的局部向量分布自适应调整HNSW图的稀疏度。同时提出基于代价的自适应搜索算法以最小化不必要的图探索，并设计了高效的索引维护机制来增量更新受影响的HNSW图。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，RaG-Tree在查询性能上优于最先进的基线方法，同时具备轻量级索引和快速增量更新的优势。

Conclusion: RaG-Tree通过R-tree与分区感知HNSW图的统一耦合，有效解决了MR-ANNS中范围剪枝与最近邻搜索的效率问题，在查询性能、索引开销和动态更新方面均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Multi-attribute range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (MR-ANNS), which retrieves high-dimensional vectors satisfying multiple attribute constraints, is a fundamental operation in modern AI applications. Existing MR-ANNS indexes either exploit a single attribute for range localization or recursively partition objects along individual attributes, which may limit their ability to exploit attribute correlations for effective range pruning and attribute-vector correlations for efficient nearest-neighbor search. In this paper, we propose RaG-Tree, a unified index that couples an R-tree with partition-aware HNSW graphs for MR-ANNS. RaG-Tree leverages hierarchical R-tree partitions for effective range pruning and adapts the sparsity of each HNSW graph to the local vector distributions within its partition, enabling lightweight indexing and efficient query processing. To support efficient query processing and dynamic updates, we develop a cost-based adaptive search algorithm that minimizes unnecessary graph exploration, together with an efficient index maintenance mechanism for incrementally updating affected partition-aware HNSW graphs. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets show that RaG-Tree achieves superior query performance over state-of-the-art baselines, while also providing lightweight indexing and fast incremental updates.

</details>


### [269] [Six Dimensions of Benchmarking Time-Series Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01459)
*Jalal Mostafa,Sandro Melissano,Nicholas Tan Jerome,Suren Chilingaryan,Andreas Kopmann*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciTSv2基准测试框架，从六个工作负载维度（连接并行度、批量写入、时序规律性、多元时间序列、混合负载和系统指标）系统评估了四种具有不同存储引擎的时序数据库（InfluxDB、TimescaleDB、ClickHouse、DataLayerTS），揭示了单轴基准测试难以发现的架构行为与性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序数据库采用不同的存储架构以适应特定的工作负载特征，但传统基准测试方法往往无法揭示这些架构差异带来的性能特征和瓶颈。系统架构师在构建数据后端时，需要理解哪些存储引擎对其特定管线高效，且哪些引擎面临的未来可扩展性风险最低。

Method: 设计并实现了SciTSv2基准测试框架，涵盖六个工作负载维度：连接并行度、批量写入、时序规律性、多元时间序列、混合工作负载和系统指标。利用该框架对四种代表不同存储引擎的TSDB进行系统评估：InfluxDB（Time-Structured Merge树）、TimescaleDB（基于关系数据库）、ClickHouse（列式存储）和DataLayerTS（专精于规则时间序列）。

Result: 每个评估维度都揭示了传统单轴基准测试所掩盖的架构行为，包括依赖于时间序列规律性的性能权衡、并发读写之间的竞争，以及不同的CPU、I/O和磁盘带宽瓶颈。结合细粒度的系统指标，SciTSv2能够将性能结果追溯到其底层架构原因。

Conclusion: SciTSv2作为诊断工具，帮助架构师将性能表现与底层架构原因关联起来，基于实证和工作负载特定的证据来支持存储引擎选型决策，而不仅仅依赖传统基准的片面结果。

Abstract: Time-series databases (TSDBs) employ diverse storage architectures optimized for specific workload characteristics, leading to distinct performance profiles and bottlenecks that are often not apparent under conventional benchmarking approaches. System architects designing robust data backends must understand which storage engines are efficient for their particular pipelines and which exhibit the lowest risk of encountering future scalability constraints. This paper presents SciTSv2, a benchmarking framework that evaluates time-series databases across six workload dimensions: connection parallelism, batch ingestion, time-series regularity, multi-variate series, mixed workloads, and system metrics. We exploit SciTSv2 to systematically evaluate 4 TSDBs representing distinct storage engines: InfluxDB (Time-Structured Merge tree), TimescaleDB (based on relational databases), ClickHouse (columnar), and DataLayerTS (specialized in regular time-series). We show that each dimension surfaces architectural behavior that dedicated, single-axis benchmarks obscure, including regularity-dependent trade-offs, contention between concurrent reads and writes, and distinct CPU, I/O, and disk-bandwidth-bound bottlenecks. Paired with fine-grained system metrics, SciTSv2 gives architects a diagnostic tool for tracing performance outcomes back to their underlying architectural causes, supporting storage engine selections grounded in empirical, workload-specific evidence.

</details>


### [270] [Multimodal Embeddings for 3D Similarity Search in Semantic Web-of-Things Digital-Twin Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01852)
*Oussama Zaid,Romaric Gaudel,Hassan Thomas,Maria Massri,Philippe Raipin-Parv{é}dy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将多模态嵌入层扩展到语义物联网（SWoT）平台的框架，通过将本体类型化的3D点云、时间属性和语义标签编码为潜在向量表示并存储于知识图谱旁，实现图谱过滤与相似性搜索相结合的混合本体-向量查询，解决3D数字孪生中缺乏超越严格本体等价的相似性表达问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWoT平台虽然能够通过领域本体建模物理基础设施并支持结构化逻辑查询，但缺乏表达超越严格本体等价的相似性机制。在电信基础设施和工业物联网等领域的3D数字孪生场景中，查询需要将本体约束与异构、随时间演变的场景数据的多模态相似性搜索相结合，这一能力缺口是本文的核心驱动力。

Method: 提出一个扩展SWoT平台的多模态嵌入层框架：将包含3D点云、时间属性和语义标签的本体类型化实体编码为潜在向量表示，与知识图谱并行存储，从而支持图谱过滤与向量相似性搜索相结合的混合查询。该框架在Orange Research的Thing'in平台上实现，采用Clock-G时间图数据库作为底层支撑。

Result: 在S3DIS数据集上的可行性评估表明：图过滤能够有效在时间和关系约束下缩小搜索范围；通用预训练编码器产生的向量表示足以支持相似性检索，并可作为下游预测任务的初步编码步骤。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了将多模态嵌入层集成到SWoT平台的可行性，证明混合本体-向量查询能够满足3D数字孪生场景的相似性搜索需求。通用预训练编码器的充分性也表明无需专门训练编码模型即可实现有效检索，为后续预测任务提供了基础。

Abstract: Semantic Web of Things (SWoT) platforms model physical infrastructure as knowledge graphs typed against domain ontologies, enabling expressive structural and logical queries. However, they lack native mechanisms to express similarity beyond strict ontological equivalence, which represents a critical gap for 3D digital twins in domains such as telecom infrastructure and industrial IoT, where queries must combine ontological constraints with multimodal similarity search over heterogeneous, temporally-evolving scene data. We propose a framework that extends SWoT platforms with a multimodal embedding layer: ontology-typed entities comprising 3D point clouds, temporal attributes, and semantic labels are encoded into latent vector representations stored alongside the knowledge graph, enabling hybrid ontology-vector queries that combine graph-based filtering with similarity search. Implemented on Orange Research's Thing'in platform with the Clock-G temporal graph database, a feasibility evaluation on S3DIS demonstrates that graph filtering effectively restricts the search pool under temporal and relational constraints, and that general-purpose pretrained encoders produce representations sufficient for similarity retrieval and as a preliminary encoding step for downstream predictive tasks.

</details>


### [271] [Fast Discovery of Inclusion Dependencies with Desbordante](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02213)
*Alexander Smirnov,Anton Chizhov,Ilya Shchuckin,Nikita Bobrov,George Chernishev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对包含依赖发现算法Spider和Faida提出了高效的工程实现技术，包括并行化、数据缓冲、SIMD执行和哈希表优化，并在开源数据画像工具Desbordante中实现，相比Metanome分别获得最高5倍和8倍的运行时间改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 包含依赖发现是计算代价高昂的任务，但已有研究仅关注算法层面而忽略了工程实现细节，而工程细节对性能提升同样至关重要。

Method: 对经典算法Spider提出高效的并行化技术，同时降低内存消耗；对近似算法Faida应用四种优化：数据缓冲、SIMD执行、哈希表选择和并行化。两者均在C++实现的开源数据画像工具Desbordante中实现。

Result: Spider相比Metanome最高实现5倍运行时间缩减，Faida最高实现8倍运行时间缩减。

Conclusion: 工程层面的实现优化对包含依赖发现算法的实际性能提升至关重要，合理的并行化、内存管理和底层硬件优化可以显著超越仅关注算法设计的传统实现。

Abstract: Inclusion dependency is a relation between attributes of tables that indicates possible Primary Key-Foreign Key references. Automatic discovery of inclusion dependencies is a relevant problem for both academic and industrial communities. The core concern for this problem is the efficiency of discovery process, since it is a computationally expensive task. However, existing studies only address the algorithmic side, while leaving out the implementation aspect. At the same time, engineering details are at least as important as the algorithmic ones for achieving good performance.
  In this paper, we describe techniques for efficient implementation of two algorithms for discovery of inclusion dependencies - Spider and Faida. The first one is a classic algorithm whose ideas lie in the foundation of many other inclusion dependency discovery algorithms. We propose an efficient parallelization technique, which greatly speeds up the algorithm while simultaneously reducing its memory consumption. The second one is the state-of-the-art approximate algorithm, which we approach by applying four types of optimizations: data buffering, SIMD-enabled execution, careful hash-table selection and parallelization.
  In order to experimentally evaluate our techniques, we have implemented these algorithms in Desbordante - an open-source science-intensive data profiler written in C++. For Spider, we have evaluated several different options, and in case of Faida we have demonstrated that all our optimization techniques yield results. We also compared our implementations with Metanome - a Java-based data profiler. Overall, we report up to 5x improvement in terms of run time reduction for Spider and up to 8x for Faida.

</details>


### [272] [Oasis: Hiding the Cost of Querying Parquet Files in the Datapath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02268)
*Jonas Dann,Luca Tagliavini,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oasis，一种数据处理的SmartNIC，将Parquet解码卸载到网络数据通路中作为自定义硬件加速器，并与DuckDB进行端到端集成。评估表明Oasis能将Parquet解码的开销隐藏在网络数据通路之后，最少化开销，最佳情况下几乎使DuckDB查询吞吐量翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生数据库系统通过分解计算和存储资源来提高成本效率，但数据湖和数据湖仓在对Parquet等压缩文件格式进行逐查询解码时，CPU周期消耗严重，且存储和网络带宽的增长速度超过CPU成本性能，加剧了这一瓶颈，削弱了云的性价比优势。

Method: Oasis包含三个核心组件：硬件解码器架构（将Parquet解码卸载到SmartNIC网络数据通路）、软件抽象层、以及与DuckDB的端到端集成。其核心思想是将解码过程与查询执行重叠，隐藏在网络数据通路之后。

Result: 评估显示Oasis能以最小开销将Parquet解码成本隐藏在网络数据通路之后，使扫描与查询执行的其余部分重叠。在最佳情况下，几乎使DuckDB的查询吞吐量翻倍。

Conclusion: Oasis验证了将数据解码卸载到SmartNIC等专用硬件上可以有效缓解数据湖查询中的CPU解码瓶颈，展示了跨栈硬件专业化在云数据库场景中的价值和可行性。

Abstract: Cloud-native database systems disaggregate compute and storage resources to improve cost efficiency over traditional monolithic architectures through elasticity and resource pooling. Studies of production data warehouse workloads show that scans (including round trips to storage) account for roughly half of total query runtime. Data lakes and lakehouses amplify this bottleneck through per-query decoding of storage-optimized, compressed file formats such as Parquet. As storage and network bandwidth continue to outpace CPU cost-performance, the CPU cycles spent on decoding increasingly undermine the cloud's cost-efficiency promise. This has led to a wave of specialization across the stack with custom hardware at cloud-vendor scale at the extreme end. We build on this trend and present Oasis, a data-processing SmartNIC that offloads Parquet decoding into the network datapath as a custom hardware accelerator. Oasis features a hardware decoder architecture, software abstraction layer, and end-to-end integration with DuckDB. Our evaluation shows that Oasis hides the cost of Parquet decoding behind the network datapath with minimal overhead, overlapping the scan with the remainder of the query execution. In the best case, this almost doubles DuckDB query throughput.

</details>


### [273] [FastGFDs: Efficient Validation of Graph Functional Dependencies with Desbordante](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02321)
*Anton Chernikov,Yurii Litvinov,Kirill Smirnov,George Chernishev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出FastGFDs算法，一种适用于消费级PC的图函数依赖（GFD）验证算法。该算法采用Core-First分解和紧凑路径索引（CPI）技术，与原有的并行方案相比，在真实图数据上实现了最高3倍（平均2.6倍）的性能提升，并将内存消耗降低了5倍。同时提供了开源的GFD验证实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFD验证是一个计算代价极高的问题，其中子图匹配约占99%的运行时间。原有算法仅针对高性能服务器集群设计，限制了其应用范围。本文旨在将GFD验证扩展到消费级PC环境，使其面向更广泛的用户群体。

Method: 提出FastGFDs算法，采用顺序执行方式处理整个图，创新性地结合了Core-First分解和紧凑路径索引（CPI）两种技术。与原始的并行方案不同，该算法不依赖集群环境，适用于单节点低端环境。

Result: 在真实图数据上的实验表明，FastGFDs相比并行方案实现了最高3倍（平均2.6倍）的性能提升，同时内存消耗降低了5倍。此外，提供了唯一公开可用的GFD验证算法实现，集成在Desbordante开源数据剖析工具中。

Conclusion: FastGFDs成功将GFD验证带入了消费级PC时代，是设计低端单节点环境中高效GFD验证算法的第一步。该研究为后续GFD验证算法的优化提供了公开可比较的基准实现。

Abstract: Graph functional dependencies (GFD) are a recently-developed concept aimed at capturing both topological structures in graphs and functional dependencies between attributes. The process of verifying whether a given GFD holds over a particular graph is referred to as GFD validation. In this very computationally expensive problem, locating suitable subgraphs accounts for about 99% of the total run time. The concept's authors originally proposed a parallel scheme (algorithm), targeting specifically clusters of high-performance servers.
  The goal of this study is to open GFD validation to a broader public by making it possible to run it on a consumer class PC. Our initial experiments demonstrated that the existing algorithm may not be optimal for these purposes. Therefore, we propose FastGFDs - a GFD validation algorithm that employs a recently developed graph matching technique. In contrast to the parallel scheme, it is sequential and operates on the entire graph. Its novelty lies in the use of Core-First Decomposition and the Compact Path Index (CPI). We compare it with the naive sequential algorithm and the parallel scheme, evaluating run times and memory consumption. The current study is the first step towards designing an efficient algorithm for GFD validation in low-end single-node environments.
  We also provide an open-source implementation of GFD validation over large data graphs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the only publicly available implementation of an algorithm for this problem. It is developed in Desbordante - an open-source high-performance data profiler aimed at science-intensive tasks.
  Finally, our experiments on a real-life graph demonstrated up to three times performance (2.6x on average) improvement over the parallel scheme. Employing the new subgraph matching algorithm also reduced memory consumption by five times.

</details>


### [274] [Token-Native Storage: Read and Write in your Agent's Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02376)
*Kumar Shivendu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出"token原生存储"的概念：将文本直接以模型的BPE token ID形式存储，而非传统的UTF-8格式。实验表明这在压缩率和读写速度上均优于传统方案，并呼吁AI社区标准化共享词汇表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前搜索引擎和数据库仍以UTF-8存储文本，但实际读写这些文本的AI系统（embedder、reranker、LLM agent）工作于token ID而非字符，每次访问都需在两者间转换，造成额外的计算开销。

Method: 提出直接将文本保存为模型BPE token ID的存储方案。具体包括：1) 以uint16打包r50k ID（无需压缩即可比UTF-8小2.25倍）；2) 引入熵编码器使压缩比达3.30倍；3) 根据词频重新排序BPE词汇表（而非默认的merge order），使整数编解码器streamvbyte恢复大部分压缩比且解码快约7倍；4) 提出标准化共享tokenizer词汇表。

Result: 跨6种tokenizer和3种语料（英语、代码、印地语）的实验中，压缩token ID在所有情况下匹配或优于字节级编解码器（包括语料训练的zstd字典）。token原生存储避免了重复token化，读取速度提升约10-600倍。

Conclusion: token原生存储比UTF-8更小更快，主要障碍是跨模型家族tokenizer不统一。作者呼吁AI实验室发布按频率排序的词汇表，并推动共享标准化词汇表，类似于ASCII和UTF-8对文本的标准化作用。

Abstract: Search and database engines still store text as UTF-8, a format built for humans. But the systems that increasingly read and write that text (embedders, rerankers, and language-model agents) work in token IDs, not characters, so every access pays to translate between the two. As agents become the primary readers and writers of stored text, we argue for token-native storage: keep the text as the model's own byte-pair-encoding (BPE) token IDs. This is both smaller and faster. Packing r50k IDs as uint16 already beats UTF-8 by 2.25x on English with no compression, and an entropy coder reaches 3.30x. Across six tokenizers and three corpora (English, code, Hindi), compressing token IDs matches or beats every byte codec, even a corpus-trained zstd dictionary. Two findings sharpen the case. BPE numbers tokens by merge order, not frequency, and re-ranking by frequency lets a plain integer codec (streamvbyte) recover most of the entropy coder's ratio while decoding ~7x faster, a one-line change we ask AI labs to make when they publish vocabularies. And because a model reads token IDs, not text, a token-native store hands them over directly instead of re-tokenizing on every read, ~10-600x faster. The only barrier is that sharing token IDs requires a common tokenizer, which is not always true across model families yet, so we argue for standardization: a published, shared vocabulary, the way ASCII and UTF-8 standardized text.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [EmergencyBias: Bias in Text-to-Image Models under Emergency Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00598)
*Haibo Tang,Linqi Zhang,Hongxin Huan,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文定义了EmergencyBias——文本到图像（T2I）生成模型在紧急场景下的一种偏见形式，涵盖人口统计偏见和行为偏见。研究评估了七个主流T2I模型、六种紧急场景和三个维度，发现模型在无提示时存在人口统计偏见，在受控提示下行为偏见仍与人口统计特征系统性关联，并提出ActionAlign轻量校准方法减少行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I偏见研究多聚焦静态且显式的偏见（如性别、种族、地理文化表征差异），忽视了不同群体在行为表现、社会角色分配等方面的行为偏见。紧急场景因其需要刻画"谁处于风险中、谁干预、责任如何分配"，为研究此类偏见提供了有价值的视角。

Method: 构建系统化评估框架，跨七个主流T2I模型、六种代表性紧急场景和三个维度（性别、年龄、肤色）研究EmergencyBias。通过无人口统计规定的空白提示评估人口统计偏见，通过受控提示评估行为偏见，并提出ActionAlign——一种轻量级提示嵌入校准方法以减少行为差异。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）无人口统计规定的空白提示下，T2I模型在紧急场景中表现出明显的人口统计偏见；（2）受控提示下，紧急应对中的行为偏见仍与人口统计变化系统性关联，性别差异尤为突出且模型间差异显著；（3）ActionAlign优于代表性基于提示的基线方法，在降低行为差异的同时基本保持了图像质量。

Conclusion: 紧急场景是T2I模型偏见评估的重要场景，本研究为具社会后果的视觉生成提供了公平性改进的实践方向。

Abstract: Bias in Text-to-Image (T2I) generation has become an important problem in multimedia content creation and communication. However, existing studies have primarily focused on relatively static and explicit forms of bias, such as disparities in the representation of gender, race, and geo-cultural attributes. Less attention has been paid to behavioral bias in how different groups are portrayed acting, reacting, and occupying social roles. Emergency scenarios provide a revealing setting for studying such bias because they require models to depict not only who is present, but also who is at risk, who intervenes, and how responsibility is allocated. In this paper, we define EmergencyBias, a form of bias in T2I generation under emergency scenarios that includes both demographic bias and behavioral bias. We construct an evaluation framework to systematically study EmergencyBias across seven leading T2I models, six representative emergency scenarios, and three demographic dimensions. Our experimental results reveal three main findings. First, under blank prompts without demographic specification, T2I models exhibit clear demographic bias in emergency scenarios, reflected in the distributions of portrayed individuals across gender, age, and skin tone. Second, under controlled prompts, behavioral bias in emergency responses remains systematically associated with demographic variation, with particularly pronounced disparities along gender and substantial differences across models. Third, we introduce ActionAlign, a lightweight prompt-embedding calibration method that outperforms a representative prompt-based baseline in reducing behavioral disparities while largely preserving image quality. Overall, our work identifies emergency scenarios as an important setting for bias evaluation in T2I models and offers a practical direction toward fairer visual generation in socially consequential contexts.

</details>


### [276] [FATE: Frame-Level Audio-Visual Temporal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01310)
*Kaisi Guan,Bingzi Zhang,Xihua Wang,Ying Ba,Xin Cheng,Yijing Chen,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FATE（Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding），一种帧级音视频时序嵌入方法，同时捕获语义和时序对齐信息，在语义/时序检索、零样本事件定位和生成评估等任务上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频模型要么像嵌入模型那样通过池化丢失时间信息，要么像同步模型那样只预测时间偏移而缺乏语义理解，无法同时捕捉"声音是什么"和"何时发生"。需要一种能兼顾语义与时间对齐的表征方法。

Method: FATE保留帧级序列而非池化为单一嵌入，在物理时间线上对齐音频与视频帧，并仅对严格对齐的帧对计算相似度。训练采用联合目标函数，结合跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时序对比学习，将同步编码进可复用的嵌入空间。

Result: 在三个任务上，FATE在时序和语义检索上大幅超越最强基线；在零样本事件定位上匹配全监督方法；作为生成评估指标时与人类判断相关性最佳。

Conclusion: FATE成功弥合了嵌入模型与同步模型之间的鸿沟，证明了帧级对齐嵌入能够同时捕捉音视频的语义信息和时序信息，为音视频理解提供了一种统一的表征框架。

Abstract: When a dog opens its mouth and barks, humans naturally recognize what the sound is and when it occurs. Building audio-visual models with this same ability requires representations that capture both semantic and temporal alignment. Current approaches fall short on one side or the other: embedding models match semantic but lose temporal information; synchronization models capture temporal offsets but lack semantic understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose FATE, Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding. Unlike prior embedding models that pool each modality into a single embedding and discard temporal information, FATE retains frame-level sequences, aligns them on the physical timeline, and computes similarity over strictly aligned frame pairs. Unlike synchronization models that output only an offset prediction, FATE encodes synchronization in a reusable embedding space, trained with a joint objective combining cross-video semantic and within-video temporal contrastive learning to capture both what sounds and when it occurs. Across three tasks, FATE surpasses the strongest baseline on temporal and semantic retrieval by a large margin, matches fully supervised methods on event localization in a zero-shot setting, and achieves the best correlation with human judgments as a generation evaluation metric. The source code can be found at \texttt{https://github.com/guankaisi/FATE}.

</details>


### [277] [Hear, Invoke, and Understand: A Skill-Calling Multimodal Agent for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01881)
*Yuwen Wang,Tian-Hao Zhang,Minghao Cai,Yilin Ren,Ziyang Jiang,Xin Wang,Zhichao Wang,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan,Xinyuan Qian*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechAgent-R，一个工具交互式音频推理智能体，通过构建包含65,492条交互轨迹和507.6小时音频的HIU-Corpus数据集，结合轨迹监督微调和多轮强化学习，在HIU-Bench基准上实现了ID任务84.17分和OOD任务70.94分的表现，分别比基础模型提升15.40和14.23分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂声学问题需要模型执行声学操作、与外部工具交互，并基于文本或音频处理结果进行推理，而非直接从固定音频输入作答。现有工作缺乏对这类工具交互式音频推理的系统研究。

Method: 构建HIU-Corpus数据集（24个任务、8种技能、9种工具，含65,492条交互轨迹和507.6小时音频），SpeechAgent-R先通过轨迹监督微调学习结构化交互行为，再通过多轮强化学习优化决策能力。同时构建HIU-Bench基准评估任务性能、交互质量和泛化能力。

Result: SpeechAgent-R在ID任务上达到84.17分，在OOD任务上达到70.94分，相比相同智能体框架下的基础模型分别提升15.40和14.23分。

Conclusion: 学习技能和工具的协调能力能够显著提升音频智能体处理多样化任务场景和自适应工具交互的能力，验证了工具交互式音频推理范式的有效性。

Abstract: Complex acoustic problems may require models to perform acoustic operations, interact with external tools and reason over the resulting textual or processed-audio observations rather than answer directly from a fixed audio input. We study such problems as tool-interactive audio reasoning and develop SpeechAgent-R, an audio agent that coordinates its intrinsic multimodal understanding with external skills and tools. To support this capability, we construct HIU-Corpus, comprising 65,492 interaction trajectories and 507.6 hours of audio across 24 tasks, 8 skills and 9 tools. SpeechAgent-R first learns structured interaction behaviors through trajectory-based supervised fine-tuning and then improves its decisions through multi-turn reinforcement learning. We further introduce HIU-Bench to jointly evaluate task performance, interaction quality and generalization to diverse task settings. It contains 1,395 samples across 56 tasks, including in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) splits with substantial shifts in tool usage and workflow composition. SpeechAgent-R achieves 84.17 on ID tasks and 70.94 on OOD tasks, improving over the base model under the same agent harness by 15.40 and 14.23 points. These results demonstrate that learning skill and tool coordination improves audio agents' ability to handle diverse task settings and adaptive tool interactions.

</details>


### [278] [AcoustiTrace: When Plausible Sound Violates Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02035)
*Shiyang Li,Yuewen Cao,Yihao Liu,Yuandong Pu,Baochang Zhang,Xiaofei Li,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: AcoustiTrace是一个诊断基准，用于评估音视频生成模型中的声学物理真实性，通过八个测量维度覆盖声音产生、传播环境和声学接收，并基于该基准构建大规模数据集和提示套件。实验表明现有领先生成器在基础声学过程上仍存在不足，而AcoustiTrace提供的诊断可用于指导模型改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成器虽然能生成语义合理且表面同步的声音，但仍可能违反可见事件和环境所隐含的声学物理过程。已有基准缺乏对特定声学过程违规的归因能力和严重程度的量化能力，亟需一个系统化的诊断基准。

Method: 将T2AV和I2AV评估围绕声学过程组织，通过八个可测量的声学量维度覆盖声音生成、传播环境和声学接收；构建包含真实音视频记录和声学标注RGB-D观测的大规模数据集，开发针对性提示套件和验证过的评估器。

Result: 实验发现即使领先的生成器在生成看似合理的声音事件时，仍难以处理基本的声学物理过程。AcoustiTrace提供的特定声学关系诊断可用于指导模型精细化改进，使其声音生成更符合物理规律。

Conclusion: AcoustiTrace为音视频生成中的声学物理真实性提供了系统化的诊断框架，其诊断结果能够驱动模型在训练目标、奖励建模和候选选择等方面融入声学原理，为更物理忠实的声音生成开辟新方向。

Abstract: Recent audio-video generators can produce semantically plausible and apparently synchronized sound, yet may still violate the acoustic processes implied by visible events and environments. Existing benchmarks provide limited support for attributing such violations to particular acoustic processes and quantifying their severity. We introduce AcoustiTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that formalizes acoustic physical realism in audio-video generation. AcoustiTrace organizes text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) evaluation around the acoustic process, covering sound generation, propagation environment, and acoustic reception through eight dimensions grounded in measurable acoustic quantities. Based on these evaluation dimensions, we construct a large-scale dataset organized around acoustic mechanisms, comprising real-world audio-video recordings and acoustically annotated RGB-D observations, and use it to develop targeted prompt suites and validated evaluators. Experiments reveal that even leading generators still struggle with fundamental acoustic processes despite producing plausible sound events. Finally, we show that the diagnostics AcoustiTrace provides for specific acoustic relations can guide model refinement toward more physically faithful audio and open new directions for incorporating acoustic principles into training objectives, reward modeling, and candidate selection.

</details>


### [279] [Estimating SSIM from MSE for DCT-Based Compressed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02549)
*Luc Trudeau,Maria G. Martini*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出在DCT压缩图像背景下，利用仅从参考图像获取的局部统计量（方差或标准差加权），将全局PSNR/MSE近似为SSIM的两种方法，在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上验证了其准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知质量评估对于图像视频处理、压缩和流媒体系统至关重要，但传统的SSIM计算需要局部MSE信息，而实际的压缩系统中往往只能获得全局PSNR或MSE，因此需要探索如何从全局指标近似得到SSIM。

Method: 提出两种方法：通过方差加权或标准差加权的方式，将全局MSE重新分配为局部MSE，再结合参考图像中仅需的局部统计量来近似SSIM。该框架设计为可自然扩展到视频领域，参考图像推导的统计量可在同一内容的多次编码中摊销复用。

Result: 在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上、覆盖多种JPEG质量级别的实验中，两种方法均提供了准确且鲁棒的SSIM近似结果，显著优于全局MSE基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在DCT压缩图像中，无需局部MSE即可通过参考图像局部统计量近似SSIM，为高效感知质量评估提供了新路径，并具备向视频场景扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Efficient and perceptually meaningful quality assessment is a fundamental requirement for image and video processing, compression, and streaming systems. This article shows that, in the context of Discrete Cosine Transform ( DCT)-based compressed images, Structural Similarity Index ( SSIM ) can be approximated from global Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) or Mean Square Error ( MSE) using local statistics derived only from the reference image. While prior work assumes access to local MSE, we propose two approaches to approximate local MSE by redistributing the global MSE using variance or standard-deviation-based weighting. Experiments on the Kodak and Xiph Subset1 datasets across a range of JPEG quality levels demonstrate that both approaches provide accurate and robust SSIM approximations, substantially outperforming the global MSE baseline. The proposed framework is designed to extend naturally to video, where reference-derived statistics can be amortized across multiple encodes of the same content.

</details>


### [280] [A Brief Overview about D-Profile of Ginga DTV Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02565)
*Marcelo F. Moreno,Debora C. Muchaluat-Saade,Guido Lemos de Souza Filho,Raoni Kulesza,Alan L. V. Guedes*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西DTV系统标准为应对广播与宽带深度融合的新用例而进行的修订，重点阐述了Ginga中间件D-profile接收器规范的进展和新用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西DTV系统需适应广播与宽带服务深度集成的新用例，同时不能中断当前仍在从模拟向数字切换的DTV服务，因此需要一种增量式但强大的演进方案。

Method: 选择中间件层作为演进的切入点，基于开放平台和接收器配置文件的方式，在巴西DTV论坛主导的协作框架下（由学术界牵头）制定Ginga D-profile DTV接收器规范。

Result: Ginga作为巴西DTV中间件，基于接收器配置文件的开放平台方案取得了显著成果，D-profile规范成功覆盖了新的融合用例。

Conclusion: 通过D-profile Ginga接收器规范的制定，巴西DTV系统以不中断现有服务的方式实现了面向广播与宽带融合的增量式进化，该工作由学术界主导的巴西DTV论坛协作完成。

Abstract: The Brazilian DTV system standards have been recently revised in order to address new use cases related to deeper integration be- tween broadcast services and broadband services. Such an evolution could not disrupt the current DTV services since Brazil and many ISDB-T countries are still under the switch-off process from ana- log to digital. The middleware layer is the best candidate for such an incremental (yet powerful) evolution. In the case of Ginga, the Brazilian DTV middleware, as an open platform based on receiver profiles, the results were impressive. In this paper, we focus on the advances and new use cases addressed by the D-profile Ginga DTV receiver specification carried out in the Brazilian DTV Forum under a collaborative effort led by the Academia

</details>
